# Vietnamese Exchange Rulebook, 2020-01-01 → 2026-08-25

**Purpose.** This is the dated rule reference the `plutus.market` exchange simulator is
implemented from, and the document a reviewer checks the simulator against. Every row is
a *dated interval*: a rule value, the venue it binds, the window it was in force, the
instrument that established it, and how much we trust it.

**Coverage window.** 2020-01-01 → 2026-08-25, covering HSX/HOSE, HNX, UPCoM and the
HNX derivatives market (HNXDS). The window straddles the KRX trading-system cutover on
2025-05-05, which changed order admission, matching priority, amendment, foreign-room
accounting and derivatives margin. It also straddles four rulebook re-issues and a
statutory reorganisation of which exchange lists what.

**Evidence standard, stated plainly.**

- `high` — the rule text was read in the governing instrument (or in an exchange-published
  reproduction of it), for the interval claimed.
- `medium` — corroborated across independent secondary sources (broker rule sheets,
  exchange press releases, legal-database summaries), or read in a primary text but for a
  neighbouring interval and carried by continuity.
- `low` — single source, or an inference, or a number whose instrument nobody has opened.
- `UNVERIFIED` — we could not establish it at all. It appears as `UNVERIFIED` in the
  value column, never as a plausible guess.

Three things this document deliberately does **not** do. It does not present a
low-confidence value as settled. It does not silently pick a side when sources conflict —
both readings appear, with the one we adopt and why. And it does not claim a gazetted
citation for a number that in fact comes from a broker web page; where the governing
article merely *delegates* to an appendix nobody has obtained, the row says so.

**The single largest structural weakness.** Under the VNX regime (2022-03-31 onward) the
rulebook body delegates trading hours, trading units, maximum order size, the tick grid
and the price bands to **Phụ lục III** of the Quy chế. Nobody has obtained Phụ lục III for
any version (2022, 2025 or 2026). Every tick, lot and band number for 2022-03-31 onward is
therefore sourced to broker rule sheets plus HOSE's own web publications, not to the
gazetted appendix. Obtaining Phụ lục III is the highest-value single retrieval task
outstanding and would move roughly a dozen rows from `medium` to `high`.

---

## 1. Confidence summary

Read this before using any number below.

Counted over the dated-interval tables in §2–§8. **Primary rows** carry a rule and a
value. **Annotation rows** (those beginning "—") carry a caveat, a corpus measurement, a
rejected reading or a modelling consequence attached to the row above; they are counted
separately because a high-confidence *caveat* is not a high-confidence *rule*.

| Confidence | Primary rule rows | Share | Annotation rows |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| high | 280 | 64.5% | 116 |
| medium | 99 | 22.8% | 28 |
| low | 37 | 8.5% | 12 |
| UNVERIFIED (value not established) | 15 | 3.5% | 19 |
| prose in the confidence column (3 rows, not bucketable) | 3 | 0.7% | 0 |
| **Total** | **434** | | **175** |

*Counted 2026-08-26 by script, not by hand: every pipe-table row between the `## 2.` and
`## 9.` headings whose last cell is non-empty, bucketed on the first of `high` / `medium` /
`low` / `unverified` to appear in that cell, and classed as an annotation when its first
cell begins with `—`. **This replaces an earlier hand count** (400 primary / 133
annotation) which the same script reproduces only to within ~4 rows; the rule above is
stated so the next person gets the same answer rather than a differently-wrong one. Split
confidences such as `high (rule) / low (article number)` bucket on the first term, which
flatters the total slightly — read the domain table below, not the headline.*

§9 (code corrections), §10 (the KRX delta) and §12 (the fee/tax model) are *derived views*
of the same rules and are not counted again here.

**The 2026-08-26 jump is real but narrow.** High-confidence rows rose by 32 primary and 31
annotation in a single day because **QĐ 26/QĐ-HĐTV and its Phụ lục 2 were obtained and read
in full** (§9.6). Essentially all of that gain lands in **one domain and one side of one
date boundary**: post-KRX derivatives margin. It bought **nothing** for 2017-05-01 →
2025-05-04, and it *cost* confidence there — the 80/90/100 margin ladder fell from `high`
to `low` when its citation chain broke. **A rising headline percentage concealing a
downgrade on the majority of the window is exactly the failure mode this table exists to
prevent.**

**Read the 64.5% with the caveats below, not on its own.** A large share of the high-confidence
rows concentrate in three places — the band arithmetic (§3.4, primary-sourced *and*
corpus-reproduced), the HOSE 2021-07-05→2025-05-04 rulebook (QĐ 352 was read in full), and
now the post-KRX margin stack (QĐ 26 + Phụ lục 2).
The parameter *tables* that a simulator actually reads from — ticks, lots, bands after
2022-03-31 — are corroborated rather than gazetted-verified, because Phụ lục III was never
obtained. And 22.5% of the coverage window (2020-01-01 → 2021-07-04) has no primary text at
any venue.

### Domain strength, best to worst

| Domain | Verdict |
|---|---|
| **Bands & reference price** | **Strongest.** Band arithmetic, rounding direction, both degenerate branches, the warrant derived-limit formula and the UPCoM illiquidity band are all primary-sourced and, for 2021-02→2022-12, empirically reproduced on 816,820 corpus name-days. |
| **Sessions & order types (HOSE, 2021-07-05 →)** | Strong. QĐ 352/QĐ-SGDHCM was read in full; HOSE's own post-KRX regulation was read in Vietnamese and English. |
| **Foreign ownership (room mechanics)** | Strong for both sides of the KRX boundary — the pre- and post-KRX decrement rules were read verbatim in successive primary rulebooks. Weak for sector caps. |
| **Settlement (2022-08-29 → 2024-11-01)** | Strong. VSD Decision 109/QĐ-VSD's intraday timetable was read verbatim. |
| **Tick & lot (2022-03-31 →)** | Mixed. Values corroborated and corpus-verified; the gazetted appendix carrying them is unobtained. |
| **Derivatives margin, 2025-05-05 →** | **Now the best-sourced part of the derivatives domain.** QĐ 26/QĐ-HĐTV and **Phụ lục 2** were read in full on 2026-08-26: the whole `MR = Max(ΣPgm, 0)` stack, all four components, the 21-scenario grid, the Kendall-tau grouping, the haircuts, Điều 13's monitoring and Điều 29's ladder are quoted verbatim. Three residual holes, all inside a document we now hold: the equation converting VaR into the initial-margin ratio, the `k` term in the scenario formula, and Phụ lục 3/6/7/8. |
| **Derivatives margin, 2017-05-01 → 2025-05-04** | **Weak on values, and weaker after 2026-08-26 than before it.** The central finding — no published maintenance margin ratio — still holds. But the **80/90/100 margin-utilisation ladder is now `low`**: it was sourced to "Article 13" of a chain ending in QĐ 26, QĐ 26's Điều 13 has now been read and does not contain it, and **QĐ 61 and QĐ 12 have never been read**. Not disproven for this interval — *unsupported*. The initial-margin series (10/13/17%) is press-sourced; no `quyết định` number exists for it, and 17.5% (our current constant) matches nothing at any date. |
| **Fees & taxes** | **Weakest of the fee/tax split: the tax half.** Not one tax row is sourced to a primary text that anyone opened. Two tax rows carry a URL that does not mention the instrument cited. The fee half is materially better but was mis-dated by 3 years on the single most consequential derivatives charge. |
| **2020-01-01 → 2021-07-04, all domains** | **Weakest interval.** 22.5% of the window. The governing HOSE instruments are now *identified* (QĐ 66 and QĐ 67/QĐ-SGDHCM of 2018-03-02, amended by QĐ 462 of 2020-08-14 and QĐ 894 of 2020-12-30) but **nobody has read their articles**. HNX QĐ 653/QĐ-SGDHN and the UPCoM QĐ 455/QĐ-SGDHN texts were likewise never retrieved. Everything here is continuity inference plus corpus measurement. |
| **UPCoM before 2022-11-16** | **Effectively uncovered.** The governing HNX decision was never read; every UPCoM value is verified only from VNX QĐ 34/QĐ-HĐTV (2022-11-16) onward. |
| **2026-03-16 → 2026-08-25** | 163 days (6.7% of the window) governed by VNX QĐ 22/QĐ-HĐTV of 2026-03-16, whose trading articles were compared and found materially unchanged — but whose Phụ lục III was not obtained and whose effective date is confirmed only as the signing date. |

### Things the paper must not claim

1. That every rule in the window traces to a gazetted document with a number and date.
   It does not, for 2020-01-01 → 2021-07-04 at any venue, for UPCoM before 2022-11-16,
   for any tick/lot/band value after 2022-03-31 (appendix unobtained), for the derivatives
   contract template as an issued instrument, or for the derivatives PIT base before
   2026-07-01 (a ministry letter, not a gazetted normative document).
2. That the KRX cutover left settlement, bands or ticks untouched. It changed the
   **definition of the closing price**, and therefore the reference price on any no-trade
   day whose reference was not the prior close.
3. That "T+1.5" is a regime. It is retail shorthand; no gazetted document uses it.
4. That the **80 / 90 / 100 ladder is a margin rule**. In the post-KRX regime it is a
   **position-limit** rule (QĐ 26 **Điều 29**); **Điều 13**, which governs margin
   monitoring, contains no percentage at all. For the pre-KRX regime the margin reading is
   **unverified, not established** — say "unsourced", never "the rule is". And do not cite
   "Article 13" for it in any regime. See §9.6.
5. That the post-KRX margin requirement is computed intraday, or by something called
   **COMS**. It is struck **once, after the close** (QĐ 26 Điều 5.5), and the string `COMS`
   appears nowhere in the instrument.
6. That reading QĐ 26 settled the derivatives margin domain. It settled **2025-05-05
   onward only** — roughly the last 20% of the coverage window.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LO time in force | ALL | Day order. May be entered in continuous *and* auction phases. An unfilled continuous-session LO is carried into the following auction and participates in the cross. | ATO/ATC time in force | HSX, HNX (ATC), HNXDS | Enterable only inside their own auction window; unfilled remainder auto-cancelled at the cross; never rest, never carry | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 14.3(b), 14.4(b); MBS VN30F §3.3–3.4 | high |
| MP semantics | HSX | Walk the book from the best opposite price; residue converts to an LO **one tick beyond** the last matched price (buy: +1 tick, sell: −1 tick), capped at ceiling/floor if the last match was already there. **Cancelled at entry if no opposite limit order exists** | 2020-01-01 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 14.2(a)–(đ) | high |

Operationally the ATO/ATC queues as a ceiling-buy / floor-sell by arrival time; it still ranks ahead of limit orders priced inside the band | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | VNX QĐ 22/2026 Điều 22 states it affirmatively; HOSE regulation 2025-04-26 §5.3–5.4; ASEANSC HNX §2.3 | high |
| — over-broad secondary reading | | Two outlets gloss this as "any earlier LO now outranks an ATO/ATC". **We reject that**: the rulebook carve-out is specific to ceiling-buy / floor-sell | | | | DNSE, Thị trường Tài chính Tiền tệ | high (that the narrow reading is right) |
| ATO/ATC volume in the auction calculation | HNXDS (explicit), HSX (implied) | The unpriced ATO/ATC quantity on a side is **added to that side's quantity at every price level** when computing executable volume | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | MBS VN30F §3.3–3.4 (both editions); QĐ 352 Điều 14.3(a)/14.4(a) express the same thing as an imputed order price | high |
| Auction-order-only book | HSX, HNXDS | If both sides hold only ATO/ATC: cross at the reference price (ATO) or last traded price (ATC) when the sides are equal; +1 tick if buy volume is larger; −1 tick if sell volume is larger; clamped | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | MBS VN30F §3.3–3.4; QĐ 352 Điều 14.3(a)/14.4(a) | high |
| Auction-order-only book | HNX | **Opposite outcome.** "Đợt khớp lệnh định kỳ xác định giá đóng cửa sẽ không xác định được giá khớp lệnh nếu chỉ có lệnh ATC trên sổ lệnh" — the HNX closing auction produces **no price at all**. A shared auction routine across venues will manufacture phantom HNX closing prints | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | ASEANSC HNX sheet | medium |
| Imputed price recorded for an ATO/ATC order | HSX | ATO-only book: reference price if one side only or sides equal; reference +1 tick if buy > sell; −1 tick if sell > buy. ATC-only book: same, anchored on the most recent matched price (else reference). With limit orders present: an ATO/ATC **buy** takes the highest of {best bid + 1 tick (capped at ceiling), the highest opposite ask, the anchor}; an ATO/ATC **sell** the lowest of {best ask − 1 tick (floored at floor), the lowest opposite bid, the anchor} | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 14.3(a), 14.4(a) verbatim; reproduced unchanged in HOSE's 2025 regulation | high |
| — clamp asymmetry | HSX | ATO clamps the **floor only** ("nếu thấp hơn giá sàn thì ghi nhận là giá sàn"); ATC clamps **both** directions. Immaterial except in the degenerate one-tick-band case, but the two texts genuinely differ | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 14.3(a) vs 14.4(a) | medium |
| — counter-intuitive clause | HSX | For an ATO **buy** the text really does say "giá bán cao nhất của bên đối ứng" (the *highest* opposite ask). This is what makes an ATO effectively price-insensitive inside the band. Not a transcription error | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 14.3(a), confirmed in the 2025 text | high |
| ATO/ATC display during the auction | HSX, HNX | No longer shown as a bare tag. Displayed at a price: the indicative cross price, else the last matched price, else the reference; or, where limit residual remains, best bid +1 tick (buy) / best ask −1 tick (sell). The board also switches from the 3 best resting levels to the 3 best levels **expected to remain after** the cross | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | HOSE regulation §5.4; SHS 2025 §8 | medium |
| — direction of the disclosure change | | Sources frame it two ways ("3 best levels after matching instead of the whole book" vs "expected residual after the projected match"). We adopt only what both agree on: **the auction feed became an indicative-match feed, not a raw depth feed** | 2025-05-05 | current | market practice | HOSE briefing; TTTCTT | medium |
| Simultaneous own buy and sell in one auction | HSX, HNX | Barred: an investor may not have both a buy and a sell for the same security in the same call auction, except orders already resting from the preceding continuous session | 2021-02-15 | current | exchange | Broker sheets attribute it to Thông tư 120/2020/TT-BTC, **never read**. Kind corrected from `statutory` to `exchange` for that reason. `effective_from` cannot precede TT 120's own commencement | medium |
| Self-crossing in continuous trading | HNX | While your buy/sell rests or is partly filled you may not enter a sell priced at or below your resting buy, or a buy at or above your resting sell | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | ASEANSC HNX sheet | medium |
| Definition of a fill | ALL | A trade is established when the system matches or records it; both sides are then obliged to settle | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | VNX QĐ 22/2025 Điều 16 (2026: Điều 18) | high |
| Trade elimination (loại bỏ giao dịch) | ALL | The only route to undoing a trade. Three grounds: system failure, force majeure, or a rule-breaching trade that seriously harms investors. Requires exchange report to VNX **and SSC approval** | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | VNX QĐ 22/2025 Điều 16 | medium |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Auctions are locked | ALL | No amendment and no cancellation of LO, ATO, ATC while an opening or closing auction is running. **Unchanged across the whole window** — several write-ups present this as a KRX novelty; it is not | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 17.1; VNX QĐ 17 Điều 22; QĐ 22/2025 Điều 21 | high |
| Resting LOs freeze at the auction boundary | HSX, HNX | The auction lock covers LOs **carried in from the continuous session** ("bao gồm cả các lệnh LO được chuyển từ phiên khớp lệnh liên tục sang"). Whether the parenthetical is present in QĐ 17 Điều 22.4 is **UNVERIFIED** — if new, this is an unreported KRX delta affecting cancellation behaviour | medium |
| Post-close session is locked | HNX | No amend, no cancel in the after-hours session | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | VNX QĐ 22/2025 Điều 21.5; ASEANSC HNX §3 | high |
| Derivatives auction lock | HNXDS | The **whole** periodic session is locked — both 08:45–09:00 and 14:30–14:45, for their entire duration, not merely at the cross. | Amendment in continuous session — HOSE | HSX | Amendment **is** cancel-the-order-and-enter-a-new-one; time priority **always** restarts. There is no priority-preserving amendment on HOSE in this interval | 2020-01-01 | 2022-03-30 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 17.1–17.3, read verbatim | high |
| Amendment in continuous session — unified VNX rule | HSX, HNX | An LO may have price and/or quantity amended, or be cancelled. Priority **preserved only if quantity is reduced**; restarts on a quantity increase and/or a price change. | — CONFLICT: did HOSE actually implement it? | HSX | Vietcap's KRX handbook §2.1 records pre-KRX HOSE as "does not allow direct order amendments", with brokers offering cancel-and-replace, while HNX "allows modification of both price and volume simultaneously". **We adopt: the rulebook permitted priority-preserving amendment from 2022-03-31, but the legacy HOSE engine did not implement it.** Both readings are recorded because this changes queue-position modelling for three years of the sample | 2022-03-31 | 2025-05-04 | — | QĐ 17 Điều 22.3 vs Vietcap handbook §2.1 | medium |
| Amendment — price XOR quantity | HSX, HNX | One amendment may change price **or** quantity, never both: "không được sửa đồng thời thông tin khối lượng và giá trên cùng một lệnh đặt". | — CONFLICT | HSX | HOSE's own web regulation and investor guide say only "Lệnh giới hạn được phép sửa giá, khối lượng và hủy lệnh trong thời gian giao dịch", with no simultaneity ban. **We adopt the VNX rulebook** (higher rank, verbatim, and Vietcap labels it a "Unified Rule – HNX & HOSE"), and record that HOSE's own publications omit it | 2025-05-05 | current | — | HOSE regulation vs VNX QĐ 22/2025 Điều 21.3 | medium |

| Amendment — derivatives | HNXDS | Price/quantity amendable in continuous matching; priority preserved only on a quantity decrease. **"Không được phép đồng thời sửa giá và khối lượng"** from KRX | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | VNX QĐ 21 Điều 24.3 (new; absent from QĐ 20); MBS VN30F 05/2025 §7.1 | high |

---

## 3. Price bands and reference prices

This is the best-evidenced domain: the arithmetic, the rounding direction and both
degenerate branches are primary-sourced, and for 2021-02-05 → 2022-12-30 they were
reproduced against 816,820 corpus name-days. Two caveats govern the whole section.
First, **nothing in the corpus tests 2025-05-05 onward or calendar 2020** — the band
tables span 2021-02-05 → 2022-12-30 only. Second, extrapolating the empirical findings
forward is defensible for the *arithmetic, rounding and degenerate branches* (whose text
really is unchanged from QĐ 352 through QĐ 22/2026) but **not** for the reference-price
and widened-band rows, which changed at KRX.

### 3.1 Statutory frame

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Band and reference price defined; numbers delegated | ALL | The MoF circular defines "biên độ dao động giá" and "giá tham chiếu" and delegates the values to VNX's rulebook, subject to SSC approval. The SSC may order a band change to stabilise the market | 2021-02-15 | current | statutory | Thông tư 120/2020/TT-BTC (2020-12-31) Điều 2–4; cross-referenced by VNX QĐ 23 Điều 19.3. **TT 120's own commencement article was not read**; 2021-02-15 is the conventionally cited date. The predecessor for 2020-01-01→2021-02-14 (believed TT 203/2015/TT-BTC) is unverified | medium |
| Circuit breaker (ngắt mạch thị trường) | ALL | Provisioned in the rulebooks and in the trading system; **no thresholds ever published; no activation observed in the window.** Not "bands only" — the UPCoM rulebook carves out "trường hợp tạm ngừng/đình chỉ theo cơ chế ngắt mạch được cài đặt tự động", so the mechanism is a provisioned system function | 2022-01-01 | current | statutory | "Cơ chế ngắt mạch thị trường thực hiện theo Điều 5 Thông tư 120/2020/TT-BTC" — VNX QĐ 17 Điều 15, QĐ 22/2025 Điều 15, QĐ 22/2026 Điều 17, QĐ 34 Điều 13, QĐ 23/2026 Điều 14 | medium |
| SSC / exchange power to change a band | ALL | SSC may change bands to stabilise the market (khoản 2 Điều 4 TT 120/2020). Separately QĐ 352 Điều 10.6 lets SGDCK apply a different reference-price method with SSC approval, and Điều 12.3 / 13.1(c) let it adjust the band with SSC approval after three sessions with no close | 2021-02-15 | current | statutory | TT 120/2020 as cross-referenced by QĐ 34 Điều 18.3 and QĐ 23/2026 Điều 19.3 | medium |
| Any temporary band override 2020–2026? | ALL | **UNVERIFIED — and this is the largest unclosed risk to the "no band change in the window" claim.** The corpus shows none between 2021-02 and 2022-12: monthly median implied bands flat at 0.0690–0.0692 (HOSE), 0.0974–0.0984 (HNX), 0.1475–0.1482 (UPCoM) across all 23 months. March-2020 COVID and any 2026 widening proposal were never checked | — | — | — | Absence of evidence + corpus | UNVERIFIED |

### 3.3 Widened bands

The widened band and the no-reference-adjustment list are **deliberately complementary**
and must be implemented as one coupled rule: wherever the reference is *not* adjusted for
an event that mechanically moves the price, the band is widened instead.

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| First trading day of a new listing | HSX | ±20%, **one day only** (day-1 close becomes day-2 reference) | 2021-07-05 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 11.1(b); VNX QĐ 17 Điều 31.6(a) | high |
| First trading day — durative trigger | HSX | ±20%, applied **"cho đến khi xác lập được giá từ kết quả khớp lệnh lô chẵn"** (until a round-lot matched price exists). Odd-lot orders may not be entered until then | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | VNX QĐ 22/2025 Điều 29.3(a); QĐ 22/2026 Điều 31.3(a). **The durative clause does not appear anywhere in QĐ 17** — it enters at KRX. Grepping "lô chẵn" across the three listed rulebooks shows it entering only in 2025 | high |
| First trading day | HNX | ±30% | 2022-03-31 | current | exchange | VNX QĐ 17 Phụ lục III §2.4; QĐ 22/2026 §2.4 adds the durative "cho đến khi giá đóng cửa được xác lập từ kết quả khớp lệnh lô chẵn" | high |
| First trading day | UPCOM | ±40%, until a reference price is established from continuous matching | 2022-11-16 | current | exchange | VNX QĐ 34 Điều 18.2(a); QĐ 23/2026 Điều 19.2(a). UPCoM already had the durative clause in 2022 | high |
| Resumption after a long suspension | HSX | ±20%. Reference is **not** carried forward — set by exchange decision | 2021-07-05 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 9.7(b), Điều 12; VNX QĐ 17 Điều 31.6(b), Điều 32.3 | high |
| Resumption after a long suspension | HSX | ±20%, plus the durative round-lot clause. Trigger widened to cover **đình chỉ** alongside **tạm ngừng** | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | QĐ 22/2025 Điều 29.3(b) | high |
| Resumption threshold — inclusivity | HSX, HNX | **>25 trading days** for 2021-07-05 → 2025-05-04; **≥25 consecutive trading days** from 2025-05-05 | 2021-07-05 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 9.7(b), Điều 12 say "trên hai mươi lăm (25) ngày giao dịch" (>25). **QĐ 17 contradicts itself**: Điều 31.6(b) and Điều 33.5 say "từ 25 … trở lên" (≥25) while its own Phụ lục III §1.4/§2.4 say "trên 25". QĐ 22/2025 harmonised the appendix to ≥25. **We adopt >25 to 2025-05-04** (both HOSE instruments and the QĐ 17 appendix agree) **and ≥25 from 2025-05-05.** The unit is trading days throughout — there is no calendar-vs-trading-day ambiguity | 2021-07-05 | current | exchange | as above | high |

### 3.4 Ceiling and floor arithmetic

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Base formula | ALL | `ceiling = ref + ref × band`; `floor = ref − ref × band`. Identical wording in every venue's rulebook and in the derivatives rulebook | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 9.2; QĐ 17 Điều 31.2; QĐ 22/2026 Điều 32.2; QĐ 23/2026 Điều 19.4; QĐ 21/2025 Điều 16.1 | high |
| Rounding direction | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Ceiling rounded **DOWN**, floor rounded **UP**, to the quotation unit — the band never widens by rounding. "làm tròn xuống đối với giá trần, làm tròn lên đối với giá sàn theo đơn vị yết giá" | 2021-07-05 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 9.1; QĐ 17 Điều 31.1; QĐ 22/2025 Điều 30.1; QĐ 22/2026 Điều 32.1; QĐ 23/2026 Điều 19.6 | high |
| **Which price selects the tick** | HSX | The tick used when rounding is the tick of the **RESULTING band price**, not of the reference price. Worked example: reference 48,000đ → raw ceiling 51,360đ; the reference sits in the 50đ tier but the result sits in the 100đ tier, and the published ceiling is **51,300** (result-keyed), not 51,350 (reference-keyed). **The rulebook is silent; the corpus settles it.** | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange (established empirically) | Corpus, `hermes-parquet` 2021-02-05→2022-12-30. On the full population of 179,784 HOSE stock name-days, result-keyed reproduces **97.60%** of ceilings and floors vs 95.06% / 94.96% reference-keyed. On the 4,605 ceiling / 4,811 floor rows where the two readings **disagree**, result-keyed matches **98.8% / 98.6%** and reference-keyed **0.0% / 0.0%** | high |
| — earlier inflated figure | HSX | An earlier pass reported "99.997% vs 98.22% on 151,005 name-days". That denominator was pre-filtered to rows already fitting ±7% on the HOSE grid, making the fit rate circular. **Use 97.60%.** The conclusion is unchanged and in fact stronger on the decisive rows | | | | corpus re-run | high |
| — testability | | Only testable at HOSE. HNX, UPCoM and HNXDS have a single uniform tick, so the question does not arise | | | | | high |
| Degenerate case 1 — computed limit equals the reference | ALL | If the computed ceiling **or** floor equals the reference (band × ref < one tick), **both** are reset: ceiling = ref + 1 tick, floor = ref − 1 tick | 2021-07-05 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 9.4; QĐ 17 Điều 31.3; QĐ 22/2025 Điều 30.3; QĐ 23/2026 Điều 19.5; QĐ 21 Điều 16.2 | high |
| — corpus confirmation | UPCOM | Common at the 100đ tick: at reference 200/300/400/500/600đ the corpus shows ceiling 300/400/500/600/700 and floor 100/200/300/400/500 — exactly ref ± 1 tick. At reference 700đ (15% = 105đ > 1 tick) the normal formula resumes (ceiling 800, floor 600) | 2021-02-05 | 2022-12-30 | market practice | corpus | high |
| — HOSE branch never exercised | HSX | Minimum HOSE reference in the corpus is 1,450đ; the branch needs ref < ~143đ (7% × ref < 10đ). **Textually verified, empirically untested.** | | | | corpus | high |
| Degenerate case 2 — adjusted floor ≤ 0 | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Floor is set **equal to the reference price** — not to zero and not to one tick. **The floor therefore never goes to zero or negative in this market; a simulator must clamp at the reference, not at zero** | 2021-07-05 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 9.5; QĐ 17 Điều 31.4; QĐ 22/2025 Điều 30.4; QĐ 34 Điều 18.6 | high |
| Degenerate case 3 — reference equals one tick | HSX, HNX | Ceiling = ref + 1 tick, floor = **ref**. **Not in QĐ 352** — this branch starts 2022-03-31 | 2022-03-31 | current | exchange | QĐ 17 Điều 31.5; QĐ 22/2025 Điều 30.5; QĐ 22/2026 Điều 32.5 | high |
| Degenerate case 3 | UPCOM | Same | 2022-11-16 | current | exchange | QĐ 34 Điều 18.7; QĐ 23/2026 Điều 19.7 | high |
| — corpus confirmation | UPCOM | 184 UPCoM name-days with reference = 100đ show ceiling 200đ and floor 100đ: a stock at the minimum tick simply cannot fall | 2021-02-05 | 2022-12-30 | market practice | corpus | high |
| Covered warrants — **no percentage band** | HSX | Limits derived from the underlying and the conversion ratio: `ceiling_CW = ref_CW + (ceiling_und − ref_und) × 1/CR`; `floor_CW = ref_CW − (ref_und − floor_und) × 1/CR`. If the computed warrant floor ≤ 0, floor = the smallest quotation unit (= 10đ) — **unlike stocks, which clamp at the reference** | 2021-07-05 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 9.3 ("đơn vị yết giá nhỏ nhất **bằng 10 đồng**"); QĐ 17 Điều 31.2(b) | high |
| Covered warrants — same value, changed wording | HSX | The explicit "bằng 10 đồng" is **dropped**; the text says only "đơn vị yết giá nhỏ nhất", resolving to 10đ via the uniform CW quotation unit in Phụ lục III §1.2. Do not quote "10 đồng" as gazetted text for this interval | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | QĐ 22/2026 Điều 32.2(b) | medium |
| — corpus confirmation | HSX | On 46,090 warrant name-days joined to their underlying, `(ceilCW−refCW)/(ceilUnd−refUnd)` equals `(refCW−floorCW)/(refUnd−floorUnd)` in **96.7%** of testable rows, and that ratio is essentially constant per warrant with clean values (0.2, 0.25, 1/3, 0.5, 1.0, 0.096) = 1/CR. **The floor-at-10đ branch is not an edge case: it fires on 16,275 of 46,090 warrant name-days (35%)**, because 7% of the underlying divided by the conversion ratio routinely exceeds the warrant's own price. Warrants are also why 45,664 corpus rows fit no percentage band at all | 2021-02-05 | 2022-12-30 | market practice | corpus | high |
| HOSE edge case — reference equals one tick (warrants context) | HSX | If the reference price equals one tick, ceiling = ref + 1 tick and floor = ref | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | HOSE 2025 regulation | medium |

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Reference price — normal session | HSX, HNX | Previous session's **closing price** | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 10.1; QĐ 17 Điều 32.2; QĐ 22/2025 Điều 31.2 | high |
| **Closing price definition — pre-KRX** | HSX, HNX | "Giá đóng cửa là giá thực hiện tại lần khớp lệnh cuối cùng trong ngày giao dịch. Trong trường hợp **không có giá thực hiện** trong ngày giao dịch, giá đóng cửa được xác định là **giá đóng cửa của ngày giao dịch gần nhất trước đó**" — the previous close **carries forward** | 2020-01-01 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 2.5; QĐ 17 Điều 3.17 | high |
| **Closing price definition — post-KRX (CHANGED)** | HSX, HNX | Closing price is the last **round-lot** match. "Trong trường hợp không có giá thực hiện khớp lệnh lô chẵn trong ngày giao dịch, giá đóng cửa được xác định là **giá tham chiếu đầu ngày**" | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | QĐ 22/2025 Điều 3.17; QĐ 22/2026 Điều 3.17 | high |
| — **why this matters, and it is the most consequential missed delta in this research** | HSX, HNX | The two readings diverge on **any no-trade session whose reference was not the prior close** — an ex-rights date, an exchange-decided resumption reference, a first listing day. Pre-2025 the *unadjusted* prior close rolls forward and silently undoes the corporate-action adjustment; from 2025-05-05 the *adjusted* reference rolls forward. Also: odd-lot prints can no longer set the close. **Any blanket claim that "nothing in this domain changed at KRX" is false** | | | | | high |
| Reference price — normal session | UPCOM | **Volume-weighted average of round-lot prices executed by CONTINUOUS MATCHING on the most recent preceding trading day.** Three exclusions: put-through trades, odd-lot trades, and any day with no continuous match (search steps back). Rounded **half-up** to 100đ | 2022-11-16 | current | exchange | QĐ 34 Điều 19.4 verbatim; QĐ 23/2026 Điều 20.4. Corroborated by an HNX summary. **Not a close** — this is a real per-venue difference | high |
| — rounding evidence, restated honestly | UPCOM | On 410,999 joined UPCoM name-days: half-up **98.70%**, round-down 97.03%, round-up 97.01% — a 1.7-point margin, not the 14 points an earlier pass reported (84.4% / 85.2% could not be reproduced). On the **18,298 rows where direction actually matters**, half-up wins 80.8% vs 43.3% / 42.8%; splitting further, above .5 half-up wins 78.0% vs round-down 7.7%, below .5 half-up wins 83.9% vs round-up 2.5%. **~19% of decisive rows match none of the three**, consistent with `quote_average` including put-through and odd-lot trades the rule excludes. Half-up is the right reading; it is not a knockout | 2021-02-05 | 2022-12-30 | market practice | corpus | high |

### 3.6 Corporate actions

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Gazetted **principle** for the ex-date reference | HSX, HNX | What is gazetted is a principle, not algebra: on the ex-rights date the reference is the most recent close "**điều chỉnh theo giá trị cổ tức được nhận hoặc giá trị của các quyền kèm theo**" | 2021-07-05 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 10.3; QĐ 17 Điều 32.4; QĐ 22/2026 Điều 33.4 | high |
| Same, VWAP-based | UPCOM | Identical except the base is the round-lot VWAP of the most recent session | 2022-11-16 | current | exchange | QĐ 34 Điều 19.5; QĐ 23/2026 Điều 20.5 | high |
| The actual adjustment **arithmetic** | ALL | `P' = (P + Σᵢ(Paᵢ × aᵢ) − C) / (1 + Σᵢaᵢ + Σⱼbⱼ)` where P = pre-ex base price (close at HOSE/HNX, round-lot VWAP at UPCoM), C = cash dividend per existing share, aᵢ = new shares per existing share from a rights issue at subscription price Paᵢ, bⱼ = new shares per existing share from a stock dividend or bonus (price zero). Degenerate forms: cash only `P' = P − C`; stock dividend ratio b `P' = P/(1+b)`; rights ratio a at Pa `P' = (P + Pa·a)/(1+a)`; split 1→n `P' = P/n`; consolidation m→1 `P' = P×m`. Then rounded to the quotation unit. Matching quantity rule: `qty × (1 + Σa + Σb)`, `× n` for a split, `÷ m` for a consolidation | 2020-01-01 | current | **market practice** | **NOT IN ANY GAZETTED DOCUMENT.** Broker and market-education sources only. **MARK THIS CLEARLY IN THE PAPER** — it is the one place in this domain where the traceability claim cannot be met. The conservation principle it encodes (market cap unchanged across the event) is what the sources agree on. **The rounding direction after adjustment is unspecified everywhere** | medium |
| Split and consolidation | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Reference on the day trading **resumes** = the close (HOSE/HNX) or round-lot VWAP (UPCoM) of the day before the event, adjusted by the ratio. | Trading-halt triggers | HSX, HNX | **Six** classes, verbatim: (a) automatic system trigger on abnormal price or volume, thresholds held as SSC-approved system parameters; (b) the issuer executes a **stock split or consolidation, a corporate demerger, or another charter-capital reduction**; (c) **partial conversion of convertible bonds into shares**; (d) the issuer fails to explain/remedy a warning, control or restriction within the exchange's deadline; (đ) at the SSC's request; (e) exchange discretion to protect investors and market stability, reported to VNX and the SSC. Decision within **5 working days** | 2022-03-31 | current | exchange | QĐ 17 Điều 40.1 and 40.2, read verbatim (an earlier pass reported only 3 classes from a law-firm summary and omitted exactly the two that matter) | high |
| — design consequence | | Triggers (b) and (c) are **corporate-action-driven halts** — which is why the split/consolidation reference rule is framed as a resumption. The simulator should **generate** those halts from its corporate-action feed and treat (a), (d), (đ), (e) as exogenous | | | | | high |
| RES triggers | HSX, HNX | Two: audited annual or reviewed semi-annual financial statements more than **45 days** late; repeat disclosure violation after being placed under control | 2022-03-31 | current | exchange | QĐ 17 Điều 39.1 | high |
| Automatic halt thresholds | ALL | **UNVERIFIED — never published.** "ngưỡng tạm ngừng … cài đặt bằng tham số trên hệ thống công nghệ thông tin sau khi được UBCKNN chấp thuận" | 2022-03-31 | current | exchange | QĐ 17 Điều 40.1(a) | UNVERIFIED |

### 3.7 Corpus hazards affecting band verification

| Finding | Detail | Confidence |
|---|---|---|
| **`quote_ticker.exchangeid` is a CURRENT attribute, not a dated one** | The corpus stores one venue per ticker, recorded as of the last update, so historical venue attribution is contaminated by transfers and delistings. Concretely: **1,842** name-days tagged HSX fit a 15%/100đ band exactly and **1,499** fit 10%/100đ (ATG, CLG, FTM, LCM, PXI, PXS, PXT, RIC, TS4, PDC and similar HOSE-delisted names); conversely **1,937** UPCoM-tagged rows fit HOSE's 7%/tier grid. **27,730** band rows have no exchange at all (mostly VN30F). `quote_ticker.startdate` and `expdate` are null in **0/2273** rows — the corpus carries no temporal venue information at all. **The band engine's own fitted band is a better venue label for historical rows than the ticker table.** | high |
| UPCoM band-fit coverage, restated honestly | Of 412,041 UPCoM stock name-days: 301,732 fit ±15%/100đ, 70,577 fit ±40%/100đ (90.4% together), 19,235 fit ±10%/100đ, 1,937 fit HOSE's ±7% tier grid, 953 fit ±20%, and **17,606 (4.3%) fit no percentage band at all**. "Almost all of the rest are ±40%" was wrong; ~9.6% of UPCoM name-days are not explained by UPCoM's own rules | high |
| Ceiling and floor are **transposed** in some rows | `quote_ceil` < `quote_floor` in **7 of 1,872** VN30F contract-days (0.37%) and 0 of 179,784 HOSE stock name-days. Example: VN30F2106 on 2021-02-08 carries ceil 1066.80 and floor 1227.20 against reference 1147.00. Any arithmetic assuming ordering must detect and repair this | high |
| The three band tables do not align | `quote_ceil` has 819,267 rows, `quote_floor` 819,266, `quote_reference` 816,949 | high |
| Band-table coverage | `quote_ceil`, `quote_floor`, `quote_reference` span **2021-02-05 → 2022-12-30 only** (816,820 joined name-days). Every empirical finding is validated on the QĐ 352 and QĐ 17 regimes; nothing tests the KRX era, the 2026 rulebook, or calendar 2020 | high |
| `quote_settlementprice` is **not** the daily settlement price | It holds intraday timestamped snapshots (microsecond resolution during the closing period), 3,223 rows over 2022-06-13 → 2022-12-15, including VN30IN index rows. It **cannot** be used to verify the futures reference-price rule; that needs VSDC's daily settlement series, which is in neither corpus | high |
| Reproduction artefacts | `bands2.parquet` in the session scratchpad carries 816,820 name-days of ceiling/floor/reference joined to exchange and instrument type, with a fitted band/tick label per row, and reproduces every empirical claim in §3 | high |

---

## 4. Tick size, trading unit and odd lots

### 4.1 Tick size (đơn vị yết giá)

**Note on the article citations below.** Under the VNX regime the operative article does
*not* carry the numbers. QĐ 17 Điều 12.4 reads "SGDCK thực hiện công bố thông tin các nội
dung về tổ chức hoạt động giao dịch … theo quy định tại **Phụ lục III** ban hành kèm theo
Quy chế này" — so for 2022-03-31 → 2025-05-04 Phụ lục III is *normative*. From QĐ 22/2025
the wording loosens to "theo thông báo của SGDCK" (Điều 27.1, 27.2, 28). Either way the
appendix was never obtained, so every number below from 2022-03-31 onward is corroborated,
not gazetted-verified.

| Odd lots — price-formation constraints | ALL | Three constraints a fill model must honour: (1) LO only, subject to the same amend/cancel rules as round lots; (2) odd-lot prices obey the same band and tick rules but **must not** be used to set the reference price or the index price; (3) odd-lot orders are **barred entirely** for a newly listed security, or one resuming after the 25-session threshold, until a price has been established from **round-lot** matching | 2022-03-31 | current | exchange | QĐ 17 Điều 33.2, 33.4, 33.5 (as amended by QĐ 30 to include covered warrants); QĐ 22/2025 Điều 32; QĐ 34 Điều 21 (UPCoM equivalent, worded against the continuous-matching reference price) | high |

It has **zero rows in `quote_close`**, so this is a **latent** defect, not a live tick error today. | Price representability limit | | `quote_close` is exactly representable at **2 decimal places in thousand VND for 100.0000% of 3,899,486 rows**, i.e. 10đ resolution. **A 1đ negotiated tick is therefore unrepresentable in the daily price series** — no put-through price in this corpus can ever be validated against the 1đ grid | | | | corpus | high |

---

## 5. Settlement, pre-funding and short selling

### 5.1 The cycle

**The single most important correction in this document.** The settlement *date* has been
T+2 since **2016-01-01**, not since 2022-08-29. What changed on 2022-08-29 was the **time
of day**, not the number of days. Our spec's "T+3 (to 2022-08-26), T+2 (from 2022-08-29)"
is wrong as a statement about cycle length — though its *behaviour* column (the day a
bought share first becomes sellable) is right, because settlement completed after the close
until 2022-08-26 and so the first sellable session was T+3.

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Cycle length — shares, fund certificates, covered warrants | ALL | **T+2** in VSDC settlement business days | 2016-01-01 | current | depository | VSD Decision 211/QĐ-VSD (2015-12-18, introduced T+2 from 2016-01-01, replacing T+3) — **existence, date and supersession confirmed from Decision 109/QĐ-VSD's own preamble**, which expressly replaces "Quyết định số 211/QĐ-VSD ngày 18/12/2015". Decision 109 Art. 4(4) verbatim: "Ngày thanh toán giao dịch cổ phiếu, chứng chỉ quỹ, chứng quyền có bảo đảm là ngày làm việc thứ hai liền kề sau ngày giao dịch (T+2)" | high |
| Settlement clock — pre-2022 | ALL | VSD completed securities settlement and the bank completed cash settlement between **15:30 and 16:00 on T+2** — after the close. Consequence: shares bought on T were credited too late to be sold on T+2, so the **first sellable session was T+3** | 2016-01-01 | 2022-08-26 | depository | Decision 211/QĐ-VSD, described as the baseline in HSC's client notice on Decisions 109 and 110 ("15h30-16h00 ngày T+2" → "11h00-11h30 ngày T+2") | high |
| — exact minute uncertain | | Vietstock (Sep 2015) and the VSDC history page word the 2016 change as moving completion to "**16h30** ngày T+2". The material fact — after the close, hence sellable only T+3 — is not in doubt. **Note also that VSDC's history page says settlement moved to "chiều T+2", which reads as though shares became tradeable on T+2 from 2016. They did not** | | | | | medium |
| Settlement clock — current | ALL | On T+2: members must have cash in the settlement account by **10:00**; bank balance check 10:00–11:30; VSDC executes securities settlement and the bank executes cash settlement **11:00–11:30** (extendable to **12:00** where settlement-support mechanisms are invoked); custodian members must allocate **both cash and securities** to client accounts no later than **13:00**; members report allocation results by **16:30** | 2022-08-29 | 2024-11-01 | depository | VSD Decision 109/QĐ-VSD (signed 2022-08-19, effective 2022-08-29), Art. 4 — **timetable read verbatim**. Companion Decision 110/QĐ-VSD on depository members | high |
| Settlement clock — current | ALL | **Assumed unchanged**, but the operative text is unread | 2024-11-02 | current | depository | VSDC Decision 48/QĐ-HĐTV (2024-10-30, eff. 2024-11-02) then Decision 39/QĐ-HĐTV (2025-04-29, eff. on KRX go-live). **Neither was read; three retrieval routes failed. VSDC's public settlement page carries the T+2 cycle but NO clock times at all.** The 13:00 / 16:30 deadlines for 2025-05-05 → 2026-08-25 rest on Decision 109's timetable plus continuity plus behavioural evidence | medium |
| — two distinct instants | | The **depository settlement instant** is 11:00–11:30 on T+2. The **client allocation deadline** is 13:00 on T+2 and is a *custodian-member obligation*, not the VSDC settlement moment. Our spec's phrase "delivery before 13:00" conflates them | | | | | high |
| First sellable instant | ALL | Open of **T+3** | 2016-01-01 | 2022-08-26 | depository | Vietstock body text: investors may now sell "trong phiên giao dịch chiều ngày T+2 **thay vì chờ đến ngày T+3**" | high |
| First sellable instant | ALL | **13:00 on T+2** — the start of the afternoon continuous session. Not sellable in the T+2 morning session | 2022-08-29 | current | depository | Decision 109 Art. 4 + the Government policy portal ("bán chứng khoán trong phiên giao dịch buổi chiều cùng ngày" on T+2); brokers credit somewhere between ~12:00 and 13:00, with 13:00 the regulatory backstop | high |
| — bar-level modelling | | Treat the position as unsellable through the T+2 **morning** bar and sellable from the first T+2 afternoon bar. On daily bars stamped midnight, a 13:00 threshold is not met by the T+2 bar, so `T+2 @ 13:00` behaves as T+3 — the conservative direction | | | | | high |
| Sale proceeds become spendable | ALL | Open of T+3 (same allocation event as securities) | 2016-01-01 | 2022-08-26 | depository | by symmetry with the row above | medium |
| Sale proceeds become spendable | ALL | No later than **13:00 on T+2**, in the **same** custodian-member allocation event as securities. **Unsettled sale proceeds are NOT automatically available for a same-day or T+1 purchase** | 2022-08-29 | current | depository | Decision 109 Art. 4 ("Chậm nhất 13h00"); Government policy portal | high |
| Cash and securities settle on the same cycle and at the same instant | ALL | Yes on both counts — by DVP at the depository and by a **single** allocation deadline at the broker. **Sell-then-rebuy on the same day is therefore not possible on settled cash alone**; without the advance product the buying power appears only in the T+2 afternoon | 2022-08-29 | current | depository | as above | high |
| Delivery versus payment | ALL | Securities transfer at VSDC **if and only if** the cash leg settles at the settlement bank: "việc chuyển giao chứng khoán tại VSDC diễn ra khi và chỉ khi việc thanh toán tiền được thực hiện tại NHTT (nguyên tắc DVP)". No partial settlement of a matched trade | 2020-01-01 | current | **depository practice** | VSDC's "Bù trừ và Thanh toán" page. **Kind corrected from `statutory`**: the previously cited Circular 119/2020/TT-BTC Art. 24 is *multilateral netting*, not DVP, and it sits in the CCP-gated Chapter IV, so it is not even in force. **No gazetted DVP article has been located** | medium |
| Cycle by instrument type | ALL | Equities / fund certificates / covered warrants **T+2**; listed corporate bonds **T+1** (settlement executed 14:00–14:30, client allocation before 15:00); government debt instruments **T+1**; privately placed corporate bonds **T+0**; carbon credits / emission allowances **T+0**; index futures **daily variation margin T+1**; government-bond futures **physical delivery E+3** where E is the last trading day | 2022-08-29 | current | depository | VSDC "Bù trừ và Thanh toán"; Decision 109 Art. 4(3) for bonds; VSDC Decisions 25/QĐ-HĐTV (debt instruments, 2025-04-16), 26/QĐ-HĐTV (derivatives, 2025-04-16), 27/QĐ-HĐTV (private bonds, 2023-08-10), 17/QĐ-HĐTV (carbon, 2026-04-29). **The bond intraday timetable was not re-verified against Decisions 48 and 39** | high |
| — derivatives wording correction | HNXDS | The VSDC page says "**Thanh toán lãi lỗ vị thế** được thực hiện theo phương thức thanh toán bằng tiền với thời gian thanh toán là ngày T+1" — that is **daily variation margin** settling T+1, not the contract cash-settling T+1. Final settlement at expiry is cash-settled against the index. This matters because `margin.py` is precisely a variation-margin model | 2020-01-01 | current | depository | VSDC page | high |
| No pre-2022-08-29 row for bond/derivative cycles | | **UNVERIFIED** for 2020-01-01 → 2022-08-28 | — | — | — | — | UNVERIFIED |
| Settlement banks | ALL | **BIDV** for base securities (equities, ETFs, CWs, listed corporate bonds); **State Bank of Vietnam** for government debt instruments; **Vietcombank** for privately placed bonds; **VietinBank** for derivatives | 2020-01-01 | current | depository | VSDC "Bù trừ và Thanh toán" page (current designations confirmed verbatim). **When each designation started was not established**, so the interval start is a placeholder | medium |
| Settlement calendar | ALL | **T+2 is counted in VSDC settlement business days, published annually as a settlement-holiday calendar SEPARATE from the exchange trading calendar.** Around Tết the settlement break can be longer than the trading break | 2020-01-01 | current | depository | VSDC Announcement 4228/TB-VSDC (2025-11-20), 2026 schedule | high |
| — worked example (Tết 2026) | ALL | VSDC closed **2026-02-16 → 2026-02-20** inclusive. T+2 trades of 2026-02-12 settled **2026-02-23**; of 2026-02-13 settled **2026-02-24**. T+1 trades of 2026-02-13 settled 2026-02-23 | 2026-02-16 | 2026-02-20 | depository | 4228/TB-VSDC, verified verbatim | high |

### 5.2 Pre-funding, non-pre-funding and failure

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Buy-side pre-funding | ALL | An investor may place a buy order only when sufficient cash is **already** in the trading account, except margin trading and (nominally) day trading. The obligation is on the **securities company** to refuse uncovered orders; the exchange never sees client cash; the depository enforces only at settlement | 2016-07-01 | 2021-02-14 | statutory | Circular 203/2015/TT-BTC (signed 2015-12-21, **effective 2016-07-01**, replacing Circular 74/2011/TT-BTC of 2011-06-01) Art. 7(2), verbatim: "ngoài trừ giao dịch ký quỹ, giao dịch trong ngày theo quy định tại Điều 9, Điều 10 Thông tư này, nhà đầu tư chỉ được đặt lệnh mua khi đã ký quỹ đủ tiền trên tài khoản giao dịch chứng khoán" | high |
| Buy-side pre-funding | ALL | Same 100%-at-entry rule | 2021-02-15 | current | statutory | Circular 120/2020/TT-BTC (signed 2020-12-31, effective 2021-02-15, replacing TT 203/2015) Art. 7(1)(a): "Nhà đầu tư chỉ được đặt lệnh mua chứng khoán khi có đủ tiền trên tài khoản giao dịch"; exceptions Art. 9 (margin) and Art. 10 (day trading) | high |
| — enforcement answer | | Broker-enforced at order entry (statutory duty); depository-enforced at settlement (underfunded trades are **excluded** from settlement); exchange not involved | | | | | high |
| Sell-side pre-funding | ALL | An investor may place a sell order only for permitted securities **already available** in the depository account. So shares bought but not yet settled cannot be sold. Where a client's sell orders would exceed holdings, the securities company **must refuse** if it cannot ensure enough securities to deliver on the settlement date | 2016-07-01 | current | statutory | Circular 203/2015 Art. 7; Circular 120/2020 Art. 7(3). **This is the statutory basis for the no-short-sale constraint on ordinary cash accounts**, independent of the covered-short-selling framework | high |
| Simultaneous opposite-side orders | ALL | Circular 120/2020 Art. 7 **also** prohibits placing buy and sell orders for the same security within the same matching session, with limited carve-outs. **Whether the ban is per matching session or per trading day was not resolved, and it changes admission logic** | 2021-02-15 | current | statutory | Circular 120/2020 Art. 7; predecessor TT 203/2015 Art. 7 from 2016-07-01. Carve-outs and exact clause number **UNVERIFIED** | medium |
| Non-pre-funding (NPF) for foreign institutions | ALL | Foreign **institutional** investors may place **share** buy orders without full cash at entry. The securities company assesses settlement risk and sets the required cash by agreement (which may be zero). Cash must reach the depository member's account at the settlement bank **no later than 10:15 on T+2** — a cut-off **not** changed at KRX | 2024-11-02 | current | statutory | Circular 68/2024/TT-BTC (signed 2024-09-18, effective 2024-11-02, amending Circulars 120/2020, 119/2020, 121/2020, 96/2020). **The 2024-11-02 date is 45 days after signing — the standard MoF default — which resolves the reported conflict against "effective on signing"** | medium |
| — scope limits | | Does **not** extend to foreign individuals or to any domestic investor; does **not** cover fund certificates, CWs or bonds; is **not** a CCP — the **broker**, not a clearing house, absorbs the shortfall | 2024-11-02 | current | statutory | as above | medium |
| — staged commencement? | | **UNVERIFIED.** Circular 68/2024's English-language disclosure obligations are widely understood to have begun later than its trading provisions; a flat single effective date for the whole circular may be wrong | — | — | — | — | UNVERIFIED |
| NPF shortfall handling | ALL | **Two-stage.** (1) At settlement the securities company "uses its own money to pay the transaction on behalf of the investor" and **freezes** the corresponding shares. (2) The investor has until **the end of the afternoon session of the next business day** to pay. (3) Only on continued failure does the company ask VSDC to transfer ownership to its **proprietary account**, after which it may sell on the exchange. Where FOL allows, it may instead transfer ownership to the investor. Losses and fees allocated by the broker-client agreement | 2025-05-05 | current | statutory | Circular 119/2020 Chapter IVa **Art. 40k**, as inserted by Circulars 68/2024 and 18/2025. **This corrects a materially mis-described mechanism**: an earlier pass omitted the pay-on-behalf and freeze stages entirely and rendered the investor's repayment deadline as a "disposal by T+3" deadline | high |
| — improvement at go-live | ALL | Shares now stay in the **investor's** account until end of T+3 where repayment is confirmed, instead of moving to the broker's proprietary account from T+2. **This preserves the foreign room attached to the position** | 2025-05-05 | current | statutory | Circular 18/2025/TT-BTC | medium |
| — disclosure | ALL | Failure to pay must be disclosed within 24 hours | 2024-11-02 | current | statutory | Circular 96/2020/TT-BTC Art. 25(8) as amended — **not** Circular 119/2020 | medium |
| — FOL still binds | ALL | When the broker transfers shares from its proprietary account to the foreign investor, the transfer must respect the foreign ownership limit. **This creates a second-order room path**: shares can sit briefly on a broker's proprietary account and the FOL is tested again at the onward transfer | 2024-11-02 | current | statutory | Circular 68/2024 | medium |
| Foreign investors and margin trading | ALL | **Prohibited.** Combined with pre-funding, this meant that before 2024-11-02 every foreign order had to be fully cash-covered at entry — the constraint FTSE Russell flagged | 2017-04-01 | current | statutory/regulatory | SSC Decision 87/QĐ-UBCK Art. 10(1)(đ) bars opening a margin account for "khách hàng là nhà đầu tư nước ngoài", effective 2017-04-01; re-enacted by Circular 120/2020 Art. 9 ("Foreign investor is not allowed to conduct margin trading") from 2021-02-15 | high |
| Equity margin floors | ALL | Initial margin ratio **≥50%**, maintenance ratio **≥30%**, both set by the securities company **above** those floors. Margin-eligible securities exclude anything listed under 6 months, under warning/control/suspension, or with a non-clean audit opinion | 2017-04-01 | current | statutory/regulatory | SSC Decision 87/QĐ-UBCK (signed 2017-01-25, effective 2017-04-01, replacing **both** Decision 637/QĐ-UBCK of 2011-08-30 **and** Decision 09/QĐ-UBCK of 2013-01-08) Art. 5(1) and 5(2) | high |
| — **do not conflate with derivatives margin** | | The 50%/30% equity floors are a **completely different regime** from the derivatives initial-margin rate `margin.py` models. See §6 | | | | | high |
| Unsettled purchases as margin collateral | ALL | Shares bought but not yet credited on T+2 are not in the depository account, cannot be sold, and are **assumed** not to count as collateral. **This is inference, not a rule** — and the previously offered reasoning ("the permissible-collateral list is closed") is **refuted**: Decision 87 Art. 13(5)(b) expressly allows other securities as collateral where agreed in writing. No broker rulebook confirming the exclusion was retrievable | 2017-04-01 | current | statutory/regulatory | Decision 87/QĐ-UBCK Arts. 3, 5, 13(5)(b) | low |
| Failed settlement — support fund machinery | ALL | Where a depository member is short of cash, VSDC draws on the **Settlement Support Fund**: the defaulting member's own contribution first, then other members' contributions subject to a ceiling of **50 tỷ đồng per day** unless reported to the SSC, with repayment due **within 01 ngày** plus enforcement (trading suspension, asset liquidation). If cash still cannot be found, the securities company blocks the cash it holds, notifies VSDC of the underfunded trades, and **VSDC excludes those trades from settlement ("loại bỏ thanh toán")** | 2024-11-02 | current | statutory | Circular 119/2020 **Chapter IVa Art. 40l**, as inserted by Circulars 68/2024 and 18/2025; VSDC Decision 40/QĐ-HĐTV (2025-04-29). **Re-dated from 2021-02-15**: the unamended Chapter IV support-fund article (Art. 39) is CCP-gated. Wording is "01 **ngày**", not one *business* day; "plus penalty interest" is unsupported | medium |
| — 2021-02-15 → 2024-11-01 | ALL | **UNVERIFIED.** Whether equivalent machinery existed outside Chapter IV in the unamended Circular 119/2020 was not established | — | — | — | — | UNVERIFIED |
| Settlement deferral / cancellation **abolished** | ALL | The old remedy of deferring or cancelling settlement of a trade with short cash is removed. Instead: (i) borrow securities through the lending/matching system to make delivery; (ii) draw the Payment Support Fund for the **full** shortfall rather than a capped amount; (iii) VSDC may freeze and sell securities in a defaulting member's proprietary account. **A simulator should treat every matched trade as settling — failed settlement is no longer a modelled outcome for the buyer** | 2025-05-05 | current | statutory | Circular 18/2025/TT-BTC (signed 2025-04-26, effective 2025-05-05, amending Circulars 119/2020 and 96/2020; Chapter IVa) — **confirmed from the official gazette at vanban.chinhphu.vn**. This is also the gazetted anchor pinning the KRX go-live to a calendar date | medium |
| Failure remedies under the CCP regime | ALL | **NOT IN FORCE.** Borrow through the VSDC lending system; defer settlement by at most **3 business days** with compensation of **5% of the deferred securities' value per deferred day**; or a mandatory buy-in through the exchange. VSDC may refuse novation and exclude a trade (e.g. a sale of securities not owned, or a trade after a suspension notice), with compensation of **20%** of the refused trade value | not in force (CCP deployment, target Q1 2027) | — | statutory | Circular 119/2020 **Chapter IV** = "Clearing and settlement under the CCP mechanism", Arts. 24–40. Art. 36 carries the 3-day/5% rule verbatim; Art. 37(2) the 20%; Art. 39 states these run "from the official deployment date of the CCP mechanism". **Confirmed CCP-gated** — the earlier low-confidence inference was right | high (that they are not yet in force) |
| Whether settlement failure can affect a simulation | ALL | Settlement failure in Vietnam is a **member-level** event, not an investor-level one, and is absorbed by the support fund, borrowing, buy-in or trade exclusion before it reaches an investor. **The realistic investor-visible failure mode is a late allocation, not an unwound fill.** It is defensible to omit failed-settlement modelling entirely and say so | 2020-01-01 | current | market practice | Interpretation, flagged as such | medium |
| Advance on sale proceeds (ứng trước tiền bán) — legal status | ALL | A named, licensable service: a brokerage-licensed securities company may "cung cấp hoặc phối hợp với các tổ chức tín dụng cung cấp dịch vụ ứng trước tiền bán chứng khoán". Requires **prior written SSC approval**; the SSC has sanctioned firms for offering it without approval (reported 2025-11) | 2021-01-01 | current | statutory | Luật Chứng khoán 54/2019/QH14 Art. 86(1)(b), **confirmed word for word** | high |
| — why it is not a broker loan | ALL | A securities company may not lend money or securities in any form, except (i) margin lending under MoF guidance and (ii) lending securities to correct trade errors or effect ETF unit swaps. So the advance must be structured as an advance against the client's own receivable and/or arranged with a credit institution | 2021-02-15 | current | statutory | Circular 121/2020/TT-BTC (2020-12-31, eff. 2021-02-15) Art. 27 "Hạn chế cho vay" — **read in summary form only, not verbatim** | low |
| — the label is SPLIT | | The permission to offer the product is **statutory**; the price and the cap are **broker commercial terms** | | | | | high |
| Short selling of cash equity | ALL | **Not available at any point in 2020-01-01 → 2026-08-25.** Naked shorting is barred by the sell-side pre-funding rule. Covered short selling exists **on paper** — borrow through the VSDC securities lending system, then repurchase to repay — but has never been switched on, because the VSDC SBL system and the SSC-published eligible-securities list are not in production | 2020-01-01 | current | statutory | Circular 120/2020 Art. 7(3) and **Art. 11** "Giao dịch bán không có bảo đảm" (eligible list must meet liquidity and disclosure criteria; **barred in the 5 business days before a record date**; SSC may suspend); predecessor TT 203/2015 Art. 11 from 2016-07-01. The Nghị định 155/2020 article said to ground it was **not located** | high |
| — the honest phrasing | | Say "framework exists since 2016 (TT 203/2015 Art. 11) and re-enacted in 2020 (TT 120/2020 Art. 11), **never operationalised**" rather than "prohibited", which is legally imprecise | | | | | high |
| Day trading (T+0) for cash equity | ALL | **Legally provided for, never operational.** The investor signs a day-trading contract with a securities company that provides securities lending, and total buys must equal total sells of that security within the day, with the contract authorising borrowing or mandatory buy-in to cover shortfalls. Not operational for the same reason as short selling: no VSDC SBL system | 2016-07-01 | current | statutory | Circular 120/2020 Art. 10 and 10(2)(d) from 2021-02-15; TT 203/2015 Art. 10 from 2016-07-01 | high |
| — what retail actually does | | "Lướt T0" is achieved by selling an existing position and rebuying with an advance on proceeds — economically similar, legally a different thing, and it costs the advance fee | | | | | high |
| **The load-bearing backtest constraint** | ALL | Across the **entire** window, no short sale and no intraday round trip of the same shares is admissible on the cash market. A backtest that sells shares it does not hold, or buys and sells the same shares on the same day, is simulating a market that does not exist. Turnover is capped by the settlement cycle unless the advance facility is modelled **and charged for**. (Derivatives are different — shorting a futures contract has always been permitted) | 2020-01-01 | current | statutory | as above | high |
| Reform roadmap — short selling, SBL, T+0 | ALL | Targeted **2026–2028**, gated on stable CCP operation (target **Q1 2027**). At the MSCI June 2026 review, short selling and securities lending were still cited as unmet criteria. **Nothing here takes effect inside the window** | not in force | — | statutory | MoF Decision 2014/2025 implementing the market-upgrade programme (three pillars), referenced with Decision 3761/QĐ-BTC; SSC CCP plan announced 2025-07-18 (roadmap: Q3/2025–Q1/2026 prepare a VSDC subsidiary; Q1–Q2/2026 a circular replacing TT 119/2020; 2026 member readiness; CCP live Q1/2027). **Decision numbers 2014/2025 and 3761/QĐ-BTC come from secondary reporting; neither document was retrieved** | low |
| Foreign access via global brokers | ALL | Foreign investors may place orders through a global broker without opening an account at each Vietnamese securities company; they must still register a trading code and hold a custody account at a depository member, and settlement still runs through VSDC. **Relaxes no FOL or room rule** | **UNVERIFIED** (issuance 2026-02-03; effective date unstated — a 45-day default would give 2026-03-20, which is inference) | — | statutory | Thông tư 08/2026/TT-BTC. Number corroborated by the 2026 VNX Quy chế's citation and by FTSE reporting; content read only in a news summary | low |
| FTSE Russell reclassification | ALL | Frontier → Secondary Emerging, index inclusion beginning **2026-09-21** and completing September 2027. **Outside the window** (after 2026-08-25) | not in window | — | market practice | FTSE Russell interim review confirmation, as reported | medium |
| Custody-account structure for foreign investors | ALL | Per trading code, a foreign investor may open only **one** securities custody account at **one** custodian bank; separately, at each securities company only one custody account | 2021-08-16 | current | statutory | Circular 51/2021/TT-BTC (signed 2021-06-30, **effective 2021-08-16** — both confirmed from the government gazette). **The substantive one-account rule sits in an unread PDF attachment; only the cover metadata was read** | low |

| Daily settlement price (DSP) | HNXDS | **UNVERIFIED** for 2020-01-01 → 2022-05-31. The 2022-06-01 rule explicitly *excludes* ATO and negotiated prices as a **change**, which itself proves a different earlier rule existed. QĐ 96/QĐ-VSD (2017-03-23, eff. 2017-05-01) is the governing text and was never read | 2020-01-01 | 2022-05-31 | depository | — | UNVERIFIED |
| Daily settlement price (DSP) | HNXDS | The contract template delegates the DSP to VSDC ("Theo quy định của VSD"). VSDC determines it from the day's futures trades: the **closing (ATC) price** of that contract, and where no closing price can be determined, a **volume-weighted average of matched prices**. Prices from the **opening call auction (ATO)** and from **negotiated (thỏa thuận)** trades are **EXCLUDED** from the volume-weighted computation. Result rounded to **2 decimals** | 2022-06-01 | current | depository | Quyết định 61/QĐ-VSD (2022-05-16, effective 2022-06-01) as reported by Thời báo Tài chính Việt Nam and legal-database summaries; contract template row 15. **Article text not read** | medium |
| — DSP fallback chain incomplete | HNXDS | **UNVERIFIED**: what happens when a contract has **no matched trades at all** during the day | — | — | — | — | UNVERIFIED |
| — why the DSP matters | HNXDS | The DSP is the **reference price** for the next day's band (§3.5) *and* the mark for variation margin. The VN30F reference equals the prior close on only 86.5% of corpus contract-days, so substituting the close mis-sets the band roughly one contract-day in 7.4 | | | | | high |
| Final settlement price (FSP) — old regime | HNXDS | "**Giá trị đóng cửa của chỉ số cơ sở tại ngày giao dịch cuối cùng**" — the single VN30 index value produced by HOSE's closing call auction | 2017-08-10 | 2022-06-15 | exchange | Mẫu HĐTL Chỉ số VN30, pre-2022 edition, row 16; MBS and BSC guides. **Last expiry settled under this rule was VN30F2205 on 2022-05-19** | high |
| Final settlement price (FSP) — current regime | HNXDS | The **simple arithmetic average of the VN30 index over the last 30 minutes of the last trading day** (15 minutes of continuous matching plus the 15-minute closing call auction), **after excluding the 3 highest and the 3 lowest index values of the CONTINUOUS-matching session**. The ATC value(s) are not trimmed | 2022-06-16 | current | exchange | Quyết định 61/QĐ-VSD (2022-05-16, regulation effective 2022-06-01); contract template post-2022 edition row 16; VnEconomy 2022-05-26 | high |
| — the behavioural boundary is 2022-06-16, not 2022-06-01 | HNXDS | The regulation took effect 2022-06-01 but the first contract it applied to was VN30F2206, expiring 2022-06-16. **A simulator that settles expiries at the closing index value will be wrong for every expiry from 2022-06-16 onward** | | | | | high |
| — the 14:15–14:45 window is **derived** | HNXDS | Neither the template nor the reporting of QĐ 61 gives clock times; they say "the last 30 minutes … comprising 15 minutes of continuous matching and 15 minutes of the closing periodic auction". 14:15–14:45 follows from the session table, which itself rests on a VNX notice rather than a gazetted rule | | | | | medium |
| — **sampling frequency UNVERIFIED, and it is load-bearing** | HNXDS | The rule averages "giá trị chỉ số" without stating the dissemination frequency, so the sample size — and hence how much the 6-value trim removes — is unknown. **The FSP cannot be implemented exactly without it** | — | — | — | — | UNVERIFIED |
| — which instrument changed the FSP | HNXDS | **Resolved.** VNX QĐ 20/21 Điều 5(1)(u) lists "Phương pháp xác định giá thanh toán cuối cùng" as a template term qualified "(theo Quy chế nghiệp vụ của Tổng công ty Lưu ký và Bù trừ Chứng khoán Việt Nam)". **The exchange rulebook expressly delegates the FSP to VSDC**, so QĐ 61/QĐ-VSD is the source of the rule and the contract template reproduces it. Same delegation applies to the DSP (Điều 5(1)(t)) and the margin level (Điều 5(1)(v)) | | | | QĐ 21 Điều 5(1)(t)(u)(v) | high |
| — asymmetry worth recording | HNXDS | HNX's template spells the **FSP** formula out in full while leaving the **DSP** as "theo quy định của VSDC" | | | | | high |

### 6.3 Margin — the shape, which is not the shape our code models

**Read this section as two regimes, not one.** VSDC **QĐ 26/QĐ-HĐTV (2025-04-16)** and its
**Phụ lục 2** were obtained and read in full on **2026-08-26** (provenance: §9.6). They
redefine the margin stack from the KRX go-live, and the rows below are scoped accordingly.
**2017-05-01 → 2025-05-04** is the pre-KRX regime, described from VSDC's own web material
sitting on top of the QĐ 96 → QĐ 61 → QĐ 12 chain, **not one text of which anyone has
read**. **2025-05-05 → current** is QĐ 26, read verbatim; its rows are in the second table
of this section.

**The finding that survives both regimes.** Neither VSDC nor HNX/VNX publishes a
**maintenance margin ratio** for VN30 index futures at any date in 2020–2026. The only
published ratio is the **initial** margin ratio (per contract) and, for government bond
futures, the **delivery** margin ratio. A US-style
"maintenance-margin-as-a-fraction-of-notional" test **does not exist in Vietnam**. For the
post-KRX regime this is now a primary-sourced negative rather than an inference: the
strings **`ký quỹ duy trì`** (maintenance margin), **`ký quỹ biến đổi`** (variation margin)
and **`thời gian thực`** (real time) occur **zero times in QĐ 26 and zero times in Phụ lục
2**. The post-KRX requirement is a scenario-risk number computed **once, after the close**.

**What did NOT survive the primary read.** The **80 / 90 / 100** ladder this document
previously attributed to *margin utilisation* via "Article 13" is **not in Điều 13 of
QĐ 26**. Điều 13 contains no percentage of any kind; it is a binary test. The only
80/90/100 in QĐ 26 is **Điều 29**, and it measures the **position limit**. For the pre-KRX
regime the ladder is **not disproven — it is unverified**, because the citation chain we
relied on (QĐ 96 → QĐ 61 → QĐ 12 → QĐ 26) is now **broken at its final link** and the two
middle links have never been read. See the correction rows below and §9.6.

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Initial margin ratio — launch | HNXDS | **10%** | 2017-08-10 | 2018-07-17 | depository | Mẫu HĐTL Chỉ số VN30 row 19 ("Tỷ lệ ký quỹ ban đầu: 10%"); Nhân Dân's report of the 10→13% adjustment. **Outside the window, included to establish the series** — and note both SSI contract-template PDFs still (stalely) print 10% | high |
| Initial margin ratio | HNXDS | **13%** | 2018-07-18 | 2022-12-14 | depository | VSD announcement dated 2018-07-13, effective 2018-07-18, with SSC approval; Nhân Dân; PHS/Vietstock 2019 ("tỷ lệ ký quỹ ban đầu hiện nay … là 13%"); MBS's own sheet prints "Mức ký quỹ tại VSD — Tỷ lệ ký quỹ: 13%". **This is the value in force at the start of our window** | high |
| Initial margin ratio | HNXDS | **17%** | 2022-12-15 | current | depository | VSD notice of 2022-12-12, effective 2022-12-15, with the unanimous agreement of the SSC, on a periodic VaR recalculation. On the assessment date the permissible VaR-derived band was **8.4% – 18.3%** | high |
| — verified still in force at the end of the window | HNXDS | VSDC "PHỤ LỤC 4 — Thông báo tỷ lệ ký quỹ áp dụng cho HĐTL chỉ số VN30 và VN100", effective **2026-08-21** (published 2026-08-19), gives **0.17** for every listed VN30 and VN100 contract. Interior confirmations at effective dates 2024-08-16, 2024-11-22, 2024-12-20, 2025-12-19 | 2022-12-15 | 2026-08-21 | depository | VSDC appendices | high |
| — continuity caveat | HNXDS | Notices were also issued effective 2026-02-23, 2026-03-20, 2026-04-17, 2026-05-22, 2026-06-19 and 2026-07-17, and **those xlsx attachments were not opened**; nothing between 2022-12-15 and 2024-08-16 was sampled either. Given the 1st/10th/20th review cadence a change is possible at any of them. **State the interval as verified-at-sampled-dates, or open every notice** | | | | | medium |
| — **no `quyết định` number exists** | HNXDS | The 10→13→17% changes were issued as **thông báo** (notices) under a standing delegation in the clearing rulebook. **Citing "Quyết định XX/QĐ-VSD set margin to 17%" would be citing something that does not exist.** VSDC announcement 199445 is a notice with an xlsx attachment and no decision number | | | | | high |
| — **17.5% matches nothing** | HNXDS | No source at any date gives 17.5%. Broker sheets variously print 10%, 13% and 17%, none dated; the VN30F contract spec in one cache gives 10%. **`margin.py`'s 0.175 looks like a transcription slip for 0.17** and must be replaced by a dated schedule, not re-parameterised | | | | | high |
| Initial margin is published **per contract** | HNXDS | VSDC publishes the ratio as a table keyed on each individual listed contract (product name, 9-char derivatives code, ISIN), with a separate "tỷ lệ ký quỹ ban đầu bảo đảm thực hiện hợp đồng" (delivery margin) column that reads "–" for index futures. Every entry has been 17% since 2022-12-15, but **the ratio is capable of differing across expiries of the same product** — one of VSD's stated inputs is time to maturity. **The correct data structure is `(contract_code, effective_date) → ratio`, not a scalar** | 2022-12-15 | current | depository | VSDC PHỤ LỤC 4, effective 2026-08-21 | high |
| How VSD determines and revises the ratio | HNXDS | From (a) volatility of the futures trade price or the underlying index over an observation period of **at least 90 trading days**, assessed by a **VaR** method; (b) the contract's **time to maturity**; (c) other factors VSD considers necessary. **Re-determined periodically on the 1st, 10th and 20th of each month** (next business day if non-working) and published **at least 2 business days before** it applies. VSD may re-assess on actual market movements, effective the business day after publication. Clearing members must then set their own ratio **no lower** than VSD's | 2017-05-01 | current | depository | VSDC's official "Thông tin về ký quỹ" page §II.1(d)–(h), citing Quy chế ký quỹ, bù trừ và thanh toán CKPS issued with **Quyết định 96/QĐ-VSD ngày 23/3/2017, Phụ lục 02** — verbatim Vietnamese available on that page | high |
| Initial margin (IM) — definition and formula | HNXDS | IM is the minimum margin the clearing member must post to VSD for positions in its name that it intends to open, **before trading** — **except for offsetting trades on the same trading account** ("giao dịch đối ứng của cùng một tài khoản giao dịch"), which attract **no new IM**. `IM = ratio × contracts × price × multiplier`, where price is the **latest matched price at the moment of calculation** in-session, or the **daily settlement price** when computed at end of day | 2017-05-01 | current | depository | VSDC "Thông tin về ký quỹ" §II.1(a), §IV.1; formula per QĐ 61/QĐ-VSD Art. 5 as summarised | high |
| — **IM is recomputed on the CURRENT price** | HNXDS | The required amount moves with the market **independently of P&L**. It is not a fixed fraction of the entry notional | | | | | high |
| Variation margin (VM) — definition | HNXDS | Determined from intraday position P&L of open positions on the investor's trading account: for positions already on the account, the in-session updated trade price versus the **previous trading day's DSP** (for carried positions) or versus the position's **opening settlement price** (for positions opened during the day); for positions closed during the day, the closing settlement price versus the same baselines. **CRITICALLY: VM enters the required maintenance margin ONLY when the account portfolio's position P&L is in a LOSS state. | — the broker's real lever | | `broker_buffer` as a percentage-of-notional add-on is a plausible *shape*, and keeping it separate from `vsd_initial` is the right design — but **the broker's actual lever in Vietnam is its UTILISATION thresholds (75/85/90 style)**, which have no representation in our config | | | | | high |
| Clearing-member top-up deadline at VSD — **RESOLVED 2026-08-26 for the post-KRX regime** | HNXDS | **Before 09h30 on the next trading day.** Verbatim: "Trường hợp giá trị tài sản ký quỹ trên tài khoản nhà đầu tư nhỏ hơn giá trị ký quỹ yêu cầu, thành viên bù trừ có trách nhiệm nộp bổ sung **trước 09h30 ngày giao dịch liền kề tiếp theo**." So the broker pages that gave "09:30 T+1" were reporting the **real VSDC deadline**, not a broker term — the row that called them broker deadlines was wrong. The separate **08:45** figure for ATC breaches is still broker-only and appears nowhere in QĐ 26 | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | QĐ 26 **Điều 13 khoản 1**, read verbatim | high |
| — the *position P&L* payment is a different deadline and is not a margin top-up | HNXDS | QĐ 26 Điều 20.1 settles position P&L "**bằng tiền vào ngày làm việc liền kề sau ngày VSDC thông báo khoản lãi lỗ vị thế**" — cash, on the business day after notification. Điều 25.2(a) fixes **09h30 on the settlement day** as the moment the member's payment-account balance is struck to test solvency for that payment (and **11h00** for the GB-futures physical-settlement cash leg). **These are settlement deadlines, not margin deadlines**, and conflating them is what previously produced a "T+1 variation-margin payment" in the margin section | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | QĐ 26 Điều 20.1, Điều 25.2(a)(b) verbatim | high |
