#### The post-KRX margin stack — QĐ 26/QĐ-HĐTV and Phụ lục 2, read verbatim

Every row below is `effective_from` **2025-05-05** and `kind` **depository**, from VSDC
**Quyết định 26/QĐ-HĐTV ngày 16/4/2025** ("Quy chế bù trừ và thanh toán giao dịch chứng
khoán phái sinh") and its **Phụ lục 2 — Phương pháp xác định các giá trị ký quỹ**. QĐ 26
Điều 2 replaces **QĐ 12/QĐ-HĐTV ngày 10/8/2023** and takes effect "**kể từ ngày Hệ thống
công nghệ thông tin của gói thầu … chính thức vận hành**" — the KRX go-live, which this
document dates 2025-05-05 (§6.5). The date is therefore *mechanism-verbatim, calendar-
inferred*, exactly as for VNX QĐ 21.

`high` below means the Vietnamese was read in QĐ 26 or Phụ lục 2 at the article cited.
Where a formula or a term did not survive extraction from the source PDF, or where the
arithmetic requires a step the text does not write down, the row says so and is **not**
`high`.

| **Margin requirement (MR) — composition** | HNXDS | `MR = Max(ΣPgm, 0)`, summed over underlying-asset groups, **per investor trading account and per clearing-member own account**. Verbatim: "`MR = Max (ΣPgm,0)`", "MR: Ký quỹ yêu cầu đối với **một tài khoản nhà đầu tư, thành viên bù trừ**" | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 6.1** verbatim; QĐ 26 Điều 5.5 | high |
| **Per-group margin (Pgm)** | HNXDS | `Pgm = Max((Rm + Sm + Dm), MM)` — verbatim "`Pgm = Max ((Rm  + Sm + Dm), MM)`" — where **Rm** = ký quỹ rủi ro (scenario risk margin), **Sm** = ký quỹ song hành (basis margin), **Dm** = ký quỹ chuyển giao (delivery margin), **MM** = ký quỹ tối thiểu (minimum margin). The `Max(…, MM)` is a **floor, not an add-on**: a group whose risk components fall below MM pays MM, not `risk + MM` | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 6.2** verbatim | high |
| — **variation margin is NOT a component of MR** | HNXDS | There are four summands and VM is not among them. Position P&L is a **separate daily cash settlement** under Điều 20: "Việc thanh toán lãi lỗ vị thế … được thực hiện **bằng tiền vào ngày làm việc liền kề sau ngày VSDC thông báo** khoản lãi lỗ vị thế", determined per investor account and then **netted to one obligation per clearing member** (Điều 20.2(b)). **`MR = IM + VM` is a pre-KRX statement and must not be applied after 2025-05-04** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | QĐ 26 Điều 20.1–20.3; exhaustive string search of both texts | high |
| — the unit of assessment is the **GROUP within the ACCOUNT** | HNXDS | Pre-KRX the regulated unit was the account portfolio. Post-KRX there is a level **below** it: MR sums `Pgm` over *nhóm tài sản cơ sở*, so one account holding VN30 and GB futures produces **at least two** `Pgm` computations that are then summed. The `Max(…, 0)` at the outer level cannot rescue a negative — no `Pgm` can be negative, since each is a `Max(…, MM)` and MM ≥ 0 — so the outer `Max` is defensive drafting | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 6.1–6.2 | high (the rule) / medium (that the outer `Max` is inoperative) |
| **Rm — ký quỹ rủi ro (scenario risk margin)** | HNXDS | "Ký quỹ rủi ro là **giá trị tuyệt đối của khoản lỗ lớn nhất** trong số các khoản lỗ (Lk) xác định theo các kịch bản biến động giá của tài sản cơ sở." Per scenario *k*: `Lk = Pm × (Sk − S) × M + Pb × (S − Sk) × M`, where **Pm** = số dư vị thế mua (long balance), **Pb** = số dư vị thế bán (short balance), **Sk** = underlying price in scenario *k*, **S** = **closing price of the underlying** on the determination date, **M** = contract multiplier | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 1.1** verbatim; QĐ 26 Điều 5.1 | high |
| — Rm is marked on the **underlying's close**, not the futures price | HNXDS | `S` is "**Giá đóng cửa của tài sản cơ sở** tại ngày xác định" throughout — the VN30 index close, not the DSP of the contract. The futures price enters the margin stack only through `Sm` (which uses DSP/FSP *changes*) and `Dm`. **A simulator that scenario-shocks the futures price is modelling the wrong variable** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 1.1, 3.3 | high |
| — **how the offsetting amount reduces Rm is NOT written down** | HNXDS | QĐ 26 Điều 5.1.1 says VSDC determines Rm "**căn cứ vào tỷ lệ ký quỹ ban đầu và giá trị giảm trừ ký quỹ**", and defines the offsetting amount as "**số tiền điều chỉnh giảm giá trị ký quỹ rủi ro**" — an amount that reduces the risk margin. But **neither QĐ 26 nor Phụ lục 2 writes the combining arithmetic.** `Rm = \|min Lk\| − OA` is the obvious reading and is what we would implement, but **it is our inference, not the text**, and the text does not say whether the result is floored at zero | — | — | — | Gap in Phụ lục 2 mục 1 and mục 2 | **UNVERIFIED** |

The table enumerates scenarios 1…21 against underlying prices **S−10, S−9, S−8, …, S+8, S+9, S+10**, with the index bounded **−10 ≤ k ≤ 10** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 1.2** and its table, verbatim | high |
| **The scenario price formula** | HNXDS | As printed in every row of the table: "`Sk = S0 x (1 + tỷ lệ ký quỹ ban đầu/10)`", with "S0: Giá của tài sản cơ sở tại ngày xác định; Sk: Giá của tài sản cơ sở trong kịch bản k; −10 ≤ k ≤ 10". **As printed this formula is degenerate** — no `k` appears on the right-hand side, so it returns the same price for all 21 scenarios and the grid collapses | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 1.2 table, verbatim | high (that this is what the text prints) |
| — **the reconstruction is OURS, and it is an inference** | HNXDS | The formula that makes the table work is **`Sk = S0 × (1 + k × r/10)`** where `r` is the initial margin ratio: it reproduces the S−10…S+10 column exactly, steps the grid in tenths of the margin rate, and puts the endpoints at `S0 × (1 ± r)` — i.e. the grid spans exactly ±1 initial-margin-ratio, which is the only reading under which "dựa trên tỷ lệ ký quỹ ban đầu" means anything. The `k ×` is almost certainly a superscript or inline-math run **dropped by PDF extraction**, since the identical string is repeated in all 21 table rows. **Implement the reconstruction, but record it as reconstructed. Confirm against the original PDF before the paper cites a formula** | — | — | — | Inference from the table's own column labels | **low** (that our reconstruction is the gazetted formula) |
| — practical consequence at r = 17% | HNXDS | The grid runs from `0.83 × S0` to `1.17 × S0` in steps of `0.017 × S0`. For a single-underlying, single-expiry position the worst `Lk` is always an endpoint, so `Rm` reduces to `\|position\| × r × S × M` — **numerically identical to a flat initial-margin charge**. The 21 scenarios only start to matter once a group holds **offsetting** positions across underlyings | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Arithmetic on Phụ lục 2 mục 1.1–1.2 | high (given the reconstruction above) |
| **Initial margin ratio — the VaR method** | HNXDS | "Tỷ lệ ký quỹ ban đầu được tính toán theo phương pháp định lượng rủi ro **VaR (phương pháp tham số)** dựa trên tỷ lệ phần trăm **biến động giá 2 ngày (2 days return)** của tài sản cơ sở và **độ tin cậy là 99.73%**." The VaR itself: **`VaR = (mean + 3 × δ)`**, where `δ` is the standard deviation and `mean` the arithmetic mean of the set of 2-day underlying returns over one observation cycle, each return comparing the price on day **T** with the price on day **T−2** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 1.3(a) and (c)** verbatim | high |
| — 99.73% and `mean + 3δ` are the same statement | HNXDS | 99.73% is the two-sided 3σ mass of a normal; `mean + 3δ` is the one-sided 3σ quantile. The text asserts both, which is internally consistent **only** under an explicit normality assumption — this is the "phương pháp tham số" (parametric method) doing the work. No distributional test is prescribed | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 1.3(a), (c) | high (the text) / medium (the reading) |
| — **CONFLICT inside Phụ lục 2 on the observation window: 120 vs 250** | HNXDS | Point **(a)** says "trong khoảng thời gian **tối thiểu 120 ngày giao dịch** liền trước ngày tính toán". Point **(b)**, two lines later and describing the same calculation for index and GB futures, says "trong **kỳ quan sát tối thiểu là 250 ngày giao dịch**". **Both are minima, so they are not strictly contradictory — 250 satisfies 120 — but they cannot both be the binding parameter.** Adopt neither as *the* window; treat the window as a VSDC discretion bounded below by 120. Note this also conflicts with the pre-KRX "at least 90 trading days" recorded from VSDC's page | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 1.3(a) vs 1.3(b) | high (that both strings are present) / **UNVERIFIED** (which binds) |

A √n scaling is the market-standard shape and would be the guess; **we do not record a guess**. Obtaining this one line is the highest-value follow-up in this section | — | — | — | Gap in Phụ lục 2 mục 1.3(c) | **UNVERIFIED** |

| **Offsetting amount (giá trị giảm trừ ký quỹ, OA)** | HNXDS | `OA = (B + S) × C × Psr`, where **B** = risk margin on one standard contract with **positive** delta, **S** = risk margin on one standard contract with **negative** delta, **C** = number of standard contracts eligible for the offset, **Psr** = price relation rate between the underlyings. Available **only** where "các vị thế trên một tài khoản nhà đầu tư có **từ hai tài sản cơ sở trở lên** và thuộc **một nhóm tài sản cơ sở**" — two or more *different* underlyings, in the same VSDC-established group | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 2.2** verbatim; QĐ 26 Điều 5.1.1(a) | high |
| **Group formation — Kendall-tau ≥ 0.9** | HNXDS | VSDC may establish underlying-asset groups from "kết quả tính toán mức độ tương quan (**tương quan Kendall-tau**) giữa các tài sản cơ sở với mẫu dữ liệu là giá của tài sản cơ sở trong khoảng thời gian **tối thiểu là 3 năm** liền trước thời điểm xác định. Các tài sản cơ sở trong nhóm tài sản cơ sở phải có **tương quan dương** với hệ số tương quan **không thấp hơn 0,9** và **một tài sản cơ sở chỉ thuộc một nhóm tài sản cơ sở**." So: Kendall-tau, positive, ≥ 0.9, on ≥ 3 years of underlying prices, and membership is **exclusive** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 2.1** verbatim | high |
| — the grouping is **all-pairs**, and the worked example proves it | HNXDS | VSDC's own example: VN30/VN100 = 0.95, VN30/HNX30 = 0.91, VN100/HNX30 = 0.89. HNX30 is excluded from the {VN30, VN100} group **because of the 0.89 pair**, not because of its own link to VN30. So every pair inside a group must clear the threshold — a group is a clique, not a star. And because membership is exclusive, HNX30 cannot then form a second group with VN30 either: "do VN30 đã được lựa chọn thiết lập nhóm với VN100 nên không thể sử dụng để thiết lập nhóm VN30 và HNX30" | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 2.1, worked example, verbatim | high |

The example gloss says 0.91 "đáp ứng điều kiện **lớn hơn 0,9**" (greater than 0.9 ⇒ **>**). Nothing tests exactly 0.900. **We adopt the rule text (≥)** because the gloss is describing a value that satisfies both. Immaterial unless a coefficient lands exactly on 0.9 | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 2.1 rule vs example | medium |
| — **group selection order is NOT specified** | HNXDS | The example shows that when several valid groups compete for one asset, the choice of which forms **changes the margin outcome** — and the text gives no tie-break, no maximisation objective, and no ordering. | — **which contract supplies B and S is not stated for groups of 3+** | HNXDS | `B` and `S` are "risk margin on **one** standard contract" with positive / negative delta. | **Psr — price relation rate** | HNXDS | Per **pair** of underlyings inside a group: `Psr = 1 − Max99\|rx − ry\| / (Max\|rx\| + Max\|ry\|)`, where `rx`, `ry` are the **2-business-day** price changes of underlyings X and Y, `Max99\|rx − ry\|` is the **99th percentile** of the absolute differences, and `Max\|rx\|`, `Max\|ry\|` the maxima of the absolute changes. **The group's Psr is the MINIMUM** over its pairs: "Hệ số tương quan giá của nhóm tài sản cơ sở là **giá trị nhỏ nhất** trong tập hợp các hệ số tương quan giá của từng cặp" | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 2.2(e)** verbatim | high |

Formula: **`SM = Min(SMl, SMs)`**, the smaller of the value computed on the **long** balance and on the **short** balance | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 5 khoản 2** and **Phụ lục 2 mục 3.1** verbatim | high |
| **Sm — the per-side value** | HNXDS | `SMl/s = P × S × M × SMrate`, where **P** = long or short position balance, **S** = **closing price of the underlying** on the calculation date, **M** = contract multiplier, **SMrate** = the basis margin rate | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 3.2** verbatim | high |
| — `Min(long, short)` makes Sm a **calendar-spread charge** | HNXDS | It is zero for any one-sided book — a purely long or purely short account has `Min(SMl, 0) = 0` — and bites only when the account holds **both** long and short balances in the same underlying, i.e. a calendar spread. This is the counterpart to `OA`: **spreads across underlyings get a credit, spreads across expiries get a charge** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 3.1–3.2 | high |
| **SMrate — the basis margin rate** | HNXDS | Determined per **pair of futures contracts on the same underlying** from underlying closing prices and DSP/FSP movements. The applied `SMrate` is the **90th percentile** ("**phân vị thứ 90**") of that set across the expiry pairs of the underlying over a given period | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 3.3** verbatim | high |
| — VSDC's own worked example fixes the window at **252 trading days** | HNXDS | "HĐTL chỉ số VN30 có 4 tháng đáo hạn VN302403, VN302404, VN302406, VN302409 thì cần xác định tỷ lệ ký quỹ song hành cho từng cặp (VN302403, VN302404); (VN302403, VN302406), (VN302403, VN302409) cho mỗi ngày trong khoảng thời gian **252 ngày giao dịch** và chọn giá trị **phân vị thứ 90**". Note the pairs are all **spot-month vs each far month** — 3 pairs for 4 expiries, not all 6 combinations. **252 comes from the example, not the rule**, which says only "một khoảng thời gian nhất định" | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 3.3 example verbatim | high (the example) / medium (that 252 is the rule) |
| — extraction noise in this formula, recorded so it is not "corrected" | HNXDS | The source prints the left-hand side as `SPRt` in the formula but as **`δSPRt`** in the where-list, and prints **`Mrt2`** for `rt2` (a stray `M`). Both are artifacts; the arithmetic is unambiguous. **No `δ` belongs in the formula** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 3.3, direct reading | high |
| **Dm — ký quỹ chuyển giao (delivery margin), GB futures only** | HNXDS | Two components. **(1) Mark-to-market on the final settlement price**: `MTM = (Aq × (FSP − Cp) × m) + (Tq × (Cp − FSP) × m)`. **(2) Delivery risk margin**: `DRM = (Aq × (Cp − Lp) × m) + (Tq × (Hp − Cp) × m)`. Where **Aq** = số lượng HĐTL **mua** trái phiếu, **Tq** = số lượng HĐTL **chuyển giao** trái phiếu, **FSP** = final settlement price, **Cp** = closing price of the underlying on the **last trading day**, **Lp**/**Hp** = lowest/highest price under the scenario grid, **m** = GB futures multiplier | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 4.1(a)(b)** verbatim | high |
| — `Dm = MTM + DRM` is **inferred**, not written | HNXDS | Mục 4.1 says only "Ký quỹ chuyển giao **gồm hai giá trị thành phần** như sau" — delivery margin *comprises two component values*. It never writes the combining operator. Summation is the natural reading (they cover disjoint risks: settlement-price divergence, then price drift to delivery) but **the text does not say it**, and both components can be negative for a given side | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 4.1 | medium |
| — Lp/Hp come from the **same 21-scenario grid**, and the underlying is the **CTD** | HNXDS | Mục 4.3: `Hp` and `Lp` are "xác định dựa trên kịch bản biến động giá theo hướng dẫn tại **mục 1.2 và 1.3** Phụ lục này" — the same grid used for Rm, so **the scenario reconstruction gap above propagates into Dm**. Mục 4.2: the underlying used for `Rm`, `Sm` **and** `Dm` on GB futures is the **cheapest-to-deliver bond** ("trái phiếu **rẻ nhất để chuyển giao (CTD)**") in the deliverable list of the **spot-month** contract, per **Phụ lục 8** — which we do **not** have | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | Phụ lục 2 mục 4.2–4.3 verbatim | high (the delegation) / **UNVERIFIED** (the CTD method itself, Phụ lục 8 unobtained) |
| **MM — ký quỹ tối thiểu (minimum margin)** | HNXDS | `MM = P × MF`, where **P** = "Số dư vị thế HĐTL **cuối ngày**" (end-of-day futures position balance) and **MF** = the minimum margin value determined **for one contract expiry month**, with the parenthetical "(giá trị này **không được xác định tại ngày giao dịch cuối cùng**)" — MF is not struck on the last trading day. Purpose, from Điều 5.4: to cover costs if a clearing member defaults — "**giá dịch vụ giao dịch đóng vị thế bắt buộc, chi phí hành chính và chi phí liên quan khác**" | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 5.1** and **QĐ 26 Điều 5 khoản 4** verbatim | high |
| **MF — the per-expiry minimum margin, from the bid-ask spread** | HNXDS | `Minimum margin per contract = R × M × St`, where **M** = multiplier, **St** = underlying price on the calculation date, and **R** = the **arithmetic mean** (for liquid products) or **median** (for illiquid products) of the per-trade quantity `(Giá chào bán thấp nhất − Giá chào mua cao nhất) / (Giá chào bán thấp nhất + Giá chào mua cao nhất)` — lowest ask minus highest bid over lowest ask plus highest bid — evaluated "**theo từng giao dịch** trong khoảng thời gian **tối thiểu 252 ngày giao dịch** liền trước ngày tính toán". So MM is a **half-relative-spread liquidity charge**, in the shape of an estimated cost of forcibly closing the position | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **Phụ lục 2 mục 5.2** verbatim | high |
| — three things mục 5 does not say | HNXDS | (1) **Is `P` net or gross, signed or absolute?** "Số dư vị thế" is unqualified; a signed net balance would make MM vanish on a flat spread, an absolute one would not. (2) **What makes a product "có thanh khoản"?** The mean/median switch has no liquidity test attached. (3) At a matched trade the lowest ask is at or below the highest bid, so the quoted ratio is **≤ 0** on its face; the text neither addresses the sign nor says an absolute value is taken. **All three must be modelling choices, declared as such** | — | — | — | Gaps in Phụ lục 2 mục 5.1–5.2 | **UNVERIFIED** |
| **Điều 13 — the margin monitoring test is BINARY** | HNXDS | **No percentage appears anywhere in Điều 13.** The violation predicate is a strict inequality on values: "Trường hợp **giá trị tài sản ký quỹ** trên tài khoản nhà đầu tư **nhỏ hơn giá trị ký quỹ yêu cầu**, thành viên bù trừ có trách nhiệm nộp bổ sung…" — margin asset value **<** required margin value. The cure predicate is its complement: an account is restored when its assets are "**bằng hoặc lớn hơn** mức ký quỹ yêu cầu" (**≥**). There is no warning band, no amber state, and no ratio | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 13 khoản 1 and khoản 2(c)** verbatim | high |
| **Điều 13 — the three intraday checkpoints** | HNXDS | **09h30** — against the **previous working day's** requirement, VSDC identifies **newly** violating accounts ("kiểm tra các tài khoản vi phạm mới … **không bao gồm các tài khoản đang vi phạm**") and (i) asks HNX to suspend trading on them, (ii) tells the clearing member to open **no new positions** on them, "**ngoại trừ giao dịch đối ứng để đóng vị thế**". **14h00** — VSDC checks assets on **all** violating accounts and asks HNX to **restore** any that have met the requirement. **16h30 at the latest** — VSDC strikes the day's MR per account, and restores any suspended account whose assets are now ≥ MR | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 13 khoản 2** points (a), (b), (c) verbatim | high |
| — the checkpoint asymmetry is implementable and matters | HNXDS | 09h30 can only **add** violations (it excludes accounts already in breach); 14h00 can only **remove** them (it looks at accounts already in breach). So an account that falls into breach **after 09h30** is not caught until the next day's 09h30 — **there is no midday detection sweep**, only a midday release sweep. Combined with MR being struck once after the close, the post-KRX regime **cannot issue an intraday margin call at all** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | QĐ 26 Điều 13.2(a) vs 13.2(b) | high |
| **Điều 13 — cure deadline and escalation** | HNXDS | Top-up is due "**trước 09h30 ngày giao dịch liền kề tiếp theo**" (before 09:30 the next trading day). The permitted remedies are exactly two: "a. **Nộp bổ sung tài sản ký quỹ**; b. **Thực hiện giao dịch đối ứng** để làm giảm vị thế trên tài khoản vi phạm." If unremedied "**Trong thời hạn 03 ngày làm việc** kể từ ngày VSDC gửi điện thông báo vi phạm", **VSDC directs a DIFFERENT clearing member to close the positions** — VSDC notifies the substitute member of the positions to be closed, the substitute places the offsetting trades, and the resulting positions are transferred back to the violating member for netting. The substitution is "trên cơ sở **thỏa thuận** giữa VSDC và thành viên bù trừ thực hiện thay" — by agreement, not compulsion | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 13 khoản 1 and khoản 3** verbatim | high |
| **Điều 9 — collateral haircuts (tỷ lệ chiết khấu)** | HNXDS | **a. 5%** — "đối với **trái phiếu Chính phủ, trái phiếu được Chính phủ bảo lãnh**". **b. 30%** — "đối với chứng khoán nằm trong danh mục chứng khoán cấu thành chỉ số **VN30, HNX30**". **c. 40%** — "đối với **các chứng khoán còn lại**". These are in the **body** of the rulebook, not an appendix | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 9 khoản 1** verbatim | high |
| **Điều 9 — VSDC may change them on one working day's notice** | HNXDS | "VSDC **có thể thay đổi** tỷ lệ chiết khấu … căn cứ vào **mức thanh khoản và rủi ro** của từng loại chứng khoán. Trong trường hợp thay đổi …, VSDC sẽ gửi văn bản thông báo cho thành viên bù trừ **chậm nhất 01 ngày làm việc trước ngày hiệu lực áp dụng**." **So 5/30/40 are defaults with a one-day-notice override, not fixed constants** — the correct data structure is a dated table, even though only one row is known | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 9 khoản 2** verbatim | high |
| — tension between Điều 9.2 and Điều 6.3 on how often haircuts can move | HNXDS | Điều 6.3 says the eligible-collateral list "**bao gồm cả tỷ lệ chiết khấu**" is published **every 6 months** and "**không thay đổi cho đến kỳ công bố tiếp theo**" — unchanged until the next publication. Điều 9.2 permits a change on **1 working day's** notice. Read together, the 6-monthly cycle is the ordinary channel and Điều 9.2 is the exception; but the texts are not reconciled in the instrument | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | QĐ 26 Điều 6.3 vs Điều 9.2 | medium |

For government and government-guaranteed bonds: "**giá xác định dựa trên đường cong lợi suất trái phiếu Chính phủ của SGDCK Hà Nội**" — priced off HNX's government bond yield curve, not a traded price. **Appendices of QĐ 26 we do NOT have.** Phụ lục 2 is in hand. **Phụ lục 3** (the
liquidity method that builds the eligible-collateral list), **Phụ lục 6** (the DSP
determination method — see §6.2, where the DSP is still only `medium`), **Phụ lục 7**
(settlement procedures) and **Phụ lục 8** (the cheapest-to-deliver method, and the
electronic-document catalogue) are all delegated to by articles read above and none was
obtained. Phụ lục 6 and Phụ lục 8 are the two that carry rule values.

| Position limits — government bond futures | HNXDS | **GB05**: professional 10,000 / institution 5,000 / **individual not permitted**. **GB10**: professional-institution 10,000 / professional-individual 3,000 / institution 5,000 / **individual not permitted** | 2019-07-04 / 2021-06-28 | current | depository | HNX published GB futures contract specifications | high |

A member may also require an investor to close positions on failure to maintain margin, on breach of the member's or statutory trading rules, or on a competent authority's forced close-out order | 2021-08-27 | current | statutory | Thông tư 58/2021/TT-BTC (2021-07-12, effective 2021-08-27) **Art. 5(3) — article number from a secondary rendering, unconfirmed**; MBS guide §19 | medium |

Do not hard-code it.** Note that QĐ 26 gives **03** working days, so either the period changed at the cutover or the 5-day summary was wrong; **the summary remains the only evidence for the pre-KRX interval and QĐ 61 Art. 28 has never been read** | 2020-01-01 | 2025-05-04 | — | LuatVietnam summary of QĐ 61 | low |

No suspension, no trading restriction | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 29 khoản 2** verbatim | high |
| **Điều 29 — action at level 3, and the invalid-trade trap** | HNXDS | (a) VSDC asks HNX to **suspend trading** on the breaching account. (b) VSDC tells the clearing member to reduce the position by offsetting trades **within 03 working days** — and then the sharp clause: "**Các giao dịch đối ứng này sẽ bị coi là không hợp lệ nếu sau khi khớp lệnh tài khoản vi phạm không giảm xuống dưới ngưỡng cảnh báo mức độ 3**" — an offsetting trade that fails to bring the account **below** level 3 is **invalid**, and is routed to the error-holding account for correction under Điều 18. **A partial reduction that leaves the account at or above 100% is void, not partial credit** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 29 khoản 3–4** verbatim | high |
| **Điều 29 — escalation and restoration** | HNXDS | Uncured after **03 working days** from VSDC's notice, VSDC "sẽ yêu cầu **thành viên bù trừ khác** thực hiện đóng vị thế đối với tài khoản vi phạm" — the same substitute-member machinery as Điều 13.3, by agreement with the substitute, with the resulting positions transferred back for netting. Trading is restored once the account is **below** level 3. Throughout, "thành viên bù trừ **vẫn phải đảm bảo nghĩa vụ ký quỹ, thanh toán** cho số vị thế … kể cả đó là tài khoản của nhà đầu tư" — the margin and settlement obligations do not pause during the breach | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 29 khoản 5–7** verbatim | high |
| — **the level-3 threshold is `≥ 100%`, and the limit is therefore breachable** | HNXDS | Level 3 fires when the count "**đạt ngưỡng** 100%" — *reaches* 100% — so an account sitting exactly at its limit is already at level 3 and suspended. The ladder is a **post-trade detection** mechanism operating during the session, not a pre-trade admission check: QĐ 26 contains no rule barring the exchange from *matching* an order that would exceed the limit. **A simulator that rejects the limit-breaching order at entry is modelling something QĐ 26 does not describe** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | QĐ 26 Điều 29.1(c), 29.3; absence of any pre-trade clause | high (the text) / medium (the negative) |
| — how the contract count is taken (Điều 27.2), which the ladder measures | HNXDS | **Ordinary account**: the total position count across futures with the **same underlying and same multiplier but different expiry months**, with opposite positions in the **same** expiry netted out and removed first. **Omnibus (tài khoản giao dịch tổng)**: same, except each expiry month contributes **the larger of its long or short count**, not the net. **So a calendar spread consumes limit on an ordinary account, and an omnibus account cannot net at all** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 27 khoản 2** points (a), (b) verbatim | high |
| — the limit-setting mechanism is now primary-sourced (the **values** still are not) | HNXDS | Điều 27.1: VSDC applies position limits per investor trading account **by account type** (cá nhân / tổ chức / chứng khoán chuyên nghiệp) **and by futures type**; VSDC sets them **with VNX** and **with SSC approval**; published on VSDC's website "**tối thiểu 02 ngày làm việc trước khi áp dụng**". Điều 28 adds that the professional-investor limit is set **on the clearing member's written request** per qualifying client, within **01 working day**. **This confirms the three-tier structure and the delegation — it does not publish the 5,000 / 10,000 / 20,000 numbers, which remain `low` above** | 2025-05-05 | current | depository | **QĐ 26 Điều 27 khoản 1 and Điều 28** verbatim | high (mechanism) / low (values, unchanged) |
| Whether limits changed inside the window | HNXDS | **UNVERIFIED.** The 5,000/10,000/20,000 triple appears in the 2017 launch announcement and in 2025/2026 broker documentation, with no VSD/VSDC notice re-issuing or revising them in between — and the current template stopped printing them | — | — | — | — | UNVERIFIED |

| VSDC **QĐ 12/QĐ-HĐTV** (2023-08-10) | replaces QĐ 61 | 2023-08-10 | medium |
| VSDC **QĐ 26/QĐ-HĐTV** (2025-04-16) | replaces QĐ 12; derivatives clearing and settlement — **READ IN FULL 2026-08-26**, together with **Phụ lục 2** (margin calculation methods). Điều 2 verbatim: effective "**kể từ ngày Hệ thống công nghệ thông tin của gói thầu 'Thiết kế, giải pháp, cung cấp lắp đặt và chuyển giao hệ thống công nghệ thông tin - Sở Giao dịch chứng khoán thành phố Hồ Chí Minh' chính thức vận hành**" and replacing "**Quyết định số 12/QĐ-HĐTV ngày 10 tháng 8 năm 2023**". Issued under Nghị quyết 84/2025/NQ-HĐTV, one of **07 quy chế** adopted together "để chuẩn bị triển khai hệ thống công nghệ thông tin **KRX**" — the rulebook names KRX explicitly. Signed **Nguyễn Sơn**, Chủ tịch HĐTV; SSC approval Công văn 1058/UBCK-PTTT of 2025-04-15. 32 articles in 7 chapters. **Phụ lục 1, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9 and the Mẫu forms were NOT obtained** | on KRX go-live — **mechanism verbatim, calendar date (2025-05-05) still inferred**, exactly as for VNX QĐ 21 | **high** (instrument, articles and Phụ lục 2) / medium (effective calendar date) |
| HNX **QĐ 508/QĐ-SGDHN** (2025-04-29) | guidance on Điều 6 and Điều 31 of the futures rulebook; almost certainly the document publishing the post-KRX derivatives session table | 2025-05-05 (assumed) | **located but never read** |
| Contract templates (Mẫu hợp đồng tương lai) | band, tick, lot, months, order limit, position limit, reference price, DSP, FSP, margin level — **all delegated here** by QĐ 21 Điều 5 | — | **HNX's web reproduction was read; the VNX-ISSUED template with an edition date has never been obtained.** This is now the single most important missing derivatives document |
| Statutory layer | Luật Chứng khoán 54/2019/QH14 → ND 42/2015/NĐ-CP → **ND 158/2020/NĐ-CP** (2020-12-31, eff. 2021-01-01) → TT 11/2016/TT-BTC → **TT 58/2021/TT-BTC** (2021-07-12, eff. 2021-08-27) → **TT 14/2025/TT-BTC** (2025-04-04, eff. 2025-06-01, amending TT 119/2020 and TT 58/2021 — reported as replacing model agreements only, **not** changing margin ratios, position limits or settlement mechanics) | | medium (dates) / low (TT 14/2025 content characterisation, one secondary summary) |
| — a note on stale citations | VSDC's own margin page still cites "Thông tư 11" (11/2016/TT-BTC) for the cash-ratio provision because the page was last updated 2021-01-18. The equivalent provision now sits in TT 58/2021 | | high |

## 7. Foreign ownership limits and foreign room

### 7.1 The limit itself

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FOL framework | ALL | Treaty ratio if the sector is covered; else sector law; else **49%** if the line is conditional for foreign investors with no ratio prescribed; else unrestricted, unless the company charter provides otherwise | 2015-09-01 | 2020-12-31 | statutory | ND 60/2015/NĐ-CP, new Điều 2a (amending ND 58/2012/NĐ-CP). **The only cited URL returns HTTP 404**, so the 49% default and the charter carve-out currently rest on nothing citable. **This is the sole authority for the first 12 months of the window.** Signing date reported as both 2015-05-26 and 2015-06-26; effective 2015-09-01 is agreed | low |
| FOL framework | ALL | Điều 139(1): (a) treaty sector → treaty ratio; (b) sector law prescribes → that law; (c) sector on the foreign market-access restriction list → the listed ratio, and where the conditional line prescribes **no** charter-capital ratio the maximum is **50%**; (d) not in (a)–(c) → **unrestricted**; (đ) multi-sector company → the **LOWEST** prescribed ratio; (e) the company may adopt a lower ratio via AGM resolution written into the charter | 2021-01-01 | current | statutory | ND 155/2020/NĐ-CP (signed 2020-12-31, effective 2021-01-01) Điều 139 khoản 1 (a)–(e), **verbatim Vietnamese obtained** | high |
| — **the default rose 49% → 50% on 2021-01-01** | | A FOL constant valid for 2020 is wrong for 2021+, and vice versa | | | | | high |
| Company self-imposed lower FOL **abolished** | ALL | Điều 139(1)(e) is **repealed**: a public company's AGM/charter may no longer fix a maximum below the statutory/treaty level. Companies that had already notified a lower ratio may **keep it or raise it**, never lower it. Companies that never notified must complete notification within 12 months | 2025-09-11 | current | statutory | ND 245/2025/NĐ-CP (signed and effective **2025-09-11**, confirmed from the 2026 VNX Quy chế's own citation), amending ND 155/2020. **This is the only structural room-relief measure that actually took effect inside the window.** It does not change any published cap by itself | high |
| Instruments **exempt** from FOL | ALL | Foreign investment is unrestricted in government bonds, government-guaranteed bonds, municipal bonds, corporate bonds, fund certificates, shares of securities investment companies, **non-voting shares** of public companies, **derivative securities** and depository receipts, save where other law provides otherwise. **Consequence: never evaluate a room check for bonds, ETFs, covered warrants or futures** | 2021-01-01 | current | statutory | ND 155/2020 Điều 139 (khoản number **not pinned** — substance confirmed by a secondary source paraphrasing the decree; independently we established Điều 139 has at least 5 khoản) | medium |
| Who counts as a foreign investor | ALL | An economic organisation in which foreign investors hold **more than 50%** of charter capital is subject to the same conditions and procedures as a foreign investor | 2021-01-01 | current | statutory | **ND 155/2020 khoản 5 Điều 139** — confirmed twice from primary HOSE documents: QĐ 352 Điều 23(3) ("… phải tuân thủ quy định tại khoản 5 Điều 139 Nghị định 155/2020/NĐ-CP") and HOSE's 2025 regulation, whose English version reads "Clause 5, Article 139". **An earlier pass attributed this to Điều 141 on a secondary summary** | high |
| — trading-code duty on crossing the threshold | ALL | A 3-working-day trading-code registration / cancellation duty when an entity crosses 50% in either direction. Attributed to Điều 141; **unverified** | 2021-01-01 | current | statutory | secondary summary | low |
| Sector cap — credit institutions | ALL | Individual foreign investor ≤5%; foreign organisation ≤15%; foreign strategic investor ≤20%; one foreign investor plus related persons ≤20%; **total foreign holding ≤30%** of a commercial bank's charter capital. Higher only by Prime Minister decision in restructuring cases | 2014-02-20 | 2025-05-18 | statutory | ND 01/2014/NĐ-CP (2014-01-03) Điều 7. **This 30% aggregate is the binding cap for every listed bank across 2020-01-01 → 2025-05-18 and is the single most common reason a Vietnamese large-cap shows zero room.** Sources differ on the exact wording of the special >30%…49% case (state-controlled banks vs PM discretion for weak-bank restructuring) | medium |
| Sector cap — credit institutions (amended) | ALL | Aggregate remains **≤30%**, except a bank that has received a **compulsory transfer** of a weak credit institution, where aggregate may exceed 30% but must not exceed **49%** during the transfer plan period; banks in which the State holds over 50% are **excluded** from that exception | 2025-05-19 | current | statutory | ND 69/2025/NĐ-CP (signed 2025-03-18, effective 2025-05-19) amending ND 01/2014. **A genuine mid-window cap change: a small set of listed banks moved from 30% to 49%. The list of qualifying banks was not verified — do not hard-code tickers** | medium |
| Sector cap — non-bank credit institutions | ALL | Aggregate ≤50%. **UNRESOLVED whether this is new in 2025 or pre-existing**: one summary describes the *original* ND 01/2014 framework as "30% for commercial banks and 50% for non-bank credit institutions", which would make it pre-existing. **Do not present the 50% as a 2025 change** | — | — | statutory | pending ND 01/2014 Điều 7 primary text | low |
| Sector cap — air carriers | ALL | Foreign ownership of a Vietnamese air transport enterprise ≤**34%** (raised from 30%) | 2020-01-01 | current | statutory | ND 89/2019/NĐ-CP (2019-11-15) amending ND 92/2016/NĐ-CP, effective 2020-01-01. **Effective exactly at the start of the window, so 30% is never correct inside 2020–2026.** A proposal to raise it to 49% was still only a proposal as of mid-2026 | medium |
| Market-access restriction list | ALL | The list of business lines restricted for foreign market access, with their foreign-ownership conditions, is the appendix to the Investment Law implementing decree, which Điều 139(1)(c) refers to | 2021-03-26 | current | statutory | ND 31/2021/NĐ-CP, **Phụ lục I** — the cited source states this outright ("Phụ lục I đính kèm Nghị định Số: 31/2021/NĐ-CP"), so it is a sourced attribution, not an inference. **Individual line-item percentages (telecoms, logistics, advertising, …) were NOT verified and must not be hard-coded** | medium |
| — dating gap nobody flagged | ALL | ND 155/2020's 50% default runs from 2021-01-01 but the operative list only exists from 2021-03-26, leaving **2021-01-01 → 2021-03-25 (84 days) with a statutory cross-reference to a list that did not yet exist.** Indeterminate in that sub-interval | 2021-01-01 | 2021-03-25 | — | — | UNVERIFIED |
| Sector cap — treaty-governed sectors | ALL | Where a binding treaty prescribes a ratio, that ratio governs and overrides the domestic default. **Mechanism only — no specific treaty percentage was verified.** The widely repeated WTO telecom figures (49% / 65%) could not be sourced primarily. **Do not hard-code them** | 2021-01-01 | current | statutory | ND 155/2020 Điều 139(1)(a) | low |
| Sector cap — securities firms and fund managers | ALL | Foreign investors may own up to **100%**, subject to qualification conditions; unqualified foreign organisations limited to **49%** | 2021-01-01 | current | statutory | One secondary source cites "Điều 77 Luật Chứng khoán 2019"; **neither the article number nor the 49% fallback was confirmed.** Should not appear as a rule row without that | low |
| How the FOL reaches the exchange | ALL | The public company determines its own business lines and maximum FOL and files a notification dossier with the SSC (form Mẫu 38 plus corporate documents). Within **7 working days** of a complete dossier the SSC issues a written acknowledgement and simultaneously forwards it to VSDC; **VSDC updates the maximum FOL in its systems within 2 working days.** Notification must be completed within 7 working days of public-company registration confirmation, and before any listing / trading-registration / offering filing | 2021-01-01 | current | statutory | ND 155/2020 Điều 142 khoản 1–3. **This is the answer to "how is the cap published": it is not exchange-determined. The cap is company-declared, SSC-acknowledged, VSDC-recorded — so a cap change has a multi-day administrative lag** | medium |
| FOL change notification deadline | ALL | Within **30 days** of any of three triggers: a change in business lines affecting the ratio, a change in the law prescribing the ratio, or a charter amendment concerning the ratio | 2021-01-01 | current | statutory | ND 155/2020 Điều 141 khoản 2. **This explains why the effective date of a statutory sector-cap change (e.g. banks on 2025-05-19) and the date the exchange actually widens a symbol's room can differ by weeks** | medium |
| Foreign trading code gates order entry | ALL | A foreign investor (and a foreign DR issuer) must be granted a **securities trading code** before trading; **one code per investor**. Registration is filed through a depository member into VSDC's online system; VSDC grants the code within **1 working day**. Dossier: Mẫu 41, authorisation to the depository member, investor identification list Mẫu 42 | 2021-01-01 | current | statutory | ND 155/2020 Điều 145–146; TT 51/2021/TT-BTC (signed 2021-06-30, effective 2021-08-16, confirmed from the gazette). **For 2020 the equivalent regime sat in ND 58/2012 and its guiding circular — not verified, so 2020 is uncovered** | medium |
| Trading-code issuance simplified | ALL | The requirement for the depository member to submit paper documents after an online-granted code is removed; VSDC sends the electronic confirmation within one working day and the investor may trade immediately on it. Registration latency drops to effectively one business day | 2025-09-11 | current | statutory | ND 245/2025/NĐ-CP | medium |
| **NVDR / room-exhaustion escape mechanism** | ALL | **No non-voting depository receipt mechanism took effect at any point in the window.** Luật Chứng khoán 54/2019/QH14 recognises depository certificates as a security type, but **no implementing framework for NVDR issuance or trading exists and no NVDR has been issued.** The simulator must **not** model an NVDR path. The only relief that actually landed in the window is ND 245/2025's abolition of self-imposed sub-statutory caps; the residual escape hatch is off-book **foreign-to-foreign put-through at room = 0** | 2020-01-01 | current | statutory | Law 54/2019 (definition only); Vietnamese legal commentary confirming no implementing framework | medium |

### 7.2 Room mechanics

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Instruments metered against room | HSX, HNX | **Shares (cổ phiếu) and closed-end fund certificates (chứng chỉ quỹ đóng) only.** No other instrument class is metered in the trading rulebook | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 23; HOSE's 2025 regulation; VNX QĐ 17 Điều 27 / QĐ 22/2025 Điều 26. Open-ended fund and ETF certificates are absent, consistent with the statutory exemption | high |
| Instruments metered against room | UPCOM | **Shares only.** The post-KRX UPCoM regulation repeats the HOSE clauses with "cổ phiếu" throughout and **never mentions chứng chỉ quỹ đóng**. Consistent with UPCoM not hosting closed-end funds, but the wording differs and the per-venue config should reflect it | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | ASEANSC UPCoM sheet 05/2025 | medium |
| Room decrement — order matching | HSX | Foreign buy volume of shares / closed-end fund certificates is deducted from remaining room **immediately after the buy order is EXECUTED (matched)**, not at order entry | 2021-06-30 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | **QĐ 352/QĐ-SGDHCM (2021-06-30) Điều 23(1)(a) verbatim** ("Khối lượng mua cổ phiếu, chứng chỉ quỹ đóng của nhà đầu tư nước ngoài **được trừ** vào khối lượng còn được phép mua **ngay sau khi lệnh mua được thực hiện**"); and **VNX QĐ 17/QĐ-HĐTV (2022-03-31) Điều 27(2)(a) verbatim** ("ngay sau khi lệnh mua **được thực hiện**") | high |
| — 2020-01-01 → 2021-06-29 | HSX | **Assumed identical.** Every restatement of the pre-KRX rule is the same, but the governing predecessor of QĐ 352 was never read | 2020-01-01 | 2021-06-29 | exchange | continuity | low |
| Room decrement — order matching | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Foreign buy volume is deducted **immediately after the buy order is ENTERED into the system**, before any matching | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | **VNX QĐ 22/2025 Điều 26 verbatim** ("tính vào ngay khi lệnh nhập"); QĐ 22/2026 Điều 28 ("ngay sau khi lệnh mua được nhập vào hệ thống"); HOSE's own guidance PDF; ASEANSC UPCoM sheet reproduces it word-for-word with "hệ thống giao dịch UPCOM" substituted | high |
| Order exceeding available room — pre-KRX | HSX | **Both behaviours, and this corrects a widely repeated error.** An order resting while room is still positive **fills up to the room and its remainder is auto-cancelled** on exhaustion. **But once room is exhausted (== 0), a further buy order is NOT ACCEPTED at entry.** QĐ 352 Điều 23(1)(a) third bullet, verbatim: "Lệnh mua hoặc một phần lệnh mua … chưa được thực hiện sẽ tự động bị hủy nếu khối lượng còn được phép mua đã hết **VÀ lệnh mua được nhập tiếp vào hệ thống giao dịch sẽ không được chấp nhận**" | 2021-06-30 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 23(1)(a). Broker restatements (SSI, FPTS, dautucophieu) quote only the first half of that sentence, which is where the "no rejection pre-KRX" story comes from | high |
| Order exceeding available room — KRX | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | A foreign buy order is **NOT ACCEPTED** if remaining room is smaller than the order quantity. No partial acceptance, no queueing for room. "Lệnh mua … sẽ không được chấp nhận nếu khối lượng còn được phép mua nhỏ hơn khối lượng đặt mua" | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | HOSE's published guidance; ASEANSC UPCoM sheet | high |
| — **the actual KRX delta** | | Not "no rejection → rejection". It is a change in the **rejection trigger**: pre-KRX reject when remaining room **== 0**; from 2025-05-05 reject whenever remaining room **< order quantity**. **That is a third distinct behaviour, not either of the two commonly described.** Operationally a foreign buyer facing thin room must now self-slice; the exchange will not fill to the room for them | | | | | high |
| Amend-down restores room | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Remaining room increases by the reduction **immediately after the amendment is entered**. No analogous rule existed pre-KRX, because unmatched quantity had never consumed room | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | HOSE guidance; ASEANSC UPCoM | high |
| Amend-up consumes room and can be rejected | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Room decreases by the increment at amendment entry; if remaining room is smaller than the increment, **the system does not accept the amendment.** Interacts with the price-XOR-quantity rule | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | HOSE guidance; ASEANSC UPCoM | high |
| Cancellation restores room | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Room increases by the cancelled quantity, whether the cancellation is by the investor **or by the trading system** (so room reverts to the matched-only figure by the close) | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | HOSE guidance | high |
| **Sell side restores room only at SETTLEMENT** | HSX | "Khối lượng bán cổ phiếu, chứng chỉ quỹ đóng của nhà đầu tư nước ngoài được cộng vào khối lượng còn được phép mua **ngay sau khi kết thúc việc thanh toán giao dịch**" | 2021-06-30 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 23(1)(a) second bullet, verbatim; QĐ 17 Điều 27(2)(a) | high |
| Sell side restores room at settlement | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Same rule assumed to continue | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | **The sell-side clause is ABSENT from HOSE's post-KRX regulation and guidance PDF (checked in Vietnamese and English).** Post-KRX it rests on Vietcap's handbook (which shows "foreign room increases after settlement of a foreign sell order" in both its Current-System and Under-KRX columns) and FPTS. **The clause's disappearance from the rulebook text is unexplained; and under the VSD daily-recomputation mechanism an intraday sell may not restore room at all before the next trading day** | medium |
| — **the load-bearing asymmetry** | | A foreign round-trip (sell then rebuy the same name) **cannot recycle its own room intraday**; room recycles two settlement days later. **This is the main reason a foreign strategy can be room-blocked despite flat net exposure** | | | | | high |
| Foreign sell orders are **not** room-constrained | ALL | Room meters **acquisition only**. No rule conditions a foreign SELL order on remaining room; a foreign investor may always sell, including at room = 0. (Negative finding: absence of a constraining clause plus an explicit settlement-time add-back) | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | Structure of the room clauses in QĐ 352 Điều 23, QĐ 17 Điều 27, QĐ 22/2025 Điều 26 | high |
| Put-through room timing — pre-KRX | HSX | For a **foreign buyer vs domestic seller** negotiated trade, remaining room decreases **immediately after the trade is EXECUTED.** Note that pre-KRX only the **sell** side could initiate a put-through | 2021-06-30 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 23(1)(b); QĐ 17 Điều 27(2)(b); Vietcap "Current System" column | high |
| Put-through room timing — pre-KRX, sell side | HSX | Where a foreign investor **SELLS to a domestic buyer**, room increases **after settlement** | 2021-06-30 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 23(1)(b), 2nd bullet, verbatim | high |
| Put-through room timing — KRX | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | **THREE branches, not four.** (1) Room decreases immediately when the **BUY-side** negotiated order is entered, for foreign buyer vs domestic seller. (2) If the foreign buyer cancels (or the domestic seller rejects), room increases immediately when the cancellation is entered. (3) Room is **unchanged** for a foreign-to-foreign negotiated trade | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | HOSE's published guidance and HOSE's trading regulation, both read in Vietnamese; ASEANSC UPCoM. **An earlier pass claimed a "full four-branch rule set obtained verbatim" including a sell-side settlement branch. That fourth branch appears nowhere in any post-KRX text — it was imported from QĐ 352. Whether it survives post-KRX is UNVERIFIED** | high |
| Foreign-to-foreign trading at room = 0 | HSX | Two foreign investors may complete a **negotiated** trade even when remaining room is zero; such a trade does not change room. "Hệ thống cho phép giao dịch thỏa thuận giữa hai nhà đầu tư nước ngoài được thực hiện trong trường hợp khối lượng còn được phép mua của nhà đầu tư nước ngoài bằng không (0)" | 2021-06-30 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 23(1)(b), final bullet, verbatim | high |
| Foreign-to-foreign trading at room = 0 | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Same permission **inferred**: the post-KRX text says only that room does not change for foreign-to-foreign, and drops the explicit room = 0 permission. **Scope is negotiated trades only** — under the at-entry rejection rule an order-matched foreign buy is screened against room before any counterparty is known, so the escape hatch is off-book only | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | post-KRX text (silence on the permission) | medium |
| Order-book display of foreign buy orders | HSX | Foreign buy orders are added to the displayed market buy volume at each price level **from the highest to the lowest price priority**; the system refreshes that display **every time a foreign buy order is entered or cancelled** | 2021-06-30 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 23 khoản 2(b) verbatim ("cập nhật thông tin chào mua … và điều chỉnh việc hiển thị mỗi khi lệnh mua của nhà đầu tư nước ngoài được nhập vào hệ thống hoặc bị hủy bỏ"). **An earlier pass asserted "no equivalent display rule was found for the pre-KRX system" — that is false** | high |
| Order-book display of foreign buy orders | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Same refresh trigger, but the displayed volume is now explicitly **capped at remaining room** ("cho đến khi hết khối lượng còn được phép mua") | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | HOSE guidance; VNX QĐ 22/2025 Điều 26 | high |
| — fill-model consequence | | **The visible depth on a near-full name is not the true resting depth.** Any book-based fill model must reproduce this | | | | | high |
| Room publication — pre-KRX | HSX | Room decrements at **match** and increments at **settlement**, but the **published display refreshes on foreign buy-order entry or cancellation**. **The displayed figure and the decremented figure moved on DIFFERENT events** — any replay of the intraday feed must respect this | 2021-06-30 | 2025-05-04 | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 23(1)(a) vs 23(2)(b) | high |
| Room publication — KRX | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | Decrement and refresh events now coincide: order entry, amendment, cancellation, settlement | 2025-05-05 | current | exchange | HOSE guidance | high |
| Total room — the initialisation mechanism | ALL | **VSDC computes and supplies to the exchange the remaining-purchasable quantity for each trading day**: "Trung tâm Lưu ký Chứng khoán Việt Nam tính toán và cung cấp cho SGDCK khối lượng chứng khoán của nhà đầu tư nước ngoài còn được phép mua trong mỗi ngày giao dịch theo các quy trình và thỏa thuận làm việc giữa hai cơ quan." The exchange then applies intraday increments | 2021-06-30 | current | exchange | QĐ 352 Điều 23 khoản 4, verbatim. **This independently corroborates the corpus's two-level structure (`quote_totalforeignroom` daily vs `quote_foreignroom` intraday). An earlier pass recorded this as "not located in a gazetted document"** | high |
| Total room — the arithmetic | ALL | `total_room = FOL% × outstanding − foreign holdings`; `remaining = total − same-day foreign net acquisition (and, from 2025-05-05, − live foreign buy-order reservations)`. **The FORMULA is unsourced**, and whether treasury / ESOP / unlisted tranches are netted is **UNVERIFIED** | 2020-01-01 | current | market practice | inference | low |
| Room checks are **per-symbol and market-wide** | ALL | Remaining room is a single per-symbol quantity shared by all foreign investors. **No per-investor reservation, allocation or queue.** Under KRX the first order entered consumes room and a later foreign order is **rejected outright** rather than queued behind it — **room becomes a race.** No tie-breaking or priority clause among competing foreign buy orders exists in any of the four primary rulebooks read | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | Negative finding from the absence of an allocation clause | medium |
| Room re-check at auction matching under KRX | ALL | **UNVERIFIED.** Only the entry-time check is documented. Whether ATO/ATC foreign orders receive any special room treatment is **also unverified** — and the simulator's ATO/ATC path for foreign orders is otherwise unspecified | — | — | — | — | UNVERIFIED |
| MP / MTL and room exhaustion | HSX | Pre-KRX: a partially matched foreign MP buy has its unmatched remainder auto-cancelled on room exhaustion. From 2025-05-05 MP is withdrawn and replaced by MTL, and the entry-time room check makes the exhaustion case unreachable for an accepted order | 2020-01-01 | current | exchange | Broker restatements; Vietcap handbook (MP → MTL) | medium |
| No foreign room on derivatives | HNXDS | Derivatives are outside the FOL regime; **no room is computed or metered for futures.** Directionally near-certain, but this is a negative finding from a document not read in full | 2020-01-01 | current | statutory | ND 155/2020 Điều 139 exemption clause; VNX QĐ 21 contains no room provision | medium |
| **Corpus hazard — `quote_foreignroom` semantics** | | The repo's documentation disagrees with itself. `README.md:359` records FOREIGN_ROOM as "**cap**, not remaining room" for the raw archive, and `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-25-exchange-fill-model.md:3921` says the same; `tests/sample_data/README.md` says "**available** foreign room". **These cannot all be right, and the 2025-05-05 semantics break depends on which.** If the archive table is the CAP, the break does not apply to it and the concern is moot; if it is remaining room, then the series means **matched trades only** before 2025-05-05 and **also live unmatched buy-order reservations** after, so any statistic pooled across that boundary mixes two different quantities. **Resolve before publishing the claim.** Note also that the spec's "minimum remaining room is 1 and no row reaches 0" is a statement about the *pre-KRX* definition only | | | | repo docs vs corpus | high (that it is unresolved) |

## 8. Fees and taxes — the dated intervals

The implementable shape is in **§12**. This section is the dated evidence behind it.

**Two health warnings.** (1) **Not one tax row below is sourced to a primary text that
anyone opened.** Two of them originally carried a URL that does not mention the instrument
cited. Rates are corroborated; the traceability chain to the named instruments is not.
(2) The fee half was **mis-dated by three years** on the single most consequential
derivatives charge — see the position-fee / clearing-fee rows.

### 8.1 Taxes

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PIT on securities transfer — individuals | ALL | **0.1% × gross transfer price of each transaction.** Base is gross sale proceeds (executed price × quantity), **not gain**: no cost basis, no loss offset, no annual reconciliation. **Sell side only**; the buy side pays no PIT. Withheld at source by the securities company at settlement | 2015-01-01 | 2026-06-30 | statutory | Luật 71/2014/QH13 (2014-11-26) removed the 20%-on-annual-gain election; Thông tư 92/2015/TT-BTC Điều 16 amending TT 111/2013/TT-BTC Điều 11.2(a)(b). **The rate is multiply corroborated (Báo Chính phủ 2026-03-27, an earlier 2018 piece, every broker schedule). The named instruments appear in none of the sources actually opened** | high (value) / medium (citation) |
| PIT on securities transfer — individuals | ALL | Unchanged for listed/registered securities: **0.1%** of transfer price per transaction, withheld at source | 2026-07-01 | current | statutory | Luật Thuế TNCN 109/2025/QH15 (passed 2025-12-10, effective 2026-07-01); ND 253/2026/NĐ-CP (2026-06-30); TT 87/2026/TT-BTC (2026-06-30, replacing TT 111/2013). **The article on ordinary (non-derivative) securities was not read.** MoF publicly defended keeping 0.1% in Nov 2025 despite pressure to tax gains at 20% | medium |
| PIT — non-public company shares (regime boundary) | ALL | Shares of a **non-public** company are no longer taxed as a securities transfer at 0.1%: **20% of net gain** (sale − purchase − reasonable costs), or **2% of gross proceeds** if the purchase price/costs cannot be determined. Securities listed or registered on HOSE/HNX/UPCoM remain at 0.1% | 2026-07-01 | current | statutory | Luật 109/2025/QH15; ND 253/2026/NĐ-CP. Out of scope for this simulator but it defines the boundary of the 0.1% rule, and reverses part of TT 25/2018/TT-BTC (effective 2018-05-01) which had reclassified unlisted JSC transfers as securities transfers at 0.1% | medium |
| PIT — instrument coverage | ALL | Named instruments: shares, rights to buy shares of a public company, bonds, treasury bills, fund certificates. **ETF units and covered warrants fall in only by the residual clause "và các loại chứng khoán khác theo quy định của pháp luật về chứng khoán"** — they are not named | 2015-01-01 | 2026-06-30 | statutory | TT 111/2013 Điều 4 as amended by TT 25/2018, read with Điều 6 Luật Chứng khoán | medium |
| — covered warrant **exercise** settlement | HSX | **UNVERIFIED.** Whether the cash difference paid by the issuer at exercise is a transfer at 0.1% or investment income at 5% could not be established | — | — | — | — | UNVERIFIED |
| PIT — no loss offset | ALL | Because the 0.1% method taxes turnover, tax is due on **every** sale including sales at a loss, and there is no year-end true-up. **Entailed by the turnover base**, not a quoted provision — and the cross-market no-offset claim (underlying vs derivatives) is separately unsourced | 2015-01-01 | current | statutory | logical consequence of the base | medium |
| — the modelling consequence | | The tax is a **deterministic function of gross sale notional**, so it belongs in the fill model, not in a P&L reconciliation step | | | | | high |
| PIT — non-resident individuals | ALL | Same **0.1%** of gross transfer price per transaction, withheld at source. No treaty relief applied at withholding | 2015-01-01 | current | statutory | Attributed to TT 111/2013 Điều 20 — **never read, and Điều 20 governs chuyển nhượng VỐN, so even the article reference is unverified.** The URL originally attached was a 2017 futures-tax article that does not discuss non-residents | low |
| **Derivatives PIT — base and rate** | HNXDS | `PIT = 0.1% × [settlement price × contract multiplier × number of contracts × VSD/VSDC published INITIAL MARGIN RATIO ÷ 2]` — equivalently **0.05% × margined value**. Charged **per matched order** (both the opening and the closing leg) and at contract maturity, withheld by the securities company. **The base is a fraction of notional scaled by the initial margin ratio, not full notional** | 2017-08-10 | 2026-06-30 | statutory | **Công văn 11133/BTC-CST (2017-08-21).** Formula triply corroborated: Vietstock (Aug 2017) verbatim "giá chuyển nhượng từng lần = (Giá thanh toán HĐTL × Hệ số nhân hợp đồng × Số lượng hợp đồng × Tỷ lệ ký quỹ ban đầu)/2"; Pinetree 2024; and TT 87/2026 Điều 5.1 reproduces it | medium |
| — **the traceability caveat the paper must carry** | HNXDS | For 2017–2026 this rule rested on a **ministry LETTER (công văn)**, not a circular or decree. A công văn is administrative guidance of lower normative rank and **is not gazetted as a legal normative document.** The letter's own text was never opened. Note also that collection began **2017-08-10** (market launch), 11 days *before* the letter — record the retroactivity explicitly rather than silently back-dating | | | | | high |
| Derivatives PIT — codified | HNXDS | Same rate and formula, now in a circular. Per-transfer price = settlement price at the moment taxable income is determined × multiplier × contracts × **VSDC-published initial margin ratio ÷ 2**. Taxable income is determined when the order is matched, or at contract maturity | 2026-07-01 | current | statutory | **Thông tư 87/2026/TT-BTC (2026-06-30) Điều 5 khoản 1**, implementing Luật 109/2025 and ND 253/2026; replaces TT 111/2013. First explicit derivatives-tax guidance at circular level, closing the gap open since Aug 2017. **Arithmetic identical to the 2017 công văn, so for simulation purposes 2017-08-10 → today is ONE continuous interval; only the legal rank of the source changes on 2026-07-01** | high |
| — **the coupling to `margin.py`** | HNXDS | The derivatives tax base is **linear in the VSD initial margin ratio.** One dated VSD series must feed both `margin.py` and the tax model, or the paper carries two mutually inconsistent margin ratios | | | | | high |
| Derivatives tax — institutional and foreign | HNXDS | Resident individuals and **foreign organisations**: 0.1% of the per-transfer price (same formula) | 2017-08-10 | current | statutory | Công văn 11133/BTC-CST; Vietstock Aug 2017 | medium |
| — domestic organisations | HNXDS | Include realised derivatives P&L in taxable profit and pay **CIT on net income (20% standard rate since 2016)**; **not** subject to the 0.1% turnover method. **The cited source explicitly does not address domestic organisations — this leg is unsupported** | 2017-08-10 | current | statutory | — | low |
| — the modelling consequence | | Account type must select between a **per-fill turnover tax** and a **no-per-fill / annual-CIT** model. Applying the retail 0.1% to an institutional account overstates costs | | | | | high |
| CIT — foreign organisations without a PE | ALL | Deemed CIT of **0.1% of total gross sale proceeds**, withheld at source, mirroring the individual rate. Domestic organisations instead pay **20% CIT on net gain**, declared not withheld | 2014-10-01 (unverified) | current | statutory | TT 103/2014/TT-BTC Điều 13; TT 78/2014/TT-BTC. **Neither read. The URL originally attached was a 2017 futures article mentioning neither. Effective date unverified — TT 103/2014 is dated 2014-08-06. Exclude from the traceable rulebook** | low |
| Cash dividend tax | ALL | **5%** of the amount paid, as income from investment capital, withheld by the paying organisation at payment. Separate from and **additional to** the 0.1% transfer tax | 2009-01-01 | 2026-06-30 | statutory | Luật Thuế TNCN 04/2007/QH12; TT 111/2013 Điều 10. **Neither read; the URL originally attached is a 2018 article about the 0.1% transfer tax that does not mention dividends, the 5% rate, or Điều 10 at all.** Almost certainly correct as background law but currently **uncited** | low |
| — modelling note | | If the simulator models dividend cash flows, the 5% must be netted off the gross dividend before crediting cash | | | | | high |
| Stock dividend / bonus share tax | ALL | Shares received are **not taxed on receipt.** Tax crystallises when they are **sold**, and **two** taxes apply to the same sale: **5%** investment-capital tax on the **par value** recorded at issue (or on the market price at sale if lower) **PLUS 0.1%** transfer tax on the actual sale proceeds. From ND 126/2020/NĐ-CP the securities company where the shares are held is the withholding agent for the 5% component | 2020-12-05 | 2026-06-30 | statutory | ND 126/2020/NĐ-CP (2020-10-19). **Article number, the exact withholding-shift date and the double-charge are all from memory; the attached URL supports none of it. Do NOT state an article number that was not read.** This is the most-litigated retail tax mechanic in Vietnam and, if the simulator models stock dividends, the biggest substantive hole | low |
| Fund and green-bond reliefs | ALL | (a) **50% PIT reduction** on profits distributed to individual investors from **securities investment funds**, for a period the Government sets; (b) **full exemption** for transfers of **open-end** fund certificates held **2 years or more** from purchase; (c) exemption for income from **green bonds** and from the **first transfer** of a green bond | 2026-07-01 | current | statutory | Luật 109/2025/QH15, all three confirmed verbatim ("giảm 50% thuế TNCN đối với lợi tức của nhà đầu tư cá nhân được chia từ quỹ đầu tư chứng khoán"; "từ 02 năm trở lên"; "miễn thuế đối với tiền lãi trái phiếu xanh; thu nhập từ chuyển nhượng lần đầu trái phiếu xanh"). **The "real-estate investment funds" extension of the 50% relief is NOT corroborated — trim it** | high |
| — a first for the tax code | | The 2-year holding exemption is the **first holding-period-dependent tax** in the Vietnamese equity code, and the first that makes the tax **path-dependent** rather than a pure per-fill function | | | | | high |
| VAT on securities service prices | ALL | The prices in the schedules are **NOT subject to VAT**: "Giá dịch vụ trong lĩnh vực chứng khoán quy định tại Thông tư này không chịu thuế giá trị gia tăng" — **identical wording verified in BOTH TT 128/2018 Điều 3.2 and TT 101/2021 Điều 3.2**, which closes the reported conflict over TT 128/2018's wording | 2019-02-15 | 2025-04-28 | statutory | as quoted | high |
| VAT on securities service prices | ALL | Published levels are **VAT-exclusive "(nếu có)"** — "chưa bao gồm thuế giá trị gia tăng (nếu có)", verified verbatim. A genuinely weaker position | 2025-04-29 | current | statutory | Quyết định 1541/QĐ-BTC | high |
| — **UNRESOLVED CONFLICT** | | Several broker schedules bill VSDC **derivatives** prices grossed up by exactly 10%: 2,805 = 2,550 × 1.1; 0.00264% = 0.0024% × 1.1; 110,000 and 1,760,000 = the floor/cap × 1.1; and Pinetree's 5,500đ per margin transfer is 5,000 × 1.1. **Brokers were applying 10% VAT during the period the circular declared the prices exempt.** Do not assert a VAT treatment without reading TT 219/2013/TT-BTC Điều 4.8 and the 2024 VAT Law | 2019-02-15 | current | — | broker schedules vs the circulars | low |

| Exchange trading service price — current | ALL | Identical numeric schedule, re-issued under the new price-setting mechanism | 2025-04-29 | current | statutory | **Quyết định 1541/QĐ-BTC (2025-04-29, effective on signing)**, issued under TT 83/2024/TT-BTC. **No numeric change to any trading-service line.** What changed is the *instrument*: a Minister's Decision, revisable without a circular | high |
| — **beware: QĐ 1541 is a RE-ISSUE** | | It dates a **republication**, not a rule's origin. Every row whose only source is QĐ 1541 has a suspect `effective_from`. Check TT 101/2021 and TT 127/2018 first | | | | | high |
| Exchange fee base is **two-sided** | ALL | `fee = rate × total transaction value of the member`, where total = **value of securities BOUGHT + value of securities SOLD.** Both legs of a trade are charged, once to each side's member. **No minimum or maximum per trade or per order, and no per-order or per-message component** — so order-spamming carries no exchange cost | 2019-02-15 | current | statutory | TT 83/2024/TT-BTC **Phụ lục II Mục A điểm 4** verbatim ("Tổng giá trị giao dịch của mỗi thành viên = Giá trị mua chứng khoán + Giá trị bán chứng khoán"); same structure in TT 127/2018 and TT 101/2021, which should be cited for 2019–2024 | high |
| Market-maker and green-bond discounts | ALL | A registered market maker may receive a reduction of **up to 80%** of the exchange's service prices on its market-making activity ("giảm tối đa 80% mức giá giao dịch"). **Green-bond**-related registration, listing, trading and depository services receive **50%**. The claimed "up to 70% of VSDC's prices" leg is **uncorroborated** | 2022-01-01 | current | statutory | TT 101/2021 Điều 3.5–3.6; carried into TT 83/2024. **These discount clauses live in the MECHANISM circular, not in QĐ 1541** — so a market-maker cost model must read the discount from the circular and the base rate from the decision | medium |
| VSD/VSDC custody fee | ALL | Shares, fund certificates, covered warrants **0.3đ per unit per month**; corporate bonds and public-debt instruments **0.2đ per unit per month**, capped at 2,000,000đ per code per month | 2019-02-15 | 2020-03-18 | statutory | TT 127/2018 Biểu giá Phần A | high |
| VSD/VSDC custody fee | ALL | Equity/funds/CW **0.27đ**; **corporate bonds AND công cụ nợ both 0.18đ**, max 2,000,000đ/code/month | 2020-03-19 | 2021-12-31 | statutory | TT 14/2020 | high |
| VSD/VSDC custody fee | ALL | Equity/funds/CW **0.27đ**; corporate bonds **0.18đ** max 2,000,000đ/code/month; **công cụ nợ 0.14đ max 1,400,000đ/code/month** | 2022-01-01 | current | statutory | TT 101/2021 Phần A Mục III điểm 13; re-issued unchanged by QĐ 1541. **The 0.27đ equity figure is continuous from 2020-03-19; the công cụ nợ split is not** | high |
| — the shape | | This is a **stock (holding) charge, not a flow charge** — it accrues on positions held and is debited monthly. It is the **only** underlying-market charge that is not per-fill: 100,000 shares held for a month costs 27,000đ regardless of turnover. Brokers typically re-bill it with their own monthly minimum, which is a **broker term** | | | | | high |
| VSDC corporate-action (rights exercise) fee | ALL | **5,000,000đ rising to 20,000,000đ per event** for >5,000 holders | 2019-02-15 | 2021-12-31 | statutory | TT 127/2018. **Amounts not independently confirmed** | medium |
| VSDC corporate-action fee | ALL | Graded by holders on the record date: **<500 → 3,500,000đ; 500–999 → 7,000,000đ; 1,000–4,999 → 10,500,000đ; >5,000 → 14,000,000đ** per event | 2022-01-01 | current | statutory | TT 101/2021 Phần A điểm 15; QĐ 1541 — verified verbatim | high |
| — **who pays** | | The corporate-action charge falls on the **ISSUER**, not the investor. **An investor receiving a dividend or exercising a right pays no VSDC event fee** — only the 5% dividend tax where applicable. So the simulator's corporate-action handler needs **no cost model on the investor side** | | | | | high |
| VSDC off-system transfer-of-ownership fee | ALL | By transaction type, on transfer value: founding-shareholder shares in lock-up **0.1%**; approved off-market transfers of listed securities **0.1%** (shares) / **0.005%** (bonds); **ETF creation/redemption swap 0.05%** of underlying basket value; corporate reorganisation (merger, split, consolidation) **0.02%**; public tender offer **0.03%**; state-capital divestment **0.03%** (shares) / 0.005% (bonds); gift/inheritance **0.1%** with exemptions for defined close-family relations | 2022-01-01 | current | statutory | TT 101/2021 Phần A điểm 17; QĐ 1541 | medium |
| — ETF arbitrage note | | The **0.05% swap fee on the underlying basket** is a real cost of creation/redemption and is **additional** to the 0.018% ETF trading fee | | | | | medium |
| VSDC securities transfer between accounts | ALL | **0.3đ per unit per transfer instance, capped at 300,000đ per instance per code.** Charged to the initiating depository member. Applies to moving a holding between member accounts (e.g. changing broker), not to trading | **start date UNVERIFIED** (sole source is QĐ 1541, a re-issue — check TT 101/2021 Phần A and TT 127/2018) | current | statutory | QĐ 1541 | medium |
| VSDC securities borrowing/lending (SBL) fee | ALL | **Zero-rated** | 2020-03-19 | 2021-12-31 | statutory | TT 14/2020 zero-rated the SBL service entirely. **The zero-rating of the SBL line specifically was not independently confirmed** — the zero-rated block observed covers listing/registration services | medium |
| VSDC SBL fee | ALL | **0.027%** of loan value on the date the loan contract is established, for shares, fund certificates, ETF units and CWs (min 500,000đ); **0.0054%** for corporate bonds (min 500,000đ); **0.0042%** for public-debt instruments (min 500,000đ). Collateral management on the loan: **0.0024% per month, min 100,000đ, max 1,600,000đ per contract per month** | 2022-01-01 | current | statutory | TT 101/2021 Phần A điểm 18; QĐ 1541. **Only relevant once the simulator models short selling or fails-borrowing** | medium |
| — 2020-01-01 → 2020-03-18 | ALL | **UNVERIFIED** | — | — | — | — | UNVERIFIED |
| Cash settlement (clearing bank) price — cap | ALL | Cap on what the settlement bank may charge a clearing member for cash settlement of the underlying market: **0.01% of the member's NET settlement value, floored at 5,000đ and capped at 300,000đ per member per day** | 2019-02-15 | 2021-12-31 | statutory | Thông tư 128/2018/TT-BTC Biểu giá Phần A điểm 9 (effective 2019-02-15) — verified verbatim ("0,01% giá trị thanh toán bù trừ ròng … tối thiểu không thấp hơn 5.000 đồng/ngày/thành viên, tối đa 300.000 đồng/ngày/thành viên"). **The separate "0.001% for corporate bonds" sub-rate was NOT corroborated — drop or flag it** | high |
| Cash settlement price — cap | ALL | **Assumed unchanged.** TT 102/2021/TT-BTC could not be opened from any mirror | 2022-01-01 | current | statutory | TT 102/2021/TT-BTC (2021-11-17) | low |
| — the shape | | Charged on the **multilateral NETTED settlement amount per member per day** with a daily floor and cap, so it is **not attributable to individual fills.** Almost always absorbed by the broker | | | | | high |
| HNX derivatives exchange trading fee | HNXDS | Index futures **3,000đ/contract**; government-bond futures **5,000đ/contract**, charged on each matched contract (i.e. on both the opening and the closing leg) | 2019-02-15 | 2020-03-18 | statutory | TT 127/2018 Biểu giá Phần B — verified verbatim. **This is the level for the first ten weeks of the window; secondary sources quoting "2,700" as "the" fee silently describe the post-cut level and will mis-cost any 2020-Q1 backtest** | high |
| HNX derivatives exchange trading fee | HNXDS | Index futures **2,700đ/contract**; GB futures **4,500đ/contract**, per matched contract, debited daily on the day's matched volume | 2020-03-19 | current | statutory | TT 14/2020; made permanent by TT 101/2021 Phần B Mục II điểm 3; re-issued unchanged by QĐ 1541. | **VSD position management fee** | HNXDS | **3,000đ per contract per account per DAY** on open positions, billed monthly. A **holding** cost, not a transaction cost | 2019-02-15 | 2020-03-18 | statutory | TT 127/2018 Biểu giá Phần B, verified verbatim ("3.000 đồng/HĐ/TK/ngày") | high |
| VSD position management fee | HNXDS | **2,550đ per contract per account per day** (a 15% cut) | 2020-03-19 | **2021-12-31** | statutory | TT 14/2020. **END DATE CORRECTED from 2025-04-28.** "Giá dịch vụ quản lý vị thế" **does not appear in TT 101/2021 Phần B Mục III**, whose item numbering 4,5,6,7,8 runs continuous with no gap where it would sit. Two independent reads agree on its absence | high |
| **VSDC derivatives clearing fee** | HNXDS | **2,550đ per novated contract ("hợp đồng thế vị")**, charged when an order **MATCHES** — once on the opening leg and again on the closing leg. | — **cost impact of the correction** | HNXDS | Under the wrong dating, every intraday round trip in a 2022-01-01 → 2025-04-28 backtest is **undercharged by 2,550đ/contract**, and every multi-day hold is **overcharged by 2,550đ/contract/day** | | | | | high |
| — **broker billing lagged the circular by up to 3 years** | HNXDS | Pinetree's dated 2024-07-11 note bills "Phí quản lý vị thế: 2,550 VNĐ/HĐ/TK/ngày" and lists **no** clearing fee; SSI's 2025-10-10 schedule bills "clearing 2,550 VND per derivative position" and **no** position fee. Both cannot be legally current. **A backtest reproducing ACTUAL 2022–2024 retail costs should use the per-day fee; one applying the gazetted schedule should use the clearing fee. State which and why — do not merge them** | 2022-01-01 | 2025-05-04 | **market practice** | Pinetree 2024-07-11 vs SSI 2025-10-10 | medium |
| — residual anomaly | HNXDS | One read of the QĐ 1541 mirror shows **both** "bù trừ chứng khoán phái sinh: 2.550 đồng/hợp đồng thế vị" **and** "quản lý vị thế: 2.550 đồng/hợp đồng/tài khoản/ngày". Either the mirror is unreliable or QĐ 1541 reintroduced a line TT 101/2021 had dropped. **For a per-day-vs-per-fill distinction with a 2× cost impact, one verbatim gazette read is mandatory before publication** | | | | | low |

| VSDC collateral management fee | HNXDS | **0.0024%** of the accumulated margin-balance value (cash + securities at par) per account per month, **min 320,000đ, max 1,600,000đ** | 2020-03-19 | **2021-12-31** | statutory | TT 14/2020 verbatim: "tối đa không quá 1.600.000 đồng … **tối thiểu không thấp hơn 320.000 đồng**". **FLOOR CORRECTED — 100,000đ is wrong for this interval** | high |
| VSDC collateral management fee | HNXDS | **0.0024%** per account per month, **min 100,000đ, max 1,600,000đ**. VPS states it verbatim: "0,0024% giá trị lũy kế số dư tài sản ký quỹ (tiền + giá trị chứng khoán tính theo mệnh giá)/tài khoản/tháng" | high |
| — the floor dominates | HNXDS | The **100,000đ/month floor** is a fixed monthly cost for anyone holding any margin, and dominates the percentage term until the accumulated balance exceeds **~4.2bn đồng** (from 2022-01-01). | — the par-value basis | HNXDS | Securities collateral is valued at **par**, so pledging a bond at par rather than market makes the fee independent of mark-to-market | | | | | high |
