| Margin cash deposit/withdrawal fee | HNXDS | **11,000đ per operation** as billed (base 10,000đ) | **BOUNDARY UNKNOWN** | current | **broker term** | DNSE, ACBS current. **No line for this charge exists in QĐ 1541**, so it is a self-priced VSDC service or a pure broker charge. **The previously published effective_from of 2025-01-01 was an invented date — deleted.** Quote the base amount, not the grossed-up figure | low |

### 8.3 Brokerage commissions

| Rule | Venue | Value | Effective from | Effective to | Kind | Source | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Commission band — underlying market | ALL | Minimum **0.15%** and maximum **0.5%** of transaction value. A broker could not charge below the floor | 2017-01-01 | 2019-02-14 | statutory | Thông tư 242/2016/TT-BTC (2016-11-11). **Never opened; the 0.15% floor figure is entirely unsourced.** TT 128/2018 confirms only that it replaced TT 242/2016 and that it sets a maximum with no floor. **Outside the window — drop from the rulebook and keep as background prose** | low |
| Commission cap, no floor — underlying market | ALL | **Maximum 0.5%** of transaction value; **the floor is REMOVED** — a broker may charge anything up to the cap, including zero. Provider: securities company; payer: the client | 2019-02-15 | 2021-12-31 | statutory | **Thông tư 128/2018/TT-BTC (2018-12-27, effective 2019-02-15)** Biểu giá Phần A điểm 2a, replacing TT 242/2016 — verified verbatim ("Tối đa 0,5% giá trị giao dịch"). Điều 3 lets each firm set its own level within the cap. **Removal of the floor is what enabled the 2020–2021 zero-fee price war (DNSE, Pinetree)** | high |
| Commission cap tightened | ALL | **Maximum 0.45%** of transaction value; still no floor. Prices stated as not subject to VAT | 2022-01-01 | current | statutory | Thông tư 102/2021/TT-BTC (2021-11-17, effective 2022-01-01), replacing TT 128/2018. **DOWNGRADED: the cited URL resolves to a document directory listing, not to the circular, and TT 102/2021 could not be opened from any mirror.** Plausible and consistent with the succession pattern, but for a traceability claim it must be re-read. No successor instrument was found as of 2026-08-25 — but a negative search could not be run to confirm | medium |
| Commission cap — derivatives | HNXDS | Index futures **max 15,000đ/contract**; GB futures **max 25,000đ/contract**. Note the shape difference from the cash market: a **fixed amount per contract**, not a percentage | 2019-02-15 | 2021-12-31 | statutory | TT 128/2018 Biểu giá Phần B | high |
| Commission cap — derivatives, tightened | HNXDS | Index futures **max 5,000đ/contract**; GB futures **max 8,000đ/contract** — a two-thirds cut | 2022-01-01 | current | statutory | TT 102/2021 Biểu giá Phần B. **DOWNGRADED (same unreadable-circular problem). Also worth re-checking that the index and GB caps are not transposed**: SSI's 2025-10-10 schedule charges 5,000đ/contract on GB futures, which would sit exactly at the claimed *index* cap | medium |
| State-divestment brokerage cap | ALL | **Max 0.03%** of transaction value, capped at **3,000,000,000đ per transaction**. Payer: the divesting entity | 2019-02-15 | current | statutory | TT 128/2018 Biểu giá Phần A điểm 2b; carried into TT 102/2021. Only relevant if the simulator models divestment auctions/put-throughs — **but it shows the regulator does use per-transaction caps, a shape the fee config should support** | medium |
| Typical retail commission — equities | ALL | **0.10%–0.35%** of transaction value, **tiered by DAILY transaction value per account**, online cheaper than broker-assisted. Example (SSI, effective 2025-10-10): online <100m đ/day **0.25%**, 100–500m **0.30%**, >500m **0.25%**; non-online 0.35% / 0.30% / 0.25%. Several entrants charge **0%**. Some firms impose a minimum charge per order | 2020-01-01 | current | **broker term** | SSI "Biểu giá dịch vụ giao dịch chứng khoán" (2025-10-10); cap set by TT 128/2018 then TT 102/2021 | medium |
| — **the tiering variable is DAILY value per account, not per-order value** | | A commission model that tiers per order will misprice. **Also: SSI's non-monotone online tier (0.25% / 0.30% / 0.25%) is REAL — confirmed on SSI's page, tiered on "Tổng giá trị giao dịch/ngày/tài khoản" — not a transcription error** | | | | | high |
| Typical retail commission — derivatives | HNXDS | **1,000–3,000đ per contract for INDEX futures**, tiered by contracts matched per **DAY**. Examples: VPS (from 2025-05-05) <200 contracts/day 2,000đ, ≥200 1,000đ; Pinetree (2024) 2,000 / 1,000 on the same 200-contract break; SSI (2025-10-10) <100 → 3,000đ, 100–300 → 2,000đ, >300 → 1,000đ; ACBS 3,000đ flat; DNSE 1,000đ flat. **GB futures are priced separately — SSI charges 5,000đ/contract** | 2024-01-01 | current | **broker term** | VPS, SSI, Pinetree, ACBS, DNSE schedules | medium |
| — **the applicable rate is only known at END OF DAY** | | VPS's note is explicit that the tier is computed on "Tổng số lượng hợp đồng tương lai chỉ số **đã khớp trong ngày**". **That is awkward for a per-fill cost model and argues for applying the commission at the daily close rather than at fill.** This is the single most useful implementation finding in the fee domain | | | | | high |
| Advance on sale proceeds (ứng trước tiền bán) | ALL | **Self-priced — no statutory cap.** Neither TT 128/2018 nor TT 102/2021 lists a price for advancing sale proceeds, so it falls under self-priced services (Điều 3.5 permits self-pricing of unlisted services) and each firm sets its own charge. `fee = amount advanced × days advanced × daily rate` | 2019-02-15 | current | **broker term** | absence from both schedules. **The STRUCTURAL claim is sourced; the numeric range is not** | high (structure) |
| — 2021 snapshot | ALL | Pinetree 0.025%/day (no min); TCBS 0.029%; VSCS 0.0329% (min 30,000đ); Mirae Asset 0.033%; VCBS 0.033%; FPTS 0.033%; SSI 0.0389% (min 50,000đ); HSC 0.04%; VNDIRECT 0.05% | 2021-05-28 | 2021-05-28 | broker term | Pinetree industry fee comparison, dated 2021-05-28 — a **competitor-published** comparison, so treat individual figures as indicative. Range 0.025–0.05%/day ≈ **9–18% p.a. on a 360-day basis** | medium |
| — 2024–2026 | ALL | Typically **0.035–0.04%/day**; DSC 0.0356%/day = **13%/yr**; cited industry span ~10.6%/yr (TCBS) to ~18.3%/yr (VNDIRECT) | 2024-01-01 | current | broker term | DSC product page (undated, so `effective_from` is the earliest year the figures were quoted, not a verified change date) | medium |
| — **ANNUALISATION BASIS CONFLICT** | | The two rows above mix bases: 0.025–0.05%/day annualised as "9–18% p.a." is **×360**, while DSC's "0.0356%/day = 13%/yr" is **×365** (0.0356 × 365 = 12.99). **A sweep configured from these rows will be internally inconsistent by ~1.4%. Declare one basis explicitly.** Also confirm whether the stated minima (30,000đ, 50,000đ) are đồng or thousand-đồng — Vietnamese fee schedules often quote the latter | | | | | high |
| — mechanics and cap | ALL | On registration the advance is credited to buying power immediately after the sell order fills on T (some brokers T+0 or T+1) and is recovered automatically from the sale proceeds at T+2 settlement; interest accrues over actual days advanced. Some brokers charge no fee when the advance is used to **buy** rather than withdraw. **CAP IS UNVERIFIED: no statutory cap exists and no broker fee schedule stating an explicit maximum percentage could be retrieved. "Up to 100% of net proceeds after fees and PIT" is the common description, not a sourced figure — mark it an assumption** | 2019-02-15 | current | broker term | ACBS, DNSE, DSC, SSI product pages | low |
| — legal status recap | | Statutory permission to offer (Luật CK Art. 86(1)(b), SSC written approval); **price and cap are broker commercial terms.** It only bites while T+2 settlement is unfinished | | | | | high |
| Fee changes attributable to KRX | ALL | **NONE.** No fee or tax change is traceable to the KRX go-live itself. QĐ 1541/QĐ-BTC, signed 2025-04-29 (six days before go-live), re-issued the existing percentage rates unchanged, and no numeric change to any verifiable line was found. What changed around 2025-05-05 is **broker DOCUMENTATION**, which finally caught up with the 2022 circular. **State it as a coincidence of timing, not causation** | 2025-05-05 | current | market practice | QĐ 1541; VPS schedule effective 2025-05-05 — which, tellingly, **still cites the already-repealed TT 101/2021 as its authority** | high |

---

## 9. CORRECTIONS TO OUR CODE

Every place `src/plutus/core/constant.py`, `src/plutus/core/order.py`,
`src/plutus/market/margin.py`, `src/plutus/market/broker.py`,
`src/plutus/market/session/deposit.py`, `src/plutus/data/audit.py` or the design spec at
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-25-exchange-simulator-design.md` holds a value this
research contradicts.

**§9.6 is different from the rest of §9.** It records corrections to **this document**, not
only to the code — the rows it lists were wrong *here* before they were wrong anywhere
else. Read it before trusting a §6.3 row you last read prior to 2026-08-26.

### 9.1 `src/plutus/market/margin.py` — the largest cluster

| File / symbol | Current value | Correct value | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `MarginConfig.vsd_initial` (line 40) | `Decimal('0.175')` | **A dated, per-contract lookup** returning `Decimal('0.13')` for 2020-01-01 → 2022-12-14 and `Decimal('0.17')` from 2022-12-15. **0.175 matches no published value at any date** — the series is 10% / 13% / 17% and 0.175 looks like a transcription slip for 0.17. Key on `(contract_code, effective_date)`: VSDC publishes per contract and names time-to-maturity as an input, so the table shape is forced even though every observed entry is 0.17 | high |
| `MarginConfig.maintenance_rate` (line 42) | `Decimal('0.175')` | **DELETE the field — do not re-parameterise it.** No maintenance margin ratio exists in Vietnamese rules at any date, and post-KRX the term `ký quỹ duy trì` is absent from the governing text entirely. **Replacement is now regime-dependent** (revised 2026-08-26, see §9.6): for **2025-05-05 →**, `MR = Max(ΣPgm, 0)` with `Pgm = Max((Rm + Sm + Dm), MM)` per underlying-asset **group** within the account, struck **once after the close**, tested by the **binary** predicate `margin_assets < MR`. For **2017-05-01 → 2025-05-04**, `MR = IM + VM` per account portfolio with the utilisation predicate — **but the 0.80 / 0.90 / 1.00 thresholds are now `low`, not `high`**, so treat them as a documented assumption, not a rule | high (that the field must go) / low (the pre-KRX thresholds) |
| `MarginConfig` docstring (lines 27-36) | `S_call / S_entry = (1 - initial_rate) / (1 - maintenance_rate)` = 0.9394, "a **6.06%** fall"; and "`maintenance_rate` is derived rather than invented: it is set to the VSD initial requirement" | **Delete both.** They are artifacts of a model shape Vietnam does not use, and the justification is doubly wrong: 0.175 is not the VSD initial requirement at any date either | high |
| Module docstring (lines 10-12) | "The values are the published Vietnamese ones, and they are config" | **False for `vsd_initial` and `maintenance_rate`.** Rewrite: the shape is a modelling assumption; the initial-margin series is press-sourced with no `quyết định` number; there is no published maintenance ratio | high |
| `evaluate_margin(...)` — the mark | Marks `posted_margin + signed × mult × (S − entry_price)`, i.e. an accumulating unrealised-P&L balance measured against **entry price** | Two separate errors. (1) **Daily P&L LEAVES OR ENTERS the account in cash on T+1**, so the margin account must **not** accumulate mark-to-market gains. (2) Mark against the **previous daily settlement price**, not `entry_price` | high |
| `evaluate_margin(...)` — the equity term | Symmetric: a favourable move raises equity and therefore the ratio | **Variation margin enters the requirement ONLY when the account is in a loss state.** A favourable move contributes zero; an adverse move adds. The current arithmetic mis-times calls in both directions | high |
| Initial-margin basis | `initial_rate × entry notional`, held fixed | **IM is recomputed on the CURRENT price** — latest matched price in-session, DSP at end of day — so the required amount moves with the market independently of P&L | high |
| Unit of assessment | `MarginState` is per **Position**; `evaluate_margin` takes one `Position` | The regulated unit is the **investor trading ACCOUNT portfolio**, aggregated to the clearing member. A two-leg calendar spread on one account is **one** MR calculation | high |
| Offsetting trades | No concept | **No new IM is charged on offsetting trades within the same trading account** | high |
| Cure window / freeze state | None; a shortfall is instantaneous and unrecoverable | **Revised 2026-08-26 (§9.6).** Needs a freeze state, a **broker-configured** client-facing cure window, and — from 2025-05-05 — a close-out-parameter-only order mode. **Drop the 80/90/100 ladder from the post-KRX margin path**: QĐ 26 Điều 13 has no warning stage, so the states are just `ok → breach → suspended → substitute-member close-out`, with a **09h30 next-trading-day** top-up deadline and **03 working days** to escalation, both of which *are* exchange-side numbers and belong in the depository config, not the broker config. Keep the ladder only on the **position-limit** path (Điều 29) and only for the pre-KRX margin path, flagged `low` | high |
| Cost of holding margin | Nothing is charged for holding posted margin | Debit the **VSDC collateral management fee**: 0.0024%/account/month with a **hard 100,000đ floor** (320,000đ before 2022-01-01), max 1,600,000đ, on cash + securities at **par** | high |
| Position limits | Not enforced anywhere | 5,000 / 10,000 / 20,000 contracts per account by investor class (**low confidence — VSDC authority, no in-window notice located**). Keep in the **clearing** config, not the exchange spec, per QĐ 21 Điều 8(1)(p) | low (values) / high (that a limit exists) |
| Per-order cap | Not enforced | **500 contracts per order** on every listed futures contract | high |
| Model shape from 2025-05-05 | Continuous pre-trade margin at VSDC | **Revised 2026-08-26 (§9.6).** Post-trade, **end-of-day** margin requirement struck from end-of-day open positions and notified **by 16h30** (not "~17:00"), with a top-up due **before 09h30 the next trading day**. Compute `MR = Max(ΣPgm, 0)`, `Pgm = Max((Rm + Sm + Dm), MM)` per underlying-asset group. **Do not call it "COMS"** — the string is not in the instrument. Also: pre-KRX IM had to be deposited *before* ordering; from the cutover IM is due no later than **09:00 on T+1** after a T0 trade (broker-sourced, unchanged) | high (shape and deadlines) / medium (the 09:00 T+1 IM figure) |
| `broker_buffer` | `Decimal('0.05')`, a percentage-of-notional add-on | The *shape* is plausible and separating it from `vsd_initial` is right, but **the broker's actual lever is its UTILISATION thresholds (75/85/90 style)**, which have no representation. Add them | high |

### 9.2 `src/plutus/core/constant.py`

| File / symbol | Current value | Correct value | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DAILY_TRADING_LIMIT` (line 39) | `{HSX: 0.07, HNX: 0.1, UPCOM: 0.15, DS: 0.07}` — one scalar per exchange for all history and all situations | Must become a function of **(venue, instrument class, security state, date)**. The ordinary values are right; **the widened values differ per venue (20/30/40%) so a single override constant will not do.** First-listing days, post-suspension resumptions, treasury-share and large-cash-dividend ex-dates, corporate splits, and the UPCoM illiquidity band are all clamped to the wrong band today | high |
| `DAILY_TRADING_LIMIT[UPCOM]` | `0.15` unconditionally | **The ±40% UPCoM illiquidity band affects 17.1% of UPCoM name-days (70,578 of 412,041).** Using 0.15 unconditionally clips fills on roughly one in six UPCoM name-days, and mis-prices exactly the thin names where a band constraint bites hardest | high |
| `DAILY_TRADING_LIMIT[DS]` | `0.07` | Correct for VN30F and VN100F; **wrong for both government bond futures, which take ±3%.** Band must be keyed to the **contract template**, not the exchange | high |
| Bonds | Subject to the venue's `daily_trading_limit` | **Bonds must be exempted from any band entirely** | high |
| Covered warrants | Get `0.07` of the warrant's own price | **Route to the derived formula** (`§3.4`). Using 7% of the warrant's own price is wrong in both directions and badly wrong for cheap warrants | high |
| Ceiling / floor computation | **Does not exist.** Nothing computes a ceiling or a floor | Implement `ceiling = floor_to_tick(ref × (1+band))`, `floor = ceil_to_tick(ref × (1−band))`, **passing the BAND PRICE into `get_tick_size`, not the reference price** (or ~2.4% of HOSE name-days are off by one tick), plus all three degenerate branches — including **floor ≤ 0 → floor = reference** (never zero) | high |
| `TICK_SIZE[HSX]` (lines 58-69) | `{(0,10): 0.01, (10,50): 0.05, (50,inf): 0.1}` with `lower ≤ p < upper` | **Correct for the whole window**, and matches the gazetted breakpoints at exactly 10.0 and 50.0 | high |
| `get_hsx_tick_size` — the ETF/CW override | `len(ticker)==8 and ticker[0] in ['C','E','F']` | **Wrongly catches HOSE closed-end funds**, whose codes are `FUC*` and 8 characters: FUCTVGF1, FUCTVGF2, FUCTVGF3, FUCTVGF4, FUCVREIT. Those are **chứng chỉ quỹ đóng** and must use the 10/50/100 banded table. Correct predicate: **ETF ⇔ code starts `FUE` or `E1`; CW ⇔ `C` + 3 letters + 4 digits; `FUC` ⇒ closed-end fund ⇒ banded table.** It also gives 8-character corporate-bond codes beginning with C a 10đ tick instead of 1đ (latent: CII42013 has no `quote_close` rows) | high |
| `TICK_SIZE[HNX]` (line 59) | `Decimal('0.1')` (=100đ) for every instrument and method; `tick_size_function = lambda _,__: Decimal('0.1')` | Correct **only** for matched round-lot stock orders. **HNX ETF certificates and listed corporate bonds take 1đ** (`Decimal('0.001')` in thousand-VND units), and put-through takes 1đ | high |
| `TICK_SIZE[UPCOM]` (line 60) | `Decimal('0.1')` | The continuous-matching tick. **Put-through is 1đ** | high |
| `TICK_SIZE[DS]` (line 58) | `Decimal('0.1')` | Correct for VN30F/VN100F as **0.1 index point**. **Government bond futures step 1 VND on a VND quote** — the same numeral attached to a different unit, exactly the confusion this document exists to prevent | high |
| Tick lookup signature | `get_tick_size(ticker, price_point)` | Needs a **third argument: trading method** (matching vs negotiated). Without it the `TICK_GRID` gate in `plutus/market/exchanges/equity.py` **rejects every legitimate put-through price** | high |
| `TRADING_UNIT` (line 36) | `{HSX: 100, HNX: 100, UPCOM: 100, DS: 1}` for all history | **HSX was 10 until 2021-01-03.** `quote_close` covers 2020 in full (94,675 HSX stock closes in 2020-01-01→2021-01-03), so the `ROUND_LOT` gate rejects orders of 10–90 shares the real HOSE accepted. Must become a dated interval. HNX/UPCOM/DS values are correct | high (that it is wrong) / medium (the 2021-01-04 boundary) |
| Maximum order size | **Does not exist.** A 10,000,000-share HOSE order would be admitted | **HOSE: 500,000 units per round-lot matching order; 99 per odd-lot order.** HNX/UPCoM: no published cap (UNVERIFIED). HNXDS: 500 contracts/order | high |
| Put-through minimum | Does not exist | HOSE **20,000**; HNX **5,000** (medium for 2022–2025); UPCoM **none**. The `ROUND_LOT` gate (`quantity % 100 == 0`) rejects a legal 20,001-share put-through, and `TICK_GRID` rejects its 1đ price. **A put-through order type needs its own admission path**: unit 1, venue minimum, 1đ tick, band still binding, no ATO/ATC participation, and **no put-through on a first trading day** | high |
| Odd lots | `ROUND_LOT` (`quantity % unit != 0` → reject) makes every odd-lot order inadmissible on every venue and every date | **HNX and UPCoM have had odd-lot matching for the whole window** — this is not a corner case. Needs a **second book**: odd-lot orders cross only with odd-lot orders, and their fills must be **excluded** from any reference-price or index computation | high |
| Session model — HSX close | `after_trading_session` starts 14:45:01; `ExchangeSpec.trading_time_end` returns 14:45 for HSX | The HOSE **put-through window runs to 15:00** in both regimes, and is unmodelled | high |
| Session model — HNX | One `lo_session` 09:00–14:30 with a nested `noon_break`, so the two continuous blocks are indistinguishable; `plo_session` is a single continuous 14:45–15:00 phase | The PLO is **two phases with different matching algorithms** from 2025-05-05: 14:45–14:55 periodic, 14:55–15:00 continuous, both round-lot only | high |
| Session model — UPCOM | Modelled correctly (`ato=None`, `atc=None`, `plo=None`, lo 09:00–15:00) | Correct. **But `ExchangeSpec.trading_time_end` raises `AttributeError` for UPCOM** (`plo_session.end if plo else atc_session.end`, both `None`). **Dead code today** — grep shows the property is never called in `src/` or `tests/` — so it is latent, not live | high |
| `AbstractTradingSession.is_current` (line ~87) | `self.start <= given_datetime.time() <= self.end` — inclusive at both ends | **Double membership** at 09:15:00, 11:30:00, 13:00:00 and 14:30:00: each instant satisfies two sessions at once. A fill engine keyed on `is_current` will **double-count boundary instants** | high |
| `CURRENCY_UNIT` (line 33) | `{'HSX': 1000, 'HNX': 1000, 'UPCOM': 1000, 'HNXDS': 1}` with the comment "Note: Review the meaning in HNXDS" | **Resolve the TODO explicitly.** Derivatives prices are **index points**, converted to VND only by the 100,000 multiplier, so a currency-unit field is meaningless for HNXDS and invites a 1000× error or a double conversion. Government-bond futures are quoted in **VND** on the same venue — a second reason the field cannot be per-venue | high |
| `TRADING_DAYS_PER_YEAR` (line 43) | `250` | **Correct, and it is not a calendar** — it is an annualisation factor used by `evaluation/performance.py` and `evaluation/metrics/risk.py`. The repo has **no trading calendar and no settlement calendar**; the settlement calendar must be added as a separate per-year data input, not hung off this constant | high |
| Fee and tax constants | **None exist.** No fee or tax constant anywhere | See **§12**. Needs, at minimum: a sell-side proportional PIT; a two-sided proportional exchange fee keyed on (venue, instrument class, date); a monthly per-unit custody accrual; a per-contract exchange fee; a per-contract-or-per-contract-day VSDC derivatives charge **whose SHAPE changes on 2022-01-01**; a monthly margin-collateral charge with a hard floor; and a broker commission tiered on **DAILY** value (equities) or **DAILY** matched contracts (derivatives) | high |
| Venue attribution | `market/adapters/datahub.py` builds `trading_unit` and the tick function from `VietnamMarketConstant` keyed on the corpus's **static** `exchangeid` | For a transferred ticker that assigns UPCoM's 100đ tick and ±15% band to rows that actually traded on HOSE under a 10đ tick and ±7% band. **The exchange model needs `(ticker, date) → venue`, not `(ticker) → venue`** — and from 2025-07-01 the venue genuinely changes over time for hundreds of tickers | high |
| `reconstruct_bands(...)` in `market/adapters/datahub.py` | Takes a tick function | **Inherits the `FUC*` bug directly**, and for UPCoM cannot work from `quote_close` alone — UPCoM's reference is a **round-lot continuous-matching VWAP**, not a close. 972 of the 1,680 corpus stock tickers are UPCoM | high |

### 9.3 `src/plutus/core/order.py` and admission

| File / symbol | Current value | Correct value | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `OrderType` (lines 47-58) | `ATO, ATC, MTL, LO, MOK, MAK, MARKET="MKT"` — **date-blind, and applied to every exchange** | Two fixes. (1) **Date-and-venue-aware admission**: HSX 2020-01-01→2025-05-04 is `LO, MP, ATO, ATC` (MTL/MOK/MAK must be **rejected** on HSX); from 2025-05-05 `LO, MTL, ATO, ATC` (MP rejected). HNX is `LO, MTL, MOK, MAK, ATC, PLO` with **no ATO**. **UPCoM is LO only.** (2) `MARKET = "MKT"` with "sell at floor or buy at ceiling for guaranteed match" semantics **matches no Vietnamese order type at any date** — not HOSE MP, not MTL. **Replace it with real MP and MTL semantics** (walk the book; residual → LO at last matched ±1 tick capped at ceiling/floor; cancel at entry if no opposite limit order) | high |
| `MOK` / `MAK` | Exposed unconditionally on every exchange | HNX and HNXDS only. **Whether HOSE admits them post-KRX is UNVERIFIED** — best evidence says no | high (that they are wrong on HSX pre-KRX and on UPCoM) |
| `PLO` | Not represented | HNX post-close only, with its own price rule (last round-lot match of the day), no amend, no cancel, and **no session at all if the day had no round-lot match** | high |
| Amend operation | **No amend operation and no per-phase legality** | A strategy can currently **cancel inside an auction, which no venue allowed at any time in 2020–2026.** Needs: auctions locked; post-close locked; from 2025-05-05 the whole derivatives periodic session locked and resting LOs frozen from 14:30; price-XOR-quantity from 2025-05-05; priority preserved only on a quantity decrease | high |
| `market/exchanges/equity.py::_admits_in_session` | Rejects any auction-phase order that is not exactly ATO/ATC, with reason "call auction accepts ATO orders only" / "…ATC orders only" | **Wrong: an LO may be entered during a call auction** (QĐ 352 Điều 14.1(c); HOSE 2025 session table gives "LO, ATO" and "LO, ATC"; ASEANSC HNX gives "LO, ATC"). **The simulator refuses a legal LO in every auction on every venue.** Conversely the same method admits **any** order type during `POST_CLOSE_PLO` without checking it is a PLO — restrict it | high |
| `market/exchanges/equity.py` lines 99-112 (`FOREIGN_ROOM`) | Rejects when `quantity > room`, with **no date parameter** | Correct only from **2025-05-05**. For any simulated date **before** 2025-05-05 the behaviour is: **accept the order and cap the fill at the remaining room**, auto-cancelling the remainder on exhaustion — **and reject at entry only once room == 0.** Must become two dated regimes | high |
| `tests/market/test_equity_admission.py::test_foreign_sell_is_not_constrained_by_room` | Admits a foreign sell with `foreign_room=0` | **Already correct** — foreign sells are never room-constrained | high |
| Cash and holdings preconditions | Order model has **no cash-availability precondition** and permits a sell without a settled long position | **100% pre-funding at order entry for the whole window** (except foreign institutions from 2024-11-02, and margin/day-trading nominally); **sell only what is already in the depository account.** A cash-equity backtest that funds a buy from unsettled proceeds, or recycles sale proceeds within the same session, **overstates achievable turnover and therefore returns** | high |
| Simultaneous opposite-side orders | Not enforced | Prohibited for the same security in the same matching session (TT 120/2020 Art. 7) — **verify whether the ban is per session or per trading day before implementing** | medium |
| Fee plumbing | `Transaction.fee` (transaction.py:115), `Order.accum_fee` (order.py:162), `Position.fee` (position.py:105) are free-form `Decimal`s that **nothing populates**; `Transaction.fee` is set only from `Order.accum_fee` deltas (transaction.py:169), and nothing sets `accum_fee`. `transaction.py:351` prorates fee by size in `acquisition_cost` | **Favourable finding**: the plumbing exists end-to-end and is simply never fed. A fee engine can be added at the **order layer** without touching the position or transaction arithmetic | high |

|---|---|---|---|
| `check_tick_grid_conformity` docstring | "The other exchanges use a flat 0.1 tick on which essentially every observed price lies, so the check would be vacuous there" | **Refuted by our own data**: 3,729 UPCoM name-days violate the 100đ grid. The check is not vacuous on UPCoM — it is a **venue-mis-tagging detector** | high |

### 9.5 `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-25-exchange-simulator-design.md`

| Location | Current text | Correct text | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| line ~300 | "It is a T+2 cycle with delivery before 13:00" | "Depository settlement 11:00–11:30 on T+2; **custodian allocation to the client by 13:00**." Two distinct instants; 13:00 is a broker obligation, not the VSDC settlement moment | high |
| line ~313 | "Settlement instants are counted in sessions the data source actually yields … so T+N is holiday-correct through Tết by construction, with no separate trading calendar" | **Wrong.** T+2 is counted in **VSDC settlement business days**, which diverge from exchange trading days around Tết — proven by Announcement 4228/TB-VSDC (VSDC closed 2026-02-16→2026-02-20; T+2 of 2026-02-12 settled 2026-02-23). **A separate settlement calendar is required as a per-year data input** | high |
| lines 325-327, row 1 | "2002-01-02 → 2012-09-03 \| T+4, sellable at session open" | **Outside the coverage window and unverified in both the date and the characterisation.** No decision number and no exact date for any 2012 step was found; VSDC's own history page says only that in 2012 it "shortened the steps in the settlement process". **Drop it, or label it explicitly unsourced** | high |
| lines 325-327, row 2 | "2012-09-04 → 2022-08-26 \| T+3, sellable at session open" | Relabel: "**cycle T+2 from 2016-01-01 (VSD Decision 211/QĐ-VSD); first sellable at T+3 open**". The *behaviour* is right; the cycle-length label is wrong, and the table cannot be cited as a cycle-length history because it has no 2016-01-01 boundary | high |
| lines 325-327, row 3 | "2022-08-29 → \| T+2, sellable from 13:00 on T+2" | Behaviour correct. **Stop attributing a cycle-length change to Decisions 109 and 110** — they changed the time of day, not the number of days | high |
| §7.1 generally | Implies the settlement regime is fully sourced | The current-regime timetable is **verbatim only to 2024-11-01**. VSDC Decisions 48/QĐ-HĐTV and 39/QĐ-HĐTV were never read, so 2024-11-02 → 2026-08-25 is continuity plus behavioural inference. Downgrade | high |
| §7.1 "UNVERIFIED" block | Lists effective dates, VSD margin ratios, tick/lot tables, band special cases, fees and taxes as needing sourcing | **Mostly discharged by this document** — but replace it with the specific residuals in **§11**, not a blanket disclaimer | high |
| Anywhere the spec calls the 2022-08-29 regime "T+1.5" | | **Never use "T+1.5" as a regime name.** Call it "T+2 with mid-day settlement" | high |

### 9.6 Corrections from primary text obtained 2026-08-26 — QĐ 26 and Phụ lục 2

**Provenance.** On **2026-08-26** two primary documents were obtained and extracted to
plain text: **Quyết định 26/QĐ-HĐTV ngày 16/4/2025** of the VSDC Hội đồng thành viên (the
derivatives clearing and settlement rulebook, 15 pages, 32 articles in 7 chapters) and its
**Phụ lục 2 — Phương pháp xác định các giá trị ký quỹ** (the margin calculation methods).
Both were read in full. Every row added or corrected on that date is traceable to a line
in those extracts, and where a formula or a threshold is at stake the Vietnamese is quoted
verbatim in the row.

**How they were obtained, and why it matters.** **`thuvienphapluat.vn` sits behind a
Cloudflare challenge that blocks automated fetching**, which is why §11 Tier 1 items 1 and
6 had stood open for so long — three prior retrieval attempts across two research passes
failed at that check, not at the search step. **These two documents came from the author
directly.** No automated retrieval route to them exists from this machine. Anyone planning
to close the remaining appendix gaps (Phụ lục 3, 6, 7, 8, and QĐ 26's Mẫu forms) should
plan on the same channel rather than re-attempting the fetch.

**A caution about what this does and does not settle.** QĐ 26 governs **2025-05-05 →**.
It says **nothing** about 2017-05-01 → 2025-05-04, and reading it does **not** retroactively
verify or refute the pre-KRX rows. Where a pre-KRX claim rested on the citation chain
QĐ 96 → QĐ 61 → QĐ 12 → **QĐ 26**, reading the last link has *removed* a support rather
than supplied one. **QĐ 61 and QĐ 12 have still never been read.**

| Row / claim | What it said before 2026-08-26 | What the primary text says | Effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| **§6.3 "Warning thresholds"** | 80 / 90 / 100 on the **margin utilisation** ratio, `high`, cited to "**Article 13**" of QĐ 96 → QĐ 61 → QĐ 12 → QĐ 26 | **QĐ 26 Điều 13 contains no percentage of any kind.** The only 80/90/100 in QĐ 26 is **Điều 29** and it measures the **position limit** | **Post-KRX: the attribution is definitively wrong.** Pre-KRX: **UNVERIFIED, not disproven** — the chain is broken at its final link and the two middle links are unread. Confidence `high` → **`low`**; the "Art. 13" citation is **withdrawn** |
| **§6.3 "Action at each level"** | The 80/90/100 action ladder, `high`, cited to "Art. 13" | Inherits the same broken citation | Scoped to `2025-05-04`; `high` → **`low`**; new post-KRX row records that the *actions* survive but the *percentage trigger* does not |
| **§6.3 "The actual call test"** | Real-time utilisation ratio, `high`, whole window | QĐ 26 Điều 13 is a **binary** test at three fixed times; `thời gian thực` occurs **zero times** | Scoped to `2025-05-04`; `high` → **`medium`** (verbatim from VSDC's page, but the page is not the instrument) |
| **§6.3 "Intraday margin checkpoints"** | "**09:30 and 14:30**", `low`, from a broker page (Pinetree) | "**Tại thời điểm 09h30**", "**Tại thời điểm 14h00**", "**Chậm nhất 16h30**" — **three** checkpoints, and the second is **14h00, not 14:30** | Corrected; `low` → **`high`** |
| **§6.3 "— haircuts"** | **UNVERIFIED**; "in rulebook appendices VSDC does not publish" | **QĐ 26 Điều 9.1: 5% / 30% / 40%** — in the **body** of the rulebook, not an appendix. Changeable on **01 working day's** notice | Resolved for 2025-05-05 →, `high`. **Pre-KRX values remain UNVERIFIED** — do not back-date |
| **§6.3 "MR — definition and scope"** | `MR = IM + VM`, `high`, `2017-05-01 → current` | Post-KRX `MR = Max(ΣPgm, 0)`, `Pgm = Max((Rm + Sm + Dm), MM)`; **VM is not a component**; P&L settles separately in cash under Điều 20 | Scoped to `2025-05-04` and cross-referenced to the post-KRX table. Later leg downgraded to `medium` (VSDC's page was last updated 2021-01-18 and cannot describe QĐ 61 or QĐ 12) |
| **§6.3 "Post-margin model (COMS)"** | MR computed "**~17:00**" using "the KRX **COMS** formula"; "QĐ 26 was never read" | **`COMS` appears nowhere** in either document. The deadline is "**Chậm nhất 16h30 ngày giao dịch**" | Title and both details corrected; `medium` → **`high`**. **Do not cite a "COMS formula" to QĐ 26** |
| **§6.3 "Portfolio margining / spread credits"** | **UNVERIFIED**; "in Phụ lục 02, which VSDC does not publish" | Phụ lục 2 answers it: **OA across underlyings** (a credit, requires Kendall-tau ≥ 0.9 grouping) and **Sm across expiries** (a charge). A VN30F2609/VN30F2612 spread gets **no credit and pays Sm** | Resolved for 2025-05-05 →, `high`. Pre-KRX still UNVERIFIED — and QĐ 96's "Phụ lục 02" is a **different appendix of a different instrument** |
| **§6.3 "Clearing-member top-up deadline"** | **UNVERIFIED**; broker "09:30 T+1" dismissed as a broker term | "**trước 09h30 ngày giao dịch liền kề tiếp theo**" — the brokers were reporting the real VSDC deadline | Resolved for 2025-05-05 →, `high`. The separate **08:45** ATC figure is still broker-only |
| **§6.3 "Margin withdrawal"** | Condition (1) = utilisation below level 3, `high`, whole window | Điều 11.1(a) is binary: assets after withdrawal must **meet the notified requirement** | Scoped to `2025-05-04`, `high` → `medium`; new post-KRX row with the 08h00–16h30 cash / 08h00–16h00 securities windows |
| **§6.3 "Delivery margin (DM)"** | E+1 → E+3, **replacing** initial margin, `high`, whole window | Post-KRX `Dm` is computed on the **last trading day and the next working day only**, only for contracts with no deliverable bond lodged, and is a **summand** inside `Pgm`, not a replacement | Scoped to `2025-05-04`; corrected post-KRX row added |
| **§6.3 "— CONFLICT on multi-day cure periods"** | 3-day (margin) vs 5-day (position limit), unresolvable, `low` | **03 working days on both sides** — Điều 13.3 and Điều 29.5 carry the identical clock. **5 days appears nowhere in QĐ 26** | Resolved post-KRX, `high`. Pre-KRX unchanged at `low` |
| **§6.4 "— cure period"** | 5 business days, undated | See above | Scoped explicitly to `2020-01-01 → 2025-05-04` and flagged as contradicted by the successor instrument |
| **§6.5 chain, QĐ 26 row** | "medium (never read)" | Read in full; Điều 2's effective-date mechanism and the QĐ 12 repeal are now verbatim | `medium` → **`high`** for instrument, articles and Phụ lục 2; the **calendar** date 2025-05-05 remains inferred |

**Consequent code corrections** (in addition to the revised `margin.py` rows in §9.1):

| File / symbol | Current value | Correct value | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `src/plutus/market/broker.py` — `BrokerTerms`, the 80/90/100 utilisation ladder | Three margin-utilisation thresholds presented as exchange-derived, sourced in our rulebook to "Article 13" | **Two separate problems.** (1) **Sourcing**: the ladder is now `low`, and for 2025-05-05 → it is **not in the governing text at all**. (2) **Object placement**: to the extent a broker runs a 75/85/90-style ladder, that is a genuine *commercial* term and `BrokerTerms` is the right home — but it must be documented as **the firm's own choice**, not as QĐ 26 Điều 13. **Do not delete the mechanism; delete the citation and the claim that it is a rule.** House rule: broker terms are assumed and per-firm; exchange rules are gazetted, dated and cited | high |
| `src/plutus/market/session/deposit.py` — `margin_status` | Returns a laddered status from a margin utilisation ratio | For a **post-KRX** simulation the depository-side status set is **binary plus escalation**: `ok` (assets ≥ MR) → `breach` (assets < MR, detected only at the 09h30 sweep) → `suspended` (new opening trades barred, offsetting permitted) → `substitute_close_out` (after 03 working days). **There is no 80% or 90% depository state after 2025-05-04.** If the laddered status is retained it must be labelled a broker-side view | high |
| Both files — regime boundary | Single regime | The margin monitoring model **changes shape on 2025-05-05**, not just parameters: continuous/real-time ratio before, once-daily binary test after. **A single code path cannot represent both correctly** | high |
| Both files — what is NOT a broker term | The cure window was previously recorded as broker-only | **Two of these numbers are now exchange-side and dated**: the **09h30 next-trading-day** top-up deadline and the **03 working days** to substitute-member close-out are in QĐ 26 Điều 13. They belong in the depository/exchange config. The **client-facing** call window remains a broker term | high |

---

## 10. THE KRX DELTA

Everything the 2025-05-05 cutover changed, before and after. Rows marked **†** were
previously believed to be KRX deltas and are **not** — they are listed because a simulator
built from the common summaries would date them wrongly.

| # | Rule | Before (to 2025-05-04) | After (from 2025-05-05) | Venues | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Closing-price definition** — and therefore the reference price | No trade that day → the **previous closing price carries forward** | No **round-lot** match that day → closing price = **that day's opening reference price**. Closing price is explicitly the last round-lot match, so odd-lot prints cannot set it | HSX, HNX | high |
| 2 | ATO/ATC priority | ATO/ATC matched **ahead of all** limit orders, unconditionally | Type priority abolished **narrowly**: ATO/ATC no longer outranks a **ceiling-buy or floor-sell LO entered earlier**; still ahead of LOs priced inside the band | HSX, HNX(ATC), HNXDS | high |
| 3 | ATO/ATC display | Shown as a bare "ATO"/"ATC" tag with no price | Displayed at a price (indicative cross, else last match, else reference; or best opposite ±1 tick). Board switches from the 3 best **resting** levels to the 3 best levels **expected to remain after** the cross | HSX, HNX | medium |
| 4 | Order amendment | Price **and** quantity in one instruction ("và/hoặc") | **Price XOR quantity** per instruction | HSX, HNX, UPCOM, HNXDS | high (HNX, UPCoM, HNXDS) / medium (HOSE — HOSE's own publications omit it) |
| 5 | Resting LOs at the auction boundary | **UNVERIFIED** whether the carry-over freeze existed | Resting LOs carried from the continuous session are **frozen** (no amend, no cancel) once the closing auction opens at 14:30 | HSX, HNX | medium |
| 6 | HOSE market order | **MP** | **MTL** (same economics, new mnemonic). MOK/MAK **not** introduced on HOSE | HSX | medium |
| 7 | Derivatives MTL residual repricing | Residual → LO at the **last matched price** | Residual → LO at last matched **±1 tick**, capped at ceiling/floor | HNXDS | high |
| 8 | Derivatives simultaneous amendment | Permitted | "Không được phép đồng thời sửa giá và khối lượng" | HNXDS | high |
| 9 | **Foreign room decrement** | At **MATCH** (order matching) / at **EXECUTION** (put-through) | At **ORDER ENTRY** (both) | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | high |
| 10 | **Foreign room rejection trigger** † | Reject at entry once room **== 0**; an order resting while room was positive filled up to the room and had its remainder auto-cancelled | Reject at entry whenever room **< order quantity**. No partial acceptance, no queueing — a foreign buyer must self-slice | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | high |
| 11 | Foreign room — amend and cancel | Unmatched quantity never consumed room, so no restore rule existed | Amend-down and cancel **restore** room immediately; amend-up consumes it and is **rejected** if room < increment | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | high |
| 12 | Foreign room display refresh events | Display refreshed on foreign buy-order **entry or cancellation**, while the decrement happened at **match** — two different event sets | Decrement and refresh coincide; displayed volume explicitly **capped at remaining room** | HSX | high |
| 13 | Put-through initiation | **Seller** enters, buyer confirms | **Either side** may enter; the other confirms | HSX, HNX | high |
| 14 | Put-through immutability | Cannot be cancelled, **but could be amended** with counterparty agreement and exchange approval | **Neither amended nor cancelled.** Only remedy: an offsetting trade, or trade elimination with VNX **and SSC** approval | HSX, HNX | high |
| 15 | Put-through room branches | 4 branches (incl. foreign-sell-to-domestic-buyer restoring room at settlement) | **3 branches** in the published text; the sell-side branch is absent and its survival is UNVERIFIED | HSX, HNX, UPCOM | high |
| 16 | HNX post-close session | Single **continuous** window 14:45–15:00 | **Split**: 14:45–14:55 **periodic** auction, 14:55–15:00 continuous. Round lot only. Still no amend/cancel | HNX | medium |
| 17 | Odd lots — HOSE sessions | Continuous only (09:15–11:30, 13:00–14:30) | Full round-lot calendar, i.e. **odd lots gain the ATO and ATC auctions** | HSX | high |
| 18 | Odd lots — HNX sessions | 09:00–14:30 (excluded from the closing auction and the post-close) | Continuous **plus the closing auction**; **still excluded from the post-close** | HNX | high |
| 19 | Odd lots — order types | Not restricted in the article text | **LO only** — ATO/ATC not accepted for odd lots | ALL | high |
| 20 | Odd lots — unit extended | Shares and fund certificates | **plus covered warrants** (QĐ 30 had already added CWs in 2022 for HOSE; the 2025 article generalises) | ALL | medium |
| 21 | Odd-lot blocking trigger | "until a **closing price** is established" | "until a price is established from **round-lot matching**" | ALL | high |
| 22 | Restricted securities — HOSE | Afternoon session only (for C/UC under QĐ 352); **delegated to the exchange** under QĐ 17 | Round lot only, **periodic auction only, all day**: ATO + 15 × 15-minute PCA + ATC, LO only in the PCA rounds; no amend/cancel in ATO/ATC or in the **last 5 minutes** of each PCA round | HSX | high |
| 23 | Post-halt resumption | Resume normal trading, no special matching session | **15-minute periodic call auction** on resumption, LO only, round **and** odd lots, no amend/cancel; then normal trading. UPCoM resumes straight into continuous matching | HSX, HNX | medium |
| 24 | Level-3 margin action — machine form | Not specified at exchange level | Trading system ingests VSDC's restricted-account list; permits cancellation, and **"chỉ nhận lệnh mới có tham số close-out"** | HNXDS | high |
| 25 | **Derivatives margin model** | **Pre-margin**: margin lodged with VSDC before an order could be placed, recomputed against live prices intraday | **Post-margin**: margin held at the clearing member, trade immediately; VSDC strikes MR from **END-OF-DAY open positions** and notifies **by 16h30**; member tops up **before 09h30 the next trading day**. **Not "COMS"** — see §9.6 | HNXDS | **high** |
| 25a | **Derivatives margin COMPOSITION** | `MR = IM + VM` per account portfolio, variation margin counted only in a loss state | **`MR = Max(ΣPgm, 0)` with `Pgm = Max((Rm + Sm + Dm), MM)` per underlying-asset GROUP within the account. Variation margin is not a component at all** — position P&L settles separately in cash (Điều 20). Four new components: scenario risk margin over a 21-point grid, basis margin across expiries, delivery margin (GB futures, last trading day + 1), and a minimum margin floor | HNXDS | high (post) / medium (pre) |
| 25b | **Margin breach detection** | Real-time utilisation ratio with **80 / 90 / 100** warning levels | **Binary test** (`assets < MR`) at **09h30 / 14h00 / 16h30**. **No warning band at all**; 09h30 only adds breaches, 14h00 only releases them, so an intraday margin call is structurally impossible. 03 working days to substitute-member close-out | HNXDS | high (post) / **low** (pre — the ladder's citation chain is broken, §9.6) |
| 25c | **Collateral haircuts** | Believed to be in an unpublished appendix | **In the rulebook body: 5% government/government-guaranteed bonds, 30% VN30/HNX30 constituents, 40% everything else**, changeable on 01 working day's notice (Điều 9). **Not a KRX delta in substance — a delta in what we could see**; the pre-KRX values remain unverified, so this row must not be read as "the haircuts changed at the cutover" | HNXDS | high (post) / UNVERIFIED (pre) |
| 26 | Derivatives IM deposit timing | Before placing orders | No later than **09:00 on T+1** after a T0 trade | HNXDS | medium |
| 27 | Derivatives account activation | Account opened on T could trade from T+1 | Same day, on VSDC approval | HNXDS | medium |
| 28 | NPF shortfall handling | Purchased shares moved to the broker's proprietary account from T+2 | Shares **stay in the investor's account to end of T+3** where repayment is confirmed — **which preserves the foreign room** | ALL | medium |
| 29 | Settlement failure remedies | Settlement of a cash-short trade could be **deferred or cancelled** | **No deferral, no cancellation.** Borrow-to-deliver; Payment Support Fund draw for the **full** shortfall; VSDC may freeze and sell a defaulting member's proprietary securities | ALL | medium |
| 30 | Derivatives contract codes | Legacy tickers (VN30Fyymm) | Legacy tickers **retained** for contracts already listed; new contracts get a 9-character coded identifier | HNXDS | low |
| 31 | Derivatives out-of-hours order entry | Entry from 08:45 only | Reportedly entry until 08:45 of the next trading day; cancellable, not amendable. **QĐ 21 contains no such provision — may be a broker feature** | HNXDS | low |
| 32 | Auction-order-only book — HNX † | | HNX's closing auction determines **no price** when only ATC orders are on the book, unlike HOSE/HNXDS which impute reference ±1 tick. **Not a KRX delta — a standing venue difference a shared auction routine will get wrong** | HNX | medium |
| — | **HOSE afternoon put-through split at 14:45** | | **CLAIMED AND REJECTED.** Two HOSE publications show a single undivided 13:00–15:00 window. The split is real on **HNX and UPCoM** only | HSX | high (that the HOSE claim fails) |
| — | Session clock times † | | **UNCHANGED.** The only session-time changes are internal to the after-hours block (HNX PLO split, odd-lot window extension) plus the restricted-securities regime. **Do not trust broker pages here**: one large broker's HOSE page still publishes a 13:00–15:00 continuous session with a 15:00 closing auction, which is wrong | ALL | medium |
| — | Daily price bands † | | **UNCHANGED** (7 / 10 / 15%, widened 20 / 30 / 40%). But the *triggers* changed: the durative "until a round-lot price exists" clause and the ≥25 harmonisation are both KRX-era | ALL | medium |
| — | Tick grid and lot sizes † | | **UNCHANGED** | ALL | medium |
| — | Settlement cycle † | | **UNCHANGED at T+2.** Launch journalism repeatedly asserted T+0/T+1. It did not happen | ALL | high |
| — | Fees and taxes † | | **NO change traceable to KRX.** QĐ 1541/QĐ-BTC (2025-04-29) re-issued the existing rates unchanged. What changed around 2025-05-05 is broker **documentation**, catching up with the 2022 circular | ALL | high |
| — | Order **types** on derivatives † | | **UNCHANGED** — QĐ 20 Điều 22 and QĐ 21 Điều 22 list identically. The *semantics* changed (rows 7, 8) | HNXDS | high |
| — | Amendment time-priority rule † | | **NOT a KRX delta.** QĐ 17 Điều 22.3 (2022-03-31) already had "priority unchanged if quantity is only reduced; recomputed on a quantity increase and/or price change". The only 2025 edit is "và/hoặc" → "hoặc", forced by row 4 | HSX, HNX | high |
| — | Odd-lot unit of 1 and reference/index exclusion † | | **NOT KRX deltas.** Both verbatim in QĐ 17 Điều 33, effective 2022-03-31 | ALL | high |
| — | Security status codes and ex-rights codes | | **ARE KRX deltas** (WID…NWN and 01–07/16 both enter with QĐ 22/2025 Phụ lục III), though they are commonly back-dated to 2022 | HSX, HNX | high |

## 11. OPEN QUESTIONS

Phrased so that each can be closed by obtaining one document or asking one person a
direct question. Ordered by how much the answer changes the simulator.

### Tier 1 — blocks a traceability claim or a load-bearing number

1. **Can we obtain Phụ lục III of the VNX Quy chế, in the 2022, 2025 and 2026 versions?** It carries the applicable-order-type matrix per exchange (QĐ 17 Điều 17.1 delegates it verbatim), the special-case price bands, the trading unit, the tick grid, and the restricted-securities method-and-time schedule. **One document moves roughly a dozen rows from `medium` to `high` and settles the MP-withdrawal and MOK/MAK-on-HOSE questions.** Note for whoever retries: ThuVienPhapLuat sits behind a Cloudflare check; the Vietstock scan of QĐ 17 is a 79-page image PDF with no text layer; this machine has `pypdf` but **no** `pdftotext`, `mutool`, `qpdf`, `pdfminer`, `PyMuPDF` or OCR — installing an OCR toolchain is the concrete unblock.
2. **What do HOSE QĐ 66 and QĐ 67/QĐ-SGDHCM (2018-03-02), as amended by QĐ 462 (2020-08-14) and QĐ 894 (2020-12-30), actually say?** These govern 2020-01-01 → 2021-07-04 — 22.5% of the window — and QĐ 894 amended the band/tick/lot instrument one day into it. Numbers are known; only the texts are missing. Cheap partial substitute: the corpus itself dates the lot change (HOSE prints in 2020 at 10-share granularity) and any HOSE odd-lot print before 2022 would date the start of on-exchange odd-lot matching.
3. **Can we read VSDC Decision 39/QĐ-HĐTV (2025-04-29) and Decision 48/QĐ-HĐTV (2024-10-30)?** The headline settlement rule (T+2, allocation by 13:00) is asserted for 2024-11-02 → 2026-08-25 from a twice-superseded document. Three retrieval routes failed and VSDC's public page carries no clock times. **Ask VSDC directly, or request the VBMA/ThuVienPhapLuat PDF from a non-blocked network.**
4. **Does Quyết định 1541/QĐ-BTC really carry BOTH the derivatives clearing fee AND the position-management fee?** One mirror read shows both; TT 101/2021 Phần B Mục III shows only the clearing fee, with continuous item numbering. **This decides whether an intraday derivatives round trip costs 2,550đ or 5,100đ per contract from 2025-04-29. Needs one verbatim gazette read.**
5. **Can we read Thông tư 102/2021/TT-BTC?** Two rows previously marked `high` (the 0.45% equity commission cap and the 5,000/8,000đ derivatives caps) rest on a URL that resolves to a directory listing. It is also needed to confirm whether the cash-settlement price changed on 2022-01-01, and whether the index and GB derivatives caps are transposed.
6. ~~**Can we read VSDC Decision 26/QĐ-HĐTV (2025-04-16) and obtain the COMS margin formula?**~~ **CLOSED 2026-08-26.** QĐ 26 and Phụ lục 2 were obtained from the author and read in full (§9.6). The presumption that the clearing rulebook did not change at the cutover was indeed unsafe: it changed the margin *shape*, not just its parameters. Haircuts turned out to be in the **body** (Điều 9), not an appendix. There is **no "COMS formula"** — the string appears nowhere in the instrument. **Three residuals split out of this item, below as 6a–6c.**
6a. **What is the equation in Phụ lục 2 mục 1.3(c) that turns `VaR` and `n` into the initial margin ratio?** The heading and the where-list survived extraction; **the formula line did not**. Without it the published 17% cannot be reproduced from the method, only accepted on VSDC's authority. **This is the single highest-value missing line in the derivatives domain**, and it is one line of one page of a document we now have — most likely recoverable from the original PDF rather than by a new retrieval.
6b. **Does the scenario formula in Phụ lục 2 mục 1.2 read `Sk = S0 × (1 + k × r/10)`?** As extracted it prints `Sk = S0 x (1 + tỷ lệ ký quỹ ban đầu/10)` with no `k`, which is degenerate — the same string repeats in all 21 table rows, so a superscript or inline-math run was almost certainly dropped. **Our reconstruction is `low` and the paper must not print the formula as gazetted until the original PDF is checked.** Same source as 6a; check both at once.
6c. **Can we obtain QĐ 26's Phụ lục 3, 6, 7 and 8?** **Phụ lục 8** carries the cheapest-to-deliver method that `Rm`, `Sm` and `Dm` all depend on for GB futures; **Phụ lục 6** carries the DSP determination method, which would move §6.2's DSP row from `medium` to `high` and is the band anchor on every derivatives day; **Phụ lục 3** carries the liquidity method behind the eligible-collateral list; **Phụ lục 7** carries the settlement procedures. Route: **the author, not the web** — see the Cloudflare note in §9.6.
6d. **What do QĐ 61/QĐ-VSD Art. 13 and QĐ 12/QĐ-HĐTV actually say?** **This got *more* urgent, not less.** Reading QĐ 26 removed the last link that supported the 80/90/100 margin ladder for the pre-KRX regime, and 2017-05-01 → 2025-05-04 is the majority of the coverage window. Until one of these two is read, **the pre-KRX margin ladder is an assumption with a broken citation**, and it is currently the largest `low`-confidence claim that a simulator actually executes on.
7. **Can we obtain the VNX-issued Mẫu hợp đồng tương lai as an instrument, with an edition date?** QĐ 21 Điều 5 delegates band, tick, lot, months, order limit, position limit, reference price, DSP, FSP and margin level to it. Everything currently rests on HNX's web reproduction plus broker PDFs of **at least two different editions** — which is why row-number citations of the form "row N of the 21-row template" match a superseded edition and are off by one from row 7 onward.
8. **What is the VN30 index dissemination frequency during 14:15–14:45?** Without it the final settlement price cannot be implemented exactly, because the effect of trimming 3 high and 3 low values depends on the sample size. **This is a phone call to HOSE or to any broker's derivatives desk.**
9. **Is `quote_foreignroom` the CAP or the REMAINING room?** `README.md:359` and the fill-model plan say cap; `tests/sample_data/README.md` says available room. **They cannot all be right, and the 2025-05-05 semantics-break argument depends on which.**

### Tier 2 — changes a rule value or an interval boundary

10. **Was any temporary band override ordered between 2020 and 2026?** The SSC has explicit statutory power (TT 120/2020 khoản 2 Điều 4) and the rulebooks add exchange-level routes. The corpus shows none for 2021-02 → 2022-12, but **March-2020 COVID and any 2026 widening proposal were never checked.** This is the largest unclosed risk to the "no band change" claim.
11. **Do VNX QĐ 22/QĐ-HĐTV (2026-03-16) and QĐ 23/QĐ-HĐTV (2026-03-18) have effective dates, and did their appendices change any tick, lot or band?** The trading *articles* were compared and are materially unchanged; the effective-date field is paywalled and Phụ lục III was not obtained. 163 days of the window.
12. **What did HNX QĐ 653/QĐ-SGDHN (2018-10-12) and HNX QĐ 455/QĐ-SGDHN (2017-06-20) actually say?** Every HNX value is verified only from 2022-03-31, every UPCoM value only from 2022-11-16. **The UPCoM foreign-room rule for 2020-01-01 → 2025-05-04 (80.3% of the window for that venue) is pure inference by symmetry.**
13. **Do QĐ 62/QĐ-SGDHN (2019-01-24) and QĐ 634/QĐ-SGDHN (2019-09-24) exist?** Neither is attested by anything in evidence — not by any repeal clause, not by HNX's own page. **Do not print them until located.**
14. **Which HOSE and HNX decisions repealed QĐ 352 and QĐ 653 on 2022-03-31?** QĐ 17 Điều 2 says only "hiệu lực kể từ ngày ký" and names no replaced instrument, so the supersession is inferred.
15. **What is the pre-2022-05-20 HNX futures rulebook?** QĐ 20 Điều 2 names no repealed predecessor, so 2020-01-01 → 2022-05-19 has no identified listing-and-trading instrument for derivatives.
16. **What is the DSP rule for 2020-01-01 → 2022-05-31, and what happens when a contract has no matched trades at all in a day?** QĐ 96/QĐ-VSD (2017-03-23) is the governing text. The DSP is the band anchor *and* the variation-margin mark, so this gap affects ~40% of the window on every derivatives day.
17. **Was the derivatives 14:45 close ever published in a primary source?** HNX QĐ 508/QĐ-SGDHN (2025-04-29) is almost certainly the document; it was located but never read. The contract template defines the hours only *relatively*, and QĐ 352 puts the HOSE close at 15:00 — so **no document in hand establishes the 14:45 derivatives close.** Only the 08:45 open is corroborated.
18. **Does the derivatives MTL residual convert at the last matched price ±1 tick (equity rule, MBS) or at best bid/ask ±1 tick (Vietcap §8)?** The two disagree and it changes the resting price after **every** derivatives market order.
19. **Are there VSDC position-limit notices inside 2020–2026?** The current contract template stopped printing the numbers and delegates to VSDC; the 5,000/10,000/20,000 triple rests on a 2017 announcement plus undated broker copies. **Also: what added the 20,000 professional tier, and when?**
20. **Did the initial margin ratio stay at 17% at every notice date?** Verified at 2024-08-16, 2024-11-22, 2024-12-20, 2025-12-19 and 2026-08-21; six 2026 notices were not opened, and nothing between 2022-12-15 and 2024-08-16 was sampled. Given the 1st/10th/20th cadence, either open every notice or state the interval as verified-at-sampled-dates.
21. **Does Thông tư 68/2024/TT-BTC have a staged commencement?** A flat 2024-11-02 for the whole circular looks too clean, and two rows depend on it.
22. **Is the TT 120/2020 Art. 7 ban on opposite-side orders per matching session or per trading day?** This changes admission logic directly.
23. **Was the non-bank credit institution 50% aggregate cap new in ND 69/2025 or pre-existing in ND 01/2014?** Needs ND 01/2014 Điều 7.
24. **Which banks actually qualify for the ND 69/2025 49% branch?** Deliberately not hard-coded; needs the list.
25. **Is HOSE put-through of 100–19,999 units genuinely impossible?** That is the literal reading of "đơn vị lô lớn: 1, khối lượng tối thiểu 20.000" together with the separate odd-lot path, but no source addresses the gap. **One question to a HOSE member firm's dealing desk.**

### Tier 3 — needed for completeness, not for correctness of the core model

26. **Thông tư 120/2020/TT-BTC full text** — the statutory layer above every exchange rulebook here. Would settle the trading-method taxonomy, the circuit breaker (its Điều 5), the SSC band power (Điều 4 khoản 2), its own commencement article, and the 2020 odd-lot question.
27. **TT 111/2013 Điều 10 and Nghị định 126/2020/NĐ-CP Điều 7** — the entire dividend and stock-dividend tax mechanic is currently uncited with a non-supporting URL. If the simulator models corporate-action cash flows, this is the biggest substantive tax hole.
28. **Did TT 87/2026/TT-BTC change anything for ordinary securities, dividends or the 5% investment-capital rate?** Every source reached covers only its derivatives article, so **2026-07-01 → 2026-08-25 has an unverified underlying-market tax regime.**
29. **TT 103/2014 Điều 13 and TT 78/2014** for the institutional CIT branch; **TT 242/2016** for the pre-2019 commission floor. Both outside the critical path for a retail simulator — candidates for dropping rather than verifying.
30. **Is covered-warrant exercise settlement taxed at 0.1% (transfer) or 5% (investment income)?**
31. **What is the operator in QĐ 22/2026 Điều 37.1** — the warrant settlement-price adjustment prints "+" where a division is required. No second rendering obtained. **Do not implement the literal "+".**
32. **The HOSE thông báo authorising odd-lot trading from 2022-09-12**, and the instrument that suspended it from 2021-01-04.
33. **The HNX government-bond trading rulebook** — needed before the instrument-classes row can claim government bonds.
34. **HOSE ex-rights codes 08–15 and 17+** — gazetted but the published list stops at 07 and 16, so the gaps are reserved-but-unpublished numbers.
35. **Circular 139/2025/TT-BTC** (HNX→HOSE deadline extension) — number, signing date and effective date all unverified. And **how far had the migration actually progressed by 2026-08-25?**
36. **Thông tư 08/2026/TT-BTC** effective date; **Luật 56/2024/QH15** effective date; **Thông tư 122/2025/TT-BTC** content and what governed warrant adjustment before 2026-03-16.
37. **The VN100 index futures listing history** — HNX's template gave "Ngày niêm yết: khi ra mắt hợp đồng" at one point and the launch is reported as 2025-10-10. Confirm, and confirm whether it traded inside the window.
38. **Did the KRX cutover involve any pilot, staged rollout or fallback window?** No document describing the rollout mechanics was found; the big-bang conclusion is inferred from the union of the enabling instruments.
39. **Was there a rounding convention for the futures ceiling/floor onto the 0.1-point grid?** QĐ 21 Điều 16 gives the formula and the degenerate cases but no rounding rule.
40. **Did KRX introduce odd-lot-specific pricing on HOSE?** "No fee found" must not harden into "no fee exists".

---

## 12. FEE AND TAX MODEL

Fees and taxes are **modelled and configurable per venue**. This section is the
implementable shape. It is deliberately generic: every charge is described by the same
seven fields, so a single config schema covers all of them.

### 12.1 Conventions — declare these once, in code

| Convention | Value |
|---|---|
| Rate representation | **Decimal fractions**, never percent strings. `0.00027`, not `"0.027%"` |
| Cash amounts | **Absolute VND**, never thousand-VND. `2550`, not `2.55` |
| Unit conversion, cash venues | The corpus stores equity prices in **thousands of VND** (`UNIT_PRICE = 1000`, `CURRENCY_UNIT[HSX/HNX/UPCOM] = 1000`). So an HSX price of `25.5` is **25,500 VND**, and `notional = price × 1000 × quantity` **before** applying any rate |
| Unit conversion, derivatives | HNXDS quotes **index points** for VN30F/VN100F. `notional = index_points × 100000 × contracts`. **`CURRENCY_UNIT['HNXDS'] = 1` is meaningless here and must not be applied as a multiplier.** Government-bond futures quote **VND** on a 100,000đ face — a second reason the field cannot be per-venue |
| Contracts vs lots | `TRADING_UNIT` is 100 for the cash venues and 1 for HNXDS. **Derivatives fees are per CONTRACT, not per lot.** State explicitly in the config whether a per-contract fee is charged per side |
| Annualisation basis for daily financing rates | **Declare one basis and use it.** The source material mixes ×360 and ×365, producing a ~1.4% inconsistency. Recommend **365** with the basis recorded in the config |
| Rounding | **UNVERIFIED for every charge.** No source states a rounding rule for any fee or tax amount. Round to whole đồng and record it as a modelling choice |
| VAT | Prices were **VAT-exempt to 2025-04-28** and **VAT-exclusive "(nếu có)" from 2025-04-29**. But brokers demonstrably billed VSDC derivatives charges grossed up 10% even during the exemption. Model VAT as a **per-charge boolean plus a rate**, default off, and note the conflict |

### 12.2 The generic charge shape

```
Charge:
  id                  # stable key
  venue               # HSX | HNX | UPCOM | HNXDS | ALL
  instrument_classes  # {stock, closed_end_fund, etf, covered_warrant,
                      #  corporate_bond, govt_debt, index_future, bond_future}
  side                # buy | sell | both | none (holding charges)
  base                # notional | net_notional | quantity | contracts |
                      #   contract_days | margin_balance | events | flat
  rate                # Decimal fraction, or absolute VND per base unit
  minimum, maximum    # optional, in VND, with the scope the cap applies to
  levied_by           # exchange | depository | settlement_bank | broker | state
  paid_by             # investor | member | issuer | divesting_entity
  debited             # per_fill | daily_close | monthly | at_settlement | per_event
  tier_variable       # none | daily_value_per_account | daily_contracts_per_account
  effective_from, effective_to
  vat_applies         # bool + rate
```

Two shapes in the source material do **not** fit a naive per-fill model and must be
supported explicitly:

- **`base = contract_days`** — the pre-2022 derivatives position-management fee accrued per
  open contract per account per **day**. Whether the day count used end-of-day open
  positions or intraday maxima is **UNVERIFIED**; retail guides imply an intraday round
  trip paid it once, i.e. end-of-day counting.
- **`tier_variable = daily_value_per_account` / `daily_contracts_per_account`** — broker
  commissions tier on the **day's total**, so **the correct rate is not knowable at fill
  time.** Accrue fills and apply commission at the **daily close**.

### 12.3 Taxes

| Charge | Venue | Base | Rate | Min / Max | Levied by | Paid by | Debited | Interval | Confidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PIT, securities transfer (individual, resident or non-resident) | ALL cash | gross sale notional | **0.001** | none | state (withheld by the broker) | investor | **per_fill, SELL side only** | 2015-01-01 → current | high (value) |
| PIT, derivatives transfer (individual; foreign organisation) | HNXDS | `settlement_price × multiplier × contracts × IM_ratio / 2` | **0.001** (≡ 0.0005 × margined value) | none | state (withheld by the broker) | investor | **per_fill, BOTH legs**, and at maturity | 2017-08-10 → current | medium |
| — **the coupling** | | The base is **linear in the VSD initial margin ratio**: 0.13 to 2022-12-14, 0.17 from 2022-12-15. **Must read the same dated series as `margin.py`** | | | | | | high |
| CIT, domestic organisation | ALL | net gain, annually | 0.20 | — | state | investor | **NOT per_fill** — annual, out of the fill model | 2016-01-01 → current | low |
| CIT, foreign organisation without a PE | ALL cash | gross sale proceeds | 0.001 | none | state (withheld) | investor | per_fill, sell side | 2014-10-01 → current | low |
| Cash dividend tax | ALL | gross cash dividend | **0.05** | none | state (withheld by the payer) | investor | **per_event** — net off before crediting cash | 2009-01-01 → 2026-06-30 | low (uncited) |
| Stock dividend / bonus tax | ALL | **par value** at issue (or market price at sale if lower) | 0.05 | none | state (withheld by the securities company from 2020-12-05) | investor | **per_fill on SALE**, in addition to the 0.1% transfer tax on proceeds | 2020-12-05 → 2026-06-30 | low (uncited) |
| Fund distribution relief | ALL | PIT on fund distributions | **50% reduction** | — | state | investor | per_event | 2026-07-01 → current | high |
| Open-end fund holding exemption | ALL | transfer of open-end fund units held **≥2 years** | **exempt** | — | state | investor | per_fill | 2026-07-01 → current | high |
| Green-bond exemption | ALL | green-bond interest; **first** transfer of a green bond | **exempt** | — | state | investor | per_fill / per_event | 2026-07-01 → current | high |
| — **the path-dependency warning** | | The 2-year exemption is the **first holding-period-dependent tax** in the code. A pure per-fill tax function is no longer sufficient from 2026-07-01 for open-end fund units — the model needs lot-level acquisition dates | | | | | | high |

### 12.4 Exchange trading service price (state-set, two-sided)

`fee = rate × (buy notional + sell notional)`, charged **to both counterparties' members**,
`debited = per_fill`, **no minimum, no maximum, no per-order component**. Levied by the
exchange, paid by the trading member, recovered inside the client commission.

| Instrument class | 2019-02-15 → 2020-03-18 | 2020-03-19 → 2021-12-31 | 2022-01-01 → current |
|---|---|---|---|
| HSX / HNX shares, non-ETF fund certificates | 0.0003 | 0.00027 | 0.00027 |
| ETF units | 0.0002 | 0.00018 | 0.00018 |
| Covered warrants | 0.0002 | 0.00018 | 0.00018 |
| UPCoM shares / fund certificates | 0.0002 | 0.00018 | 0.00018 |
| Corporate bonds | 0.00006 | 0.000054 | 0.000054 |
| Public-debt instruments (công cụ nợ) | 0.00006 | **0.000054** | **0.000042** |
| Govt-bond repo, ≤2 days | — | 0.0000045 | 0.0000035 |
| Govt-bond repo, 3–14 days | — | 0.000036 | 0.000028 |
| Govt-bond repo, >14 days | — | 0.000054 | 0.000042 |
| Index futures (per matched contract) | 3,000 VND | 2,700 VND | 2,700 VND |
| Govt-bond futures (per matched contract) | 5,000 VND | 4,500 VND | 4,500 VND |

Confidence: **high** for 2019-02-15 → 2025-01-09. For **2025-01-10 → 2025-04-28** the rates
are **assumed unchanged with no gazetted source** (see §8.2) — `low`. From 2025-04-29 they
are re-issued unchanged by QĐ 1541/QĐ-BTC — `high`. Discounts: market maker **up to 80%**
off on market-making activity; green-bond services **50%** off — both read from the
mechanism circular, not from QĐ 1541.

Put-through trades take the **same rate**, with no separate line and no per-trade minimum.
Odd lots have **no state-set fee** at all.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Custody — equity, funds, CW | ALL | **quantity held** | 0.3 VND/unit/month | — | depository | member → investor | **monthly** | 2019-02-15 → 2020-03-18 | high |
| Custody — equity, funds, CW | ALL | quantity held | **0.27 VND/unit/month** | — | depository | member → investor | monthly | 2020-03-19 → current | high |
| Custody — corporate bonds | ALL | quantity held | 0.2 → **0.18** VND/unit/month | max 2,000,000 VND/code/month | depository | member | monthly | 0.2 to 2020-03-18; 0.18 from 2020-03-19 | high |
| Custody — công cụ nợ | ALL | quantity held | 0.2 → 0.18 → **0.14** VND/unit/month | max 2,000,000 → **1,400,000** VND/code/month | depository | member | monthly | 0.14 / 1.4m from **2022-01-01** | high |
| — the shape | | **The only underlying-market charge that is not per-fill.** 100,000 shares held for a month costs 27,000đ regardless of turnover. Brokers re-bill with their own monthly minimum — a broker term | | | | | | high |
| **Derivatives position management** | HNXDS | **contract_days** (open contracts × accounts × days) | 3,000 → **2,550** VND | — | depository | member → investor | monthly, accrued daily | 3,000 to 2020-03-18; **2,550 from 2020-03-19 to 2021-12-31** | high |
| **Derivatives clearing (novation)** | HNXDS | **matched contracts, BOTH legs** | **2,550 VND per novated contract** | — | depository | member → investor | per_fill | **2022-01-01 → current** | high |
| — **the shape change** | HNXDS | The charge switched from a **holding** basis to a **per-fill** basis on **2022-01-01**, not 2025-04-29. Intraday round trips got more expensive; multi-day holds got cheaper. Worked example: opening 20 and closing 8 on one day = (20+8) × 2,550 = **71,400đ** | | | | | | high |
| — **the reality-vs-schedule fork** | HNXDS | Brokers billed the **per-day** fee through at least 2024-07-11 and had switched to the **per-fill** fee by 2025-05-05. **Provide both as a config switch and record which the run used.** A backtest reproducing actual 2022–2024 retail costs should use per-day; one applying the gazetted schedule should use per-fill | | | | | | medium |
| Derivatives collateral (margin asset) management | HNXDS | **accumulated margin balance** (cash + securities at **PAR**) per account | 0.00003 → **0.000024** /month | 400,000–2,000,000 → **min 320,000, max 1,600,000** (2020-03-19→2021-12-31) → **min 100,000, max 1,600,000** (from 2022-01-01), all VND/account/month | depository | member → investor | **monthly**, debited on the 1st of the following month; if the derivatives account lacks cash the broker withdraws from posted margin | see rate column | high |
| — the floor dominates | HNXDS | The **100,000đ/month floor is a fixed cost for anyone holding any margin**, dominating the percentage until the balance exceeds ~4.2bn đồng (~13.3bn under the earlier 320,000đ floor) | | | | | | high |
| Corporate action (rights exercise) | ALL | **events**, graded by holder count on the record date | 5,000,000–20,000,000 VND/event → **3,500,000 / 7,000,000 / 10,500,000 / 14,000,000** by band (<500 / 500–999 / 1,000–4,999 / >5,000) | — | depository | **ISSUER** | per_event | tiered schedule from 2022-01-01 | high |
| — **no investor-side cost** | | **The corporate-action handler needs no cost model on the investor side** — only the 5% dividend tax where applicable | | | | | | high |

| — **advanced fraction cap** | | **UNVERIFIED.** "Up to 100% of net proceeds after fees and PIT" is the common description, not a sourced figure. **Mark it an assumption in the paper.** Some brokers charge nothing when the advance is used to buy rather than withdraw | | | | | | low |
| — when it bites | | Only while T+2 settlement is unfinished. **It is the only way to recycle sale proceeds intraday, and it must be charged for** — otherwise the backtest overstates achievable turnover | | | | | | high |

### 12.8 Worked round-trip costs, for sanity-checking an implementation

**One VN30 futures contract, opened and closed the same day, 2023 (index 1,100 points,
IM ratio 17%):**

| Component | Amount |
|---|---|
| Exchange trading fee, 2 matched contracts × 2,700 | 5,400 VND |
| VSDC clearing fee, 2 novated contracts × 2,550 | 5,100 VND |
| PIT, 2 legs × 0.1% × (1,100 × 100,000 × 1 × 0.17 ÷ 2) | 2 × 9,350 = 18,700 VND |
| Broker commission, 2 × 2,000 | 4,000 VND |
| **Total per round-trip contract** | **≈ 33,200 VND** |
| Plus monthly: collateral management, min 100,000 VND/account/month | — |

Under the **wrong** (pre-correction) dating this same round trip would be charged 2,550đ
once instead of twice — a **2,550đ (7.7%) understatement** of the per-round-trip cost for
every day of 2022-01-01 → 2025-04-28.

**One HSX equity round trip, 2023, 10,000 shares at 25,500 VND (notional 255,000,000 VND):**

| Component | Amount |
|---|---|
| Exchange trading fee, buy leg, 0.027% | 68,850 VND |
| Exchange trading fee, sell leg, 0.027% | 68,850 VND |
| PIT, sell leg only, 0.1% | 255,000 VND |
| Broker commission, 2 legs × 0.25% | 1,275,000 VND |
| Custody, 10,000 units × 0.27đ (if held across a month end) | 2,700 VND |
| **Total** | **≈ 1,670,400 VND (0.33% of one-way notional)** |

Note the shape: **the sell-side PIT alone (0.1%) is roughly four times the two-sided
exchange fee (0.054% combined)**, and the broker commission dominates both. A fee model
that omits the PIT understates round-trip cost by about 15%; one that omits commission
understates it by about 76%.

---

## Document provenance

Assembled from seven parallel research passes (sessions/order-types, bands/reference,
tick/lot, settlement, derivatives, fees/taxes, foreign ownership, KRX changeover), each
independently audited against primary text. **Where an audit downgraded a row, the
downgraded confidence is what appears above** — no researcher's original optimism survived
into this document. Where two audits disagreed (notably on whether the price-XOR-quantity
amendment rule sits in the gazetted VNX body), both readings are recorded with the one we
adopt and why.

Corpus measurements were run against `/Users/nadan/algotrade-research/dataset/hermes-parquet/`
and reproduce from `bands2.parquet` in the session scratchpad.

**Amended 2026-08-26.** VSDC **QĐ 26/QĐ-HĐTV (2025-04-16)** and its **Phụ lục 2** were
obtained **from the author directly** — `thuvienphapluat.vn` is Cloudflare-blocked to
automated fetching, which had defeated three prior retrieval attempts — extracted to plain
text, and read in full. They added the post-KRX margin stack (§6.3) and Điều 29's
position-limit ladder (§6.4), and they **corrected twelve existing rows**, including one
previously marked `high` that is now `low` because reading the final link of its citation
chain showed the chain does not support it. **§9.6 is the full record**, and it is the
first place to look before relying on any §6.3 row. The confidence summary in §1 was
recounted by script on the same date; the counting rule is stated there.

**Standing rules for anyone extending this document.** Re-resolve every article pointer
against the specific instrument for that interval — article numbers shift between rulebook
editions, and roughly a third of the original citation errors were transcriptions carried
across a renumbering. Never assert an absence in the corpus without running the query. And
never quote Vietnamese as verbatim unless it was read in the instrument cited, not in a
broker sheet reproducing it.
